# 151: Chapter 148: The Winds of Calyrex Blow Into Every Household

"The watermelon is so sweet!"

In the ranch, people gathered here were holding big slices of watermelon, eating to the point that their mouths were full of watermelon juice.

The Pokemon that came to the ranch with people were doing the same.

Keri's Machamp and Conkeldurr stood beside her, happily devouring large pieces of watermelon with both hands.

Other Pokemon like Eevee, Fidough, and Cyclizar started playing and chasing each other on the lawn after finishing their watermelon.

"It's a pity the watermelon is too big to fit into the fridge. In this season, it would be great to have chilled watermelon." Mayor Thomas looked regretful.

"I have an idea!" Upon hearing this, D thought to himself that this was an easy problem to solve.

"Oh?" Mayor Thomas's eyes lit up, and he turned to look over.

D looked around and found the Glaceon, who was staying with Alcremie, and called it over.

After hearing him, Glaceon leaped onto the table.

"Vee!"

A gust of cold air, formed by Ice Type Energy, blew towards the watermelon.

A layer of white frost formed on the watermelon, emitting a chilly aura.

Seeing this, Mayor Thomas burst out laughing:

"I'm getting old, I forgot that Ice Type Pokemon can also help chill watermelon. Thank you very much, Glaceon."

"Vee~"

Glaceon, feeling a bit shy, ran away, jumping to D's side. It wasn't accustomed to so many people, but the familiar presence of D made it feel safe.

"It's shy!" Mrs. Dante smiled kindly, "What a well-behaved Glaceon."

Glaceon felt even more bashful.

D patted its head, "Alright, go play!"

Glaceon then turned and quickly ran off, hiding beside the Dolliv in the sheepfold.

Mayor Thomas shared the chilled watermelon with everyone, while some children in town and their Pokemon partners indulged in the "Lucky Pudding" that D had prepared in advance.

D watched this scene with some emotion, as it was the first time the ranch was so lively.

He looked around, searching the crowd for his Pokemon.

He saw a group of Grass Type Pokemon gathered around Gogoat, admiring it as it released a large amount of Grass Type Energy.

Koraidon, Dragonite, and Cyclizar were together, chatting away as they munched on watermelon.

Alcremie and Nacli were stacked on top of Naclstack, putting on a "brick stacking" performance for the other Pokemon.

Butterfree and Combee were soaking in the watermelon, enjoying the sweet juice.

Miltanks were rolling happily on the grass, chatting with a Dachsbun.

The Pawmo, draped in a Red Cloak, was surrounded by a group of children. It let out a "Pa-moh!" while performing a superhero-style lift-off.

The children gasped in admiration, turning Pawmo into a superhero, looking both cool and impressive.

D was chatting with the townspeople.

The Seed Shop Owner, Elo, approached him, and they started discussing Bell's situation.

D: "Was the method I told you about useful?"

Elo nodded but then showed a troubled expression:

"It was useful. After going home that day, I adopted an Eevee Egg from someone in town and gave it to Bell."

"Initially, Bell was quite resistant, worried that the Pokemon inside the egg would hatch suddenly at night and kill her while she slept."

D: "..." Indeed, the thoughts of someone with a persecution complex can't be understood with normal reasoning.

"What happened afterward?" he asked.

Elo: "Later, after telling her and her mom that the Pokemon inside the egg was just a harmless Eevee, Bell reluctantly accepted it and tried to bond with the egg. This morning, I saw that she had made a warm little nest for the egg using a blanket and a cardboard box."

D imagined the scene: "Isn't that nice?" So far, things seemed to be going smoothly.

Elo nodded: "Yes, I hope Bell can continue to think this way even after the Eevee hatches."

Seeing the troubled faces of Elo and his spouse, D couldn't help but sigh at the parental heart's plight.

As he did not have children and had never been a father, he could only say, "Things will get better."

Soon, Little Green from Light Green Ranch had some agricultural questions to ask Elo, so Elo bid farewell to D and went to answer them.

"Hey, it's really lively!"

At this time, Keri and Allen walked over.

"Yeah!" D quite liked this lively atmosphere. He glanced at Keri, who happened to have something to say to him.

Keri: "About the statue of Calyrex..."

Without a doubt, she sensed a business opportunity.

"No problem." D nodded, since it was all for Calyrex.

After thinking for a moment, he said, "I'll sketch out the rough appearance of Calyrex in a bit, and you can carve based on that."

As for how to sell it, D felt it was not his concern, and he believed Keri would find a way to market it.

Just as she had promoted the ranch facilities before.

It's just the beginning now, but perhaps in the future, he might see the statue of Calyrex in every household, becoming the faith of the farmers in the Paldea Region.

By then, everyone would have a statue of Calyrex at home, and Calyrex, having drawn power from this, would soar directly into the sky.

No matter how powerful this Legendary Pokemon was, it would think fondly of him.

So when he traveled to the Galar Region, Calyrex would come to meet him...

D believed that after today's events, the legend of Calyrex would surely spread among the farmers near Porto Marinada Town.

Everything was ready, just waiting for the eastern wind.

Next, it's just a matter of waiting for the eastern wind to slowly blow Calyrex into the hearts of thousands in the Paldea Region.

————

In the afternoon, the townspeople gradually left.

D initially thought there would be a lot of trash left on the ground, but when he was about to clean up, he found the lawn was not that messy.

He recalled that everyone seemed to have put their trash into the trash bin before leaving.

Sure enough, when he checked outside, he saw the trash can was filled with a heap of watermelon rinds.

Speaking of this trash can, it was thanks to Zack!

On his second day at the ranch, Zack had contacted the town's garbage station and arranged for a trash can to be delivered.

Every morning at six, a dedicated person would come to take away the garbage from the bin, sending it to either the incineration plant or as food for Garbodor.

Since there was no need to clean up the trash, D took this time to start sketching the image of Calyrex.

The image of Calyrex was still quite clear in his memory, and having learned to draw before, he was able to sketch Calyrex's general appearance.

As he was drawing, Dragonite, with a little red satchel, stood by, nodding off while waiting.

Once he finished the drawing, Dragonite snapped awake, took the letter, placed it in the satchel, and then flew off towards Porto Marinada Town.

D looked up at the sky, then slowly exhaled a breath.

He smiled and asked Koraidon and Cyclizar beside him, "Did you have fun today?"

"Kya-kee!"

"Gyaoo!"

The two Pokemon responded in unison.

"As long as you're happy. You don't seem hungry yet, so I'll cook later."

In the afternoon, the Pokemon in the ranch had eaten lots of snacks, nearly emptying his fridge's supplies.

After a busy day, D felt a bit tired and decided to take a good rest first.

Just as he returned to the house, intending to lie down for a nap, the phone suddenly rang.

---

---

---

---

# 152: Chapter 149: Parenthood

D had no choice but to get up to answer the call.

As the video call connected, Professor Sada's strikingly confident face appeared on the screen.

Seeing her, D instantly perked up.

"Professor Sada?"

Professor Sada smiled, "Long time no see, D. How have you been lately?"

Thinking about the events of the past few days, D nodded with a smile, "Pretty good."

He knew the purpose of Professor Sada's call and shouted towards the outside.

Soon, the crimson figure of Koraidon appeared in the room.

D said, "Say hello to the professor?"

Koraidon looked at the person on the screen and happily chirped, "Aga～"

Professor Sada looked at the person and Pokemon close to each other on the screen and smiled:

"You seem to be getting closer."

"In fact, Koraidon has always been obedient." D said, glancing at Koraidon.

"That's quite surprising." Professor Sada pondered for a long time and said, "Maybe your Koraidon is special."

She recalled the events of the past period.

To verify whether Koraidon and Miraidon could get along with humans, she and her husband, Professor Turo, started trying to let their son, Arven, interact with them.

But the interactions were not very successful.

Why is that?

Professor Sada fell into contemplation once again.

Looking at Professor Sada on the screen, D thought again about her and Professor Turo's future, as well as Arven's unfortunate fate.

After a brief thought, he ultimately decided to intervene.

D asked, "Professor, has your experiment reached a bottleneck?"

Professor Sada did not expect him to ask this, thought for a moment, and explained the difficulty Arven faced in interacting with Miraidon and Koraidon.

"I don't understand... After being captured by a Poke Ball, though Koraidon and Miraidon won't attack humans, they still remain cautious."

Koraidon and Miraidon?

D thought of the rather aggressive Koraidon in the game and speculated:

"Perhaps because it has a more aggressive personality, even within the same Pokemon species, there are differences in personality."

Professor Sada nodded, "That can't be ruled out as a reason. Their personalities are indeed not as docile as the Koraidon beside you."

D hesitated and then continued, "There's something else."

Professor Sada was a bit surprised, "Hmm?"

D said, "It's because of Arven."

Professor Sada paused upon hearing this.

D went on, "Both you and Professor Turo are entirely focused on research, neglecting Arven. At his young age, he might think that Koraidon and Miraidon have taken his parents away, so..."

Though he did not articulate his thoughts completely, Professor Sada understood.

She slightly frowned and then fell into deep thought.

D did not speak further, giving Professor Sada ample time to think.

Professor Turo, who had not appeared on the screen, was the first to come back to his senses.

He walked into view, appearing more composed compared to Professor Sada.

"That's our oversight. Thank you for reminding us, D."

D thought to himself that he just didn't want to see Arven face a tragic fate where both his parents died.

Seeing the two pondering their own issues, he didn't say much and only thought about how to remind them of the future explosion event at the Fourth Observation Station, where they would perish.

But no matter how long he thought, D couldn't find a good solution.

If he directly spoiled it to them, the two professors might not even believe him. And even if they did, their obsession with research wouldn't let them give it up.

Just as he was about to say something, Professor Sada interrupted, "Let's end the call here for today."

After a pause, she added, "After finishing here, Turo and I will go visit Arven. See you next time, D."

D hesitated but nodded, "Alright."

Forget it; I'll wait for a better opportunity next time!

After all, it's probably seven or eight years until the event occurs. When he becomes more familiar with the two professors, he'll explain in detail.

After ending the call, Dragonite returned.

D, no longer feeling sleepy, began steaming a batch of Grassy Seeds for the Gogoat.

Watching the steam rising from the Grassy Seeds in the pot, D suddenly remembered that Grassy Seeds could also benefit Calyrex.

Grassy Seeds can provide Grass Type Energy for Calyrex.

Just by consuming a sufficient amount of Grassy Seeds, coupled with the energy converted from the Power of Faith given by nearby farmers, Calyrex will no longer lack energy.

Perhaps it could even become like Gogoat, feeling uncomfortable without releasing energy daily.

However, unlike Gogoat's rough and direct way of releasing energy, Calyrex can channel energy through abundant harvests and blooming flowers.

Green Grass Cake can also provide a chance for the crops generated by Calyrex to mutate.

The two, when combined, can achieve a positive cycle.

In the game, when players help Calyrex reunite with its two steeds, it is willing to travel with them.

This poses the question: Will the real-world Calyrex be the same?

If Calyrex is willing to come to the ranch...

For some reason, a picture emerged in D's mind:

—Calyrex enjoying afternoon tea in the ranch, elegantly sitting on a chair, with a small round table in front featuring Green Grass Cake and Grassy Seeds, while holding a cup of Kitakami's special tea, sipping gracefully.

It's quite a beautiful scene.

If Calyrex decides to come, he wouldn't mind hosting a tea party for it every day, preparing sumptuous afternoon snacks and treats.

But all of this hinges on whether Calyrex is willing to come.

As the legendary king who once ruled Galar in ancient times, whether Calyrex would leave its homeland is unknown.

Therefore, before that day arrives, he must, like a devoted believer building a kingdom for his divine faith, accumulate enough assets to attract Calyrex.

Of course, his intentions are definitely, definitely not because of Calyrex's two steeds.

Says who!

————

League Year 198, April 16, Summer.

In the morning, something significant happened at the ranch.

Three more Nacli evolved into Naclstack.

With three more Naclstack, their size was growing, making it nearly impossible for them all to fit in the Salt House.

Early in the morning, D began directing them to dig and expand the living space.

However, this move was completely lost on a group of Naclstack who grew up indoors.

"Stack…"

They looked at each other, uttering low and frustrated growls, indicating their inability to do so.

D: "…"

Hmm, that's indeed a problem.

D fell into thought.

At this time, the only Nacli that hadn't evolved was about to cry, seeing the four large companions ahead.

Seeing its state, the considerate Alcremie immediately stepped up to comfort it, gently patting its body with tiny hands.

D caught sight of that scene from the corner of his eye.

He has always had a deep impression of this Nacli.

Back in the wild, this Nacli was the weakest in its group and appeared slightly malnourished.

So far, he's taken a free-range approach to the salt production of Nacli, which is why the Nacli often followed Alcremie without intervention.

D didn't want to lock them up for Rock Salt.

Seeing the state of this Nacli now, he immediately stepped forward, soothingly saying:

"Don't be sad. Even if you never evolve for your entire life, I'll still take care of you!"

The four other Naclstack were extremely envious.

"Nacli…"

Nacli felt anxious yet touched.

Though touched, it didn't want to remain unevolved for life! It wanted to evolve into Naclstack and give D more Rock Salt!

Seeing Nacli's reaction, D realized his mistake.

He scratched his head and quickly amended, "Don't worry, I'll do my best to help you evolve!"

---

---

---

---

# 153: Chapter 150: The Working Diglett

The method capable of helping Nacli evolve...

The first thing D thought of was to gain experience through battles to achieve training effects and thus help the Pokemon evolve.

How about letting it spar with other Naclstack?

This is indeed a good method.

Generally speaking, the evolution of Pokemon is an accumulation of life levels. When accumulated to a certain degree, a transformation occurs, turning into a more powerful existence.

Therefore, Nacli must be provided with ample nutrition and energy.

This is easy to satisfy. Later, he could make several servings of Rock Salt Fried Soil, which Nacli likes.

The stuff can enhance Nacli's body strength, making it stronger and healthier.

With this in mind, D had the other four Naclstack first share their evolution insights with Nacli, while he returned indoors to check the contents of a storage cabinet.

He then discovered there was still plenty of Rock Salt at home, but he was out of soil for making Rock Salt Fried Soil.

The soil comes from Zapapico Town and contains various nutritional elements, making it very suitable for Pokemon with Rock and Ground attributes.

Zapapico Town...

Unfortunately, the distance between Zapapico Town and Porto Marinada Town is too great; he couldn't go there to dig it himself right now and could only purchase it at the Porto Marinada Market.

The food supplies at home were running low, so it was a good opportunity to replenish them.

At 9 a.m., after finishing work on the farm, D said a word to Cyclizar and Koraidon and rode into town on a Gogoat.

They first went to the Porto Marinada Market to buy a bunch of Berries and steaks, then finally went to a soil stall and bought a large bag of soil.

The stall owner, seeing he comes over periodically, smiled and asked, "Those Pokemon must love eating it, right?"

"Absolutely." D also nodded with a smile.

Rock Salt Fried Soil doesn't spoil, so he always prepares a big pot of it and stores it. Every day, he just takes some to feed Nacli.

"Glad they love it!" said the stall owner. "I heard that the young Larvitar of Tyranitar also eats this kind of soil when they are small!"

"Tyranitar buries its eggs deep underground after laying them, and when the Larvitar hatch, they eat all the surrounding soil before moving to the surface."

"The more soil of this kind there is, the higher the chance there are Tyranitar and Larvitar inhabiting the area!"

"Really?" D only knew that Larvitar was born underground.

"Yes!" The stall owner looked confident and didn't forget to warn, "So if you find this kind of soil in the wild, you must stay away from that area because Tyranitar has a strong territorial sense and it would be trouble if they treat you as an intruder!"

"Makes sense." D nodded, not giving the matter too much thought.

Just then, someone else came to buy soil, so he turned and left, riding the Gogoat back to the farm.

The farm was still bustling with vibrant activity.

D carried several large bags indoors. He checked the old clock on the wall and, seeing there was still time, began making Rock Salt Fried Soil for Nacli.

The Pokemon were all playing outside, the room was very quiet, with only the sound of a spatula stirring in the pot, and he could hear Pokemon cries coming from outside.

D listened and figured it was Koraidon teaching Cyclizar and Dragonite.

Very soon, a large pot of soil was finished cooking, and D poured it into a basin, placing it on the floor to cool.

At that moment, a Butterfree suddenly flew in from outside, "Muhyi!"

D turned to look, "Hm? What's up?"

Butterfree incredulously pointed outside, like wanting him to go have a look, "Muhyi!"

Seeing this, D put down his work and followed Butterfree outside.

More accurately, he arrived at the side of the farmland.

This time, without Butterfree's reminder, he noticed something amiss.

Because the originally flat farmland had very noticeable bulges, as if something had burrowed through from below.

Moreover, heartbreakingly, several saplings in the field had fresh bite marks on them.

"Muhyi..." Butterfree flapped its wings gently, looking very upset.

At this moment, something started moving underground.

Then, a Pokemon with an oval-shaped brown head and red nose burrowed out from below.

That was... Diglett?!

The guess in D's mind was confirmed at that moment.

It seemed the culprit behind all this was this little Diglett!

D immediately said, "Butterfree, catch it, use Sleep Powder!"

"Muhyi!"

Seeing the farm damaged, Butterfree was also very angry. It flapped its wings, scattering drowsy spores from above.

Just as it was about to hit, the Diglett seemed to sense something and shrank back underground, quickly moving beneath the surface.

Soon, the Diglett stopped again and emerged from the ground once more.

This time, its eyes were on the person and Butterfree at the edge of the field.

"Squeak squeak squeak!"

Then, it shrank back into the ground, seemingly playing pranks deliberately.

Seeing this, a few lines of frustration appeared on D's forehead.

Diglett, a Pokemon that likes dark places, spends most of its time underground, living on things like tree roots.

Because the soil is properly turned after Diglett passes through, and their droppings enrich the soil, making it the most suitable farmland for cultivation, many farmers meticulously nurture them.

---

To feed them, farmers plant trees that Digletts like to eat around the crops, using this method to have them help cultivate the land.

But sometimes, Digletts also nibble on the crops planted by the farmers, causing disaster for them.

Just like now.

So, he's dealing with a mischievous Diglett on purpose?!

Oh no, those are the crops he worked so hard to plant!

"Koraidon!"

Confronted with the taunting Diglett, D decisively chooses to call for reinforcements.

Koraidon, who was sunbathing not far away, hears the shout and rockets over in a flash.

"Argh gaa sss!"

"Catch it!" D orders.

Upon hearing the command, Koraidon casts its gaze towards the field and, after seeing the instigator that angered D, has a speechless expression on its face.

The next second, it releases a terrifying aura from its body.

The Diglett, originally causing chaos in the farmland, suddenly feels an invisible pressure and a terrifying aura.

It instantly realizes that there is a very powerful Pokemon here.

In a panic, Diglett emits a squeaky growl and instinctively tries to burrow underground to escape.

But Koraidon wouldn't give it that chance, would it?

Koraidon stomps on the ground forcefully, causing the tunnels dug by the Diglett to collapse instantly,

Then, it lunges forward and delivers a claw swipe that knocks the Diglett, unable to retreat in time, "unconscious."

Having completed its task, Koraidon hides its merits and fame and returns to D's side.

D then goes forward to check the damage to the farmland.

Fortunately, Butterfree noticed early and the Diglett's trail hasn't spread elsewhere. Only a small patch of farmland has been affected, with two corn seedlings being nibbled.

Seeing this, D breathes a sigh of relief.

He walks to the unconscious Diglett, trying to lift it, but despite his efforts, he fails.

The bottom half of the Diglett seems as if it's firmly welded to the ground.

"What the heck, it's so hard to dig out?" D is somewhat surprised, no wonder no one knows what a Diglett looks like underground.

Butterfree flies up and asks: "Mu-ii?"

D thinks it is asking what to do, and after a brief thought, he replies with a cold smile:

"We certainly can't let it off just like this."

The "unconscious" Diglett shivers.

D notices this scene and thinks to himself, wow, it's even pretending to be knocked out!

Since that's the case, how could he not play along?

D pretends to be angry: "This guy is indeed too wicked, it must be punished properly!"

Upon hearing his tone, the unconscious Diglett starts to tremble in fear.

Although it can dig underground to escape, in front of that terrifying Pokemon, it might not necessarily be able to run away.

D glances sideways at the Diglett and says to Koraidon and Butterfree:

"How do you guys think we should punish this bad guy?"

Butterfree responds angrily: "Mu-ee!"

Koraidon: "Argh gaa sss..."

D deliberately says: "If that's the case, then let's lock it up and starve it for a month! Roast it with Flamethrower, then freeze it in the ice cellar, and finally throw it into the river!"

When it hears these words, the Diglett trembles even more dramatically.

Devil!

This human is an even scarier devil than the red-skinned Pokemon!

Butterfree imagines the scene and finds it too cruel. Although this Diglett is naughty, its crime is not worth such a punishment.

It speaks up gently: "Mu-ee..."

"Squeak squeak squeak!" Diglett thinks right, right, it wasn't malicious, it just wanted to tease the human a bit!

Upon hearing Butterfree's words, D "snorts coldly" and follows with:

"Letting it go is not impossible, unless it stays to work for me to offset the damage it caused."

Next to them, Koraidon observes the duo's back-and-forth banter, momentarily at a loss for words.

It knows that D was just talking and wouldn't really do such things.

But Diglett doesn't think so.

In its view, this human is indeed the most terrifying creature in the world!

Its mind starts racing as it imagines being locked up, roasted, frozen, and finally thrown into the river.

So scary...

Diglett deeply regrets its actions, and just then, it suddenly hears the human say it's not impossible to let it go.

Upon hearing this, Diglett quickly opens its eyes, nods repeatedly in the "startled" gaze of D.

"Squeak squeak squeak!"

It's willing! It's willing to stay and work to offset the damages it caused!

D: "..." So easily scared.

He looks at the Diglett that "voluntarily" stays to work with great interest.

In fact, upon seeing Diglett for the first time, he already had the idea to keep it.

But alas, this Diglett is too mischievous, even playing whack-a-mole with him, so he had no choice but to call Koraidon to scare it.

"Are you really willing to stay and work?" D asks.

The Diglett nods hastily: "Squeak squeak!"

D is a bit skeptical: "You wouldn't be planning to escape when I'm not paying attention, would you?"

The Diglett quickly shakes its head: "Squeak squeak!"

"Very well then, let me see your sincerity." D starts to lead the way: "Follow me! I just happen to have a job for you."

The Diglett scurries to follow.

D signals with his eyes to Koraidon, telling it to continue playing with Dragonite and Cyclizar.

---

But Koraidon didn't want to play anymore, because Cyclizar and Dragonite were also attracted over.

A few Pokemons were curious what D intended for Diglett to do, so they gathered around to observe.

Seeing this, D didn't say much, only took Diglett to the Salt House.

At this time, several Naclstack were playing on the grass outside, and the Salt House was empty inside.

D stood on the wooden floor and said to Diglett: "Help me dig a big hole here, digging downwards continuously, can you do it?"

Diglett was startled, it looked around, thinking to itself that this place was too big, it would take a long time...

But afraid the human would think it useless and lock it away, Diglett nodded repeatedly.

"Great." D didn't forget to offer some encouragement, "Do a good job, and during this time, I'll provide you with food."

"Squeak squeak." Diglett dared not hope.

D left Koraidon here to help it, like breaking the wooden planks and smashing the underground stones.

And he went to prepare lunch for the Pokemons.

Today's lunch was simple sandwiches and meat patties, and by the time D finished cooking and went to check the Salt House, Diglett didn't escape but had dug a big hole there.

Seeing this, D thought to himself that this Diglett was quite straightforward, saying it wouldn't escape when it obviously had many chances to dig a hole underground and run away.

D asked Koraidon to go eat first, then called the busy Diglett up.

Diglett nervously poked its head out from underground; it thought it would be scolded but was surprised to be greeted by food that emitted an enticing aroma.

Diglett swallowed its saliva.

D smiled and handed over the sandwich: "Here's your food, eat up!"

Diglett didn't dare move.

Seeing its demeanor, D thought it might genuinely be scared.

He thought carefully and comforted: "Relax, as long as you don't destroy my farm, I won't harm you, and the other Pokemons in the ranch won't harm you either."

"Once you've finished digging the hole, you can leave if you want, and I won't stop you. During your work, I'll provide food regularly every day."

Hearing this, Diglett blinked: "Squeak squeak squeak?"

It looked at the sandwich in front, then at the human.

For some reason, Diglett suddenly felt this human wasn't so scary when not angry.

It cautiously began eating the sandwich.

With the first bite, Diglett's two small eyes brightened instantly.

"Squeak squeak squeak!"

It had never eaten such delicious food!

Diglett sped up its eating pace.

Soon, an entire sandwich was eaten, and the stamina recovery effect made Diglett return to its best state.

Seeing Diglett's expression, D thought this might keep it from secretly running away.

After the meal, Diglett continued digging the big hole.

And D turned back to the house to check the money earned these days.

With the evolution of Nacli, the Rock Salt yield in the ranch increased significantly.

Today is April 16th.

At this rate, by the end of next month, he might have saved enough to build a house and could ask Keri to expand the small wooden house into a larger one with a bathroom, cellar, guest room, and separate kitchen.

"Whew, really exciting!"

Closing the ledger, D went to check the bowl of Rock Salt Fried Soil.

Seeing the soil had cooled down, he took the bowl to Nacli relaxing under a tree.

With a thud, D placed the bowl in front of Nacli, encouragingly looking over:

"Eat up! Finish this bowl of soil, and you should be able to evolve!"

This Nacli wasn't lacking strength but its physical quality hadn't reached that of the other Naclstack.

Innate malnutrition could only be compensated through effort later.

Even though after coming to the ranch, it always ate nutritious food, overall quality compared to other Nacli was still lacking.

The Rock Salt Fried Soil could enhance Nacli's body strength, and as long as it became stronger, it might meet the physical conditions needed for evolution.

If it couldn't evolve after eating, letting it spar with other Pokemons should allow it to evolve into a Naclstack soon.

"Salt salt..."

Nacli looked at the bowl of soil specially prepared by D, which other Naclstack didn't have.

Its heart was deeply touched, and it immediately began to eat the soil with renewed effort.

But having just finished lunch, its belly was already stuffed, after a few big bites, Nacli weakly lay on the grass, making feeble sounds.

"Salt salt..." It was overwhelmed...

D looked at the scene helplessly: "You silly child, greedy eaters, I meant to let you treat it as a snack to eat slowly, if you can't finish it at once, then leave it for next time."

Hearing this, Nacli felt a bit embarrassed.

D lifted it up, weighing a bit: "Hmm, you've gained a lot compared to when I first met you."

"Salt salt~" Nacli happily snuggled up to him.

Both it and the other Naclstack were very grateful to D.

D not only took care of them like Garganacl but also prepared various delicious dishes for them every day.

Even Garganacl doesn't do as well as D!

D simply held Nacli in his arms, relaxing together under the shade.

He looked at the wide fields ahead, feeling many emotions: "Once you've evolved into Garganacl, I won't be able to carry you anymore!"

"Salt salt!"

Nacli's eyes were full of determination.

That doesn't matter! When D can't carry them, they'll carry D!

---

---

---

---

# 154: Chapter 151: Challengers Frightened by the Grass Trial (4k)

In the afternoon, Diglett had already dug a big hole under the Salt House.

The little Diglett was panting from exhaustion; the scale of this project was really too much for it.

But it still wasn't enough for so many Naclstack to live in, and after digging out the underground, we still have to prevent the surface from collapsing.

All these require some preventive measures in advance.

Staring at the big hole Diglett dug, D fell into deep thought.

Seeing his expression, the Diglett had both anticipation and worry in its little eyes: "Chirp chirp chirp?"

"Don't worry, you won't be left out." Before the project is completed, he won't treat his employees badly.

D took out the sandwich he brought and placed it on the ground.

Diglett quickly moved over happily and started nibbling the sandwich in front of it.

"I have no experience in building burrows. If the ground collapses after digging, that would be a bit of a loss."

As D walked out, he thought to himself: "I have to consult Keri about what method to use to prevent collapses."

He walked to the pasture, intending to get some ice blocks from Glaceon's ice cellar to make a slushie.

But just at that moment, three school uniform-clad young boys and girls suddenly arrived in the pasture.

"Huh?"

Seeing them, D was a bit surprised.

The trio evidently had already noticed him too.

D heard them loudly plotting.

"Is this the farm Carter mentioned? The place where the Grass Trial is held?"

"Huh? No way? The one helping us train is a farmer?"

"Whoa! What did I just see! Is that a Dragonite? How can there be such a rare Pokémon here?"

D's heart leaped, thinking that Carter had actually advertised for him.

After the initial rain and clouds, an ordinary rave was no longer satisfying the Gogoats. For these past days, they've been itching for a fight.

Just as he was about to go over, Pawmo flew by.

The trio's gaze was immediately attracted by the dashing Pawmo wearing a Red Cloak.

The three honest students, who had always been well-behaved, saw this incredible scene and widened their eyes in disbelief.

One of the girls with short pink hair even exclaimed in awe:

"Oh my god! Is that... Pawmo? Can Pawmo really fly?"

Another boy wearing glasses adjusted them, calmly observing Pawmo, and said: "It should be using the electromagnetic levitation principle."

At this moment, someone else noticed Koraidon.

"What Pokémon is that?"

"I don't know either."

"What is this place? How can there be so many rare Pokémon?"

Looking at the trio's slack-jawed expressions, D found it amusing and then stepped forward: "Hello!"

"Sorry for the intrusion!" The trio was still quite polite.

They first greeted D, then asked: "Are you the owner here? We heard from Carter that there's a Grass Trial here to help Pokémon train..."

"That's me." D nodded with a smile. He glanced at the school uniforms on the trio and asked, "You're all students from Orange Academy like Carter, right?"

"No, I'm from Naranja Academy," the girl said.

D was a bit surprised.

The girl then explained about Paldea Academy, which is divided into Orange Academy and Naranja Academy, where new students can choose different academies based on their preferences.

Upon hearing this, D nodded. Although unexpected, he didn't think much of it.

After all, this world was originally a fusion of orange and purple, with both Professor Sada and Professor Turo present, so it seemed normal.

He glanced at the trio and asked: "You're all here to participate in the Grass Trial, right?"

The trio nodded, recalling the changes in Carter and Delibird, filled with curiosity.

What kind of special training could cause such profound changes in such a short time?

Their hearts were quite itchy.

"Follow me, but participation in the trial requires a fee of 2000 League coins," D said.

This price was set after careful consideration.

"What?" Hearing the fee requirement, the trio looked at each other: "Carter didn't mention a fee?"

D explained: "That's because after the battle, I will provide treatment services for your Pokémon, ensuring 100% recovery no matter how severe the injury."

"I see!" The trio suddenly understood.

2000 League coins, the price of four bottles of MooMoo Milk; if it indeed strengthens their Pokémon, it seems quite worth it.

Seeing their expressions, D guessed their thoughts and smoothly reminded:

"Whether you can become stronger depends on your own and your Pokémon's determination and insight."

"Got it!" The trio nodded; they had heard Carter say this.

"Very good, then let's start!"

Saying this, D led them to the edge of the battle arena.

He waved towards the sheep pen, and three eager Gogoat immediately ran over, looking at the trio with a fighting spirit.

"Baaah!"

The trio: ?

D explained to them: "The Grass Trial consists of three stages: The first stage is a battle with one Gogoat, the second stage is a battle with two Gogoat simultaneously, and the third stage requires battling three Gogoat together."

"Those who ultimately pass the trial will receive a mysterious prize that aids in Pokémon growth."

---

Gogoat?

The pink-haired girl's eyes were filled with confusion.

She looked at the three Gogoat across from her, uncertainly saying, "Is the Grass Trial really about battling Gogoat?"

This is so different from what she imagined!

Although those three Gogoat looked tall and strong, they're just Gogoat after all, just a Grass Type...

If she sent out a Pokemon with the Sap Sipper Ability, or one that counters Grass Type Pokemon, wouldn't winning this trial be easy?

D immediately guessed what the girl was thinking.

He shook his head with a smile and said, "As a Trainer, never underestimate your opponents due to Type Matchups or ability suppression. A true master always maintains the heart of a learner."

The girl was taken aback.

That's not something just anyone could say!

"May I know your name?" she used polite language.

"Of course," D slightly nodded, "I'm D, as you can see, the owner of this ranch."

"D..." the girl silently repeated the name to herself, then nodded, "I understand, Mr. D!"

D didn't say much more, simply asking, "Would you like to give it a try?"

The girl hesitated for a moment, then nodded. She pulled out her Eevee-style wallet and found 2000 League coins inside.

As she paid, she suddenly thought of something, "By the way, can one ticket only be used for one battle?"

D shook his head, "You can challenge the Grass Trial unlimited times until the moment you leave the ranch. Once you leave and want to challenge again, you'll need to repurchase a ticket."

D didn't want to be greedy, especially since the Grass Trial was meant to satisfy Gogoat's desire to battle and help them expend energy.

If it were one ticket per battle, the price would deter many challengers.

However, he wasn't worried about anyone challenging continuously.

After all, not all Trainers and Pokemon can accept continuous defeat — only someone like Carter is the exception.

The girl nodded, "I understand."

"Then, let's begin!"

D took a step back to act as the referee.

One of the Gogoat stepped onto the battle field, eagerly looking across.

The girl sent out a large-bodied Cetitan with five sturdy horns.

The battle officially began.

Gogoat immediately released a large amount of Grass Type Energy enveloping the entire battle field.

In response, Cetitan used Ice Spinner to counter, covering its feet with a layer of thin ice, its massive body spinning rapidly as it charged toward Gogoat.

The Grassy Terrain was destroyed by Ice Spinner, and the Grass Type Energy began to dissipate.

Seeing this, Gogoat became angry, its eyes fixed on Cetitan, releasing an even larger wave of Grass Type Energy from its body.

The section of Grassy Terrain destroyed by Cetitan was instantly repaired.

Next, facing the approaching Cetitan, two extremely thick Vine Whips extended from behind Gogoat, fiercely whipping towards Cetitan.

With one whip, Cetitan was dazed, its entire body spinning in place like a top.

Girl: "?"

Gogoat whipped the spinning top for a while, and Cetitan became so dizzy it knocked itself out.

"Quick, get up!" the girl shouted.

But Gogoat didn't give her the chance.

It let out a sheep's cry, and the two thick Vine Whips merged into one giant grass stick, fiercely pounding down on Cetitan.

"Boom!"

Accompanied by a loud crash, dust flew up on the battle field.

When everything settled, Cetitan had lost its fighting capability.

The two spectators: "???"

What the heck was that move? Was it Vine Whip? Was it the Vine Whip they knew?

The girl's face was full of disbelief.

Looking at the Gogoat across from her, her mind replayed the entire scene just now.

That Pokemon... was it really just a Gogoat?

D stepped forward and fed Berry Milk to Cetitan.

In no time, Cetitan revived.

D turned to see Gogoat with a relaxed expression and asked, "Want to continue?"

"Wait!" the girl asked incredulously, "I want to know, did Carter also battle this Gogoat that day?"

"Yes," D replied truthfully, "Carter is a very determined person. He underwent grueling training with me for two days, gaining invaluable experience from the Grass Trial."

The girl nodded, "I see."

She checked Cetitan's injuries, then said, "We want to continue too."

The second battle officially began.

Still Gogoat vs. Cetitan.

This time, the girl changed her strategy, not allowing Cetitan to use Ice Spinner; she feared Cetitan would be whipped like a top again.

"Cetitan, Body Slam!" the girl commanded.

With a loud call, Cetitan leaped up from the ground, its massive body blocking out the sun.

Simultaneously, Gogoat's gaze locked onto Cetitan in midair.

A surge of immense Grass Type Energy frantically gathered in its mouth.

The two male spectators sensed something amiss.

In the next second, they saw a huge grass blade glowing green forming in Gogoat's mouth.

---

"Holy crap!"

"What... is that thing?!"

Both of them widened their eyes; no words could express their feelings in this situation.

The Gogoat, clutching the giant blade of grass, charged straight toward the falling Cetitan.

The two Pokemon collided fiercely in mid-air.

The moment the giant blade of grass hit it, Cetitan lost its balance and crashed straight to the ground, creating a large pit.

The Gogoat landed steadily behind the Cetitan.

The giant blade of grass in its mouth disappeared, and then it slowly turned to look at the Cetitan in the pit.

"???"

It was over... in an instant?!

The boy with glasses stared dumbfounded at the scene. Even as calm as he usually was, he couldn't help but suck in a breath of cold air.

The girl showed the same expression on her face.

She looked unbelievably at the Cetitan that had lost its ability to battle, suddenly realizing the gap between herself and the other side.

It was a gap as immense as between the heavens and the earth!

She turned her gaze to the Rancher.

But he just raised his head with a smile and asked, "Want to continue?"

The girl shivered and shook her head repeatedly: "No, no!"

She had a deep and clear understanding of herself; continuing would only get her tormented by that terrifying Gogoat.

She wasn't a masochist, so who would want to continue?!

D was a bit disappointed; there were still two Gogoat that hadn't had their fill!

He looked at the two male students watching and asked, "What about you guys? Want to give it a try?"

The two boys were also startled and instinctively shook their heads: "Forget it, forget it! Maybe next time!"

The rare Dragonite, the unseen Pokemon, the flying Pawmo, the roaming Naclstack, the out-of-the-ordinary Gogoat...

Everything told them this place was unusual! This was definitely not an ordinary ranch!

Seeing this, D felt even more disappointed.

He started to reflect inwardly, obviously realizing the terrifying strength displayed by Gogoat had frightened these three students, making them reluctant to battle it.

This won't do.

He thought briefly and then pulled out the rhetoric he used to fool Carter.

"What a pity," he sighed, "you haven't grasped the true meaning of the Grass Trial."

Sure enough, the eyes of the three were immediately drawn over.

"Mr. D, what does that mean?" the girl couldn't help but ask.

D, without missing a beat, replied:

"The original intention of creating the Grass Trial was to help more Trainers test and train the strength and temperament of the Pokemon by their side."

"Winning alone has never been the goal of the trial; the aim is to gain more experience from battles, to learn how to handle strong opponents, and to gain courage."

"So that in the future, when facing stronger foes, your Pokemon will remember this moment, if it can endure such a tough trial, then what else in the world can stop it?"

"An unyielding spirit and tenacious guts are essential qualities for becoming a strong Trainer, just like the wild-growing grass."

"Carter accomplished this, and that's why he's progressing so quickly. It's precisely those scars that have made them who they are."

"I don't want you all to gain nothing from the trial," D explained with reason and passion, almost convincing himself.

And sure enough, after he said this, the three in front of him seemed moved.

The girl recalled what Mr. D had said earlier—a true master always carries the heart of an apprentice.

This was clearly not something an ordinary person could say.

She couldn't help but say, "Mr. D, you must be an incredible Trainer!"

A Master Level Trainer? Or a Champion? Or perhaps a retired Elite?

Not only powerful in strength but also so kind-hearted, even while living in seclusion, he never forgets to pass on his master's spirit.

He even wants to help younger Trainers grow stronger, considering them by creating such trials...

Thinking about it like that, Mr. D is simply a noble and great person!

---

# 155: Chapter 152: The Heroic Expedition Team's Emergency Operation!

Dusk fell, enveloping the entire ranch in a dim twilight.

The three challengers, exhausted and stimulated, turned away.

Standing by the ranch, they bid farewell to D: "Goodbye, Mr. D! Thank you so much for your help today! We'll be back if we get the chance!"

D waved casually: "Go on, go on!"

Judging by their appearances, it seemed like today's harvest was plentiful.

Satisfied, the three turned and left.

D then turned to check on the three Gogoats.

After releasing the energy in their bodies, they wore expressions of contentment, appearing fully relaxed.

Seeing D looking at them, the three Gogoats cheerfully greeted him.

"Baa~"

"It's alright, you can keep lying down!" D said.

On the way back to the house, he began pondering the current state of the Gogoats.

Whether it's dancing or battling, these effects don't last.

In fact, there are more than just these two methods to resolve the issue of the Gogoats' excess energy.

If the Gogoat were like a water-filled balloon, the Gogoat itself would be the balloon, while the water could be considered the Grass Type Energy provided by the Grassy Seed.

Too much water expands the balloon, losing its balance, so the excess water must be drained.

In this case, if the balloon is large enough, far exceeding the volume of water inside, no matter how much water is added, it won't overpower the balloon.

To prevent the Gogoats from needing to release energy every day as they do now, their physical strength must be enhanced, expanding their energy capacity.

The current methods in this world for enhancing physical strength...

Seem to rely solely on constant training and extreme battling.

"If only there were recipes for that," D thought.

Like the Rock Salt Fried Soil that the Nacli eats daily, it can enhance the physical strength of both Nacli and Naclstack.

Unfortunately, it has no effect on Grass Type Gogoats.

As evening fell, the Pokemon, having been out all day, began returning to their nests.

Koraidon lay beside the bed, leisurely swinging its tail.

Cyclizar and Dragonite stood by the fridge, taking out two glasses of Berry Juice, gulping them down with satisfaction.

D started preparing dinner for everyone, the enticing aroma spreading as the bright fire flickered.

Thick smoke rose through the chimney, and the sunset, like orange soda, poured its light through the windows, filling the room with a warm and harmonious atmosphere.

While watching the sizzling meat in the pan, D thought of Teddiursa and Mew.

They had been out of contact for almost half a month, and he wondered where they might be and how they were doing now.

Based on their last messages and the Pokemon who delivered them, D guessed there was a seventy percent chance they were in the Kanto Region or Johto Region.

These regions are on the same continent, very far from the Paldea Region.

Due to the time difference, it was evening here, while it should be late night there.

By now, Mew and Teddiursa were surely nestled in their dreams, right?

Like wild Pokemon, casually finding a large tree to use Mew's Secret Power to create a spacious tree hole base.

The two fluffy Pokemon snuggle within the tree hollow, falling asleep together.

Soon, dinner was ready, and the sky outside was completely dark.

D and the group of Pokemon began to enjoy dinner.

Meanwhile, far away in the Kanto Region, just as D had predicted, the time had reached late night.

Waves crashed upon the steep coast, the deep sea extending endlessly.

Today's weather wasn't great; not a single star shone in the sky, with no light visible except the glow from a lighthouse stationed on a cliff.

Mew and Teddiursa were leisurely sitting atop the lighthouse.

They had traveled to this place a few days ago.

According to the Pokemon nearby, a mysterious figure appears on the sea near here every night, lingering around.

It's an enormous creature shrouded in mist that always emits a lonely call.

A Krabby living by the sea was frightened and recently heard about the renowned Heroic Expedition Team.

This expedition team consists of two Pokemon traveling on a journey, enthusiastic about helping Pokemon in need.

Krabby recently heard they had arrived here and sought them out, asking for help in investigating the gigantic shadow.

Filled with adventurous and chivalrous spirit, Mew and Teddiursa had come here.

They remained atop the lighthouse, waiting for the mysterious Pokemon to appear.

At this moment, Mew and Teddiursa noticed three furtive figures below.

It was a group consisting of two humans and one Meowth.

Eager to sneak into the lighthouse, the Team Rocket trio had not noticed they were being observed with excitement.

Jessie, tiptoeing ahead, couldn't help but fantasize: "There must be so many valuable things here!"

James, beside her, was equally enthusiastic: "Maybe there are rare Pokemon that we can capture!"

"Then gift them to the boss, making us rich and powerful Meow!"

"Miu?"

Mew tilted its head, watching the trio.

"Huh...!"

Previously, Teddiursa had been surrounded by Meowths in the city, leading it to dislike this kind of Pokemon.

What surprised it, however, was that this Meowth could speak human language!

Just as the two Pokemon were filled with doubt, a faint mist rose from the ocean.

Sensing something, Mew turned, along with Teddiursa, and saw a shadow approaching from the distant sea.

This was a shadow larger than they could have imagined.

Even though Mew and Teddiursa had anticipated this, they were still taken aback by the sight.

"Huh!" (So big!)

The shadow was veiled in the night, surrounded by thick mist, making it impossible to discern its form clearly.

At the same time, the Team Rocket trio noticed the shadow as well.

They showed the same shock but soon Jessie had a brilliant idea.

"If we could capture this creature and give it to the boss..."

"Great idea!" James clapped in agreement.

"Let's go Meow!"

As the shadow reached the shore, the trio sprang into action, commanding their Pokemon to attack the shadow.

However, their actions angered the creature, which possessed frightening power, and it swiftly sent them flying.

"What a bothersome feeling!"

As the trio was sent flying, the lighthouse beneath Mew and Teddiursa was also affected by the mighty force, resulting in the destruction of most of it.

Mew's eyes gleamed, and it and Teddiursa floated into the air.

The shadow let out a mournful cry, then turned and retreated back into the sea.

The mist slightly dissipated, and as it turned away, Teddiursa's gaze penetrated through the dense fog, catching a glimpse of its form.

Was it... a Dragonite?

Teddiursa showed a shocked expression.

"Huh...!"

Why was there such a large Dragonite?! It was completely different from the Dragonites it had seen before!

---

---

---

---

# 156: Chapter 153: On the Road

Teddiursa's mind conjured up an image of the gentle, hardworking Dragonite from D Ranch.

In contrast, it seemed worlds apart from this Dragonite here.

Too overwhelming...

Teddiursa was taken aback, worried that it might accidentally get crushed by the other.

Meanwhile, Mew, who had been through many experiences, remained much calmer.

It carried Teddiursa toward the ocean, toward that giant shadow.

Mew flew fast, and within two minutes, they caught up with the giant Dragonite.

Detecting an unfamiliar presence approaching, the giant Dragonite turned its head and saw two figures it had never seen before.

Mew tilted its head, "miu?"

Initially, Teddiursa felt a bit scared.

But as it got closer, it sensed a deep loneliness emanating from the giant Dragonite.

Teddiursa held its breath and, recalling the Heroic Expedition Team's motto, couldn't help but ask:

"一么,一么一么!"（We are the Heroic Expedition Team, have you encountered something? Maybe we can help you!）

Beside it, Mew nodded happily in agreement, "miu!"

It did not sense any malice from the Pokemon before them.

Upon hearing this, the giant Dragonite looked at the two Pokemon in front of it with a confused expression.

It asked, "吼呜?"（What is the Heroic Expedition Team?）

Teddiursa explained, "一么!"（It's a mysterious organization traveling around the world to help Pokemon in need. The hero is Mew! I'm currently an apprentice hero!）

It gestured to Mew as the hero beside it.

The giant Dragonite looked at Mew and at its beautiful, sky-blue eyes.

"吼呜?" In its almost desperate heart, a spark of hope ignited.

Can you really help? It asked.

Mew nodded and said happily, "miu!"

The giant Dragonite further asked, "吼呜?"（Can you help me find my companions?）

These past days, it had been wandering nearby, searching for its companions.

Today, a sound resembling their call lured it here, but it hadn't seen them nearby.

Find your companions?

Teddiursa and Mew blinked, exchanging glances.

Mew asked, "miu?"（Are your companions also Dragonites?）

The giant Dragonite nodded its head vigorously and, seeing their expressions, eagerly asked, "吼呜?"（Have you seen them?）

"一么..." Teddiursa shook its head, thinking it hadn't seen any other Dragonites around.

Noticing the disappointment in the giant Dragonite's eyes, Teddiursa spoke again, "一么,一么."（However, I do know of a Dragonite, though I'm unsure if it's your companion. It's in a far, far-away place.）

It referred to the Dragonite at D Ranch.

Upon hearing this, the giant Dragonite's eyes lit up instantly.

It eagerly asked, "吼呜?"（Where is it?）

Teddiursa answered, "一么."（In the Paldea Region, there's one at D Ranch living with D.）

Paldea Region... D Ranch...

The giant Dragonite took note of these two pieces of information.

However, it soon realized it didn't know the exact location.

"miu～"

Seeing its anxious appearance, Mew smiled, and a blue light flashed through its eyes, sharing its memories of the ranch's location and D's appearance.

After learning its companions' whereabouts, the giant Dragonite hurriedly left.

Its figure gradually disappeared into the ocean, accompanied by a thick mist.

The sea nearby returned to clear weather.

Mew and Teddiursa floated mid-air, gazing at the direction where the giant Dragonite disappeared.

"一么!"

Up next, let's write a letter to inform D about this matter!

"miu～" Mew gladly agreed.

Having resolved the mysterious shadow incident, the two Pokemon first went to the client Krabby, relaying this news to it.

Upon hearing that the giant Pokemon would no longer come here, Krabby breathed a deep sigh of relief and gratefully thanked the Heroic Expedition Team, planning to treat them to a seaweed feast.

But Mew and Teddiursa declined.

As heroes roaming the world to uphold justice and assist the weak, accepting nothing from the public was their principle.

Upon hearing this, Krabby expressed further gratitude.

And so, Mew and Teddiursa returned to their temporary tree-hole base built with Secret Power.

The two Pokemon found crayons and letter paper in the corner and illustrated today's events together.

Finally, they entrusted the letter to Pidgeot, whom they met in the forest, asking it to deliver the letter to the ranch.

Pidgeot grabbed the envelope with its claws, chirped happily at Teddiursa and Mew, spread its wide wings, and flew away toward the distance.

————

The next morning, as daylight broke.

D first went to Salt House to check the progress of Diglett's work, then rode Cyclizar to Keri's abode.

Walking into the store, D immediately noticed the Calyrex statue displayed on the shelves.

Compared to the ones he carved, Keri's craftsmanship was incredibly lifelike, almost real.

D approached to take one down for a closer look and asked, "How's the sales?"

Keri smiled, "2000 League Coins each, they're very popular! Many farmers have purchased statues from me to display at home."

"I heard them say that if summer's crops harvest well, they plan to establish a special Thanksgiving in the fall to express their gratitude for Calyrex's blessings."

D: "..."

If Calyrex knew, it would surely feel very happy, wouldn't it?

So far, Calyrex's missionary work in the Paldea Region seems to be going quite smoothly.

Whether Calyrex has regained any strength is unknown.

Still, D was here not for this matter, but to inquire about Salt House's expansion work.

But before he could speak, Keri tossed over a package.

D instinctively caught it with his hands: "What's this?"

Keri: "Profit from the statues."

D opened the package and, sure enough, found a considerable amount of money inside.

He didn't refuse; after accepting it, he asked, "If I want to expand Salt House, how should I prevent the holes excavated from collapsing?"

Keri was somewhat surprised, "Are you having Naclstack dig down?"

D shook his head, then nodded, "Not Naclstack, but a Diglett that's currently working at my ranch."

After listening, Keri nodded in understanding, "That's simple, I'll come over this afternoon to handle it. You should have Diglett stop for now before it hollows out the underground for you."

"Alright." D understood.

After bidding goodbye to Keri, he returned to the ranch.

In the afternoon, while D was at the ranch, preparing a creamy ice smoothie for Glaceon, a distinct Pokemon cry suddenly echoed from outside.

He went out to investigate and saw a Pidgeot landing on his ranch.

In its claws, it held a letter.

Upon witnessing this scene, D's heart stirred, immediately realizing that Teddiursa had sent him another letter!

---

---

---

---

# 157: Chapter 154: Dudunsparce Comes to Help

D received the letter from Pidgeot's claws.

The white letter paper was covered with colorful drawings made with crayons.

A Pokemon with two pairs of large pincers, the blue ocean, a lighthouse by the sea, an orange Dragonite, and the moon in the sky.

At the end, there were still two small and cute paw prints stamped.

"Huh?" D looked at the peculiarly shaped Pokemon and guessed, "Could it be a Krabby?"

The Krabby by the sea and the moon in the sky, perhaps Mew and Teddiursa found this scene beautiful, so they shared it with him through their drawing.

As for the orange Dragonite...

D didn't think much of it, he just thought Teddiursa and Mew encountered a wild Dragonite outside.

After admiring the drawing, he put away the letter and stopped Pidgeot, who was about to fly away, asking Cyclizar to grab a box of Poffins to give it.

Smelling the Poffins, Pidgeot, who initially had no particular expression and was only fulfilling a commission, immediately showed a happy expression.

"Pidg!"

Seeing its reaction, D smiled and asked, "By the way, Pidgeot, can you still find Mew and Teddiursa?"

He mainly wanted to send them some food.

Upon hearing this, Pidgeot thought carefully and, feeling a little troubled, let out a growl indicating it didn't know either.

Because the Heroic Expedition Team is constantly moving.

If they had left the familiar forest, Pidgeot wouldn't be able to find them.

Judging by Pidgeot's demeanor, D roughly guessed its meaning, nodded, and added:

"Alright, then I'd like to ask you for a favor. Consider this food as a reward, okay?"

"Pidg!" Pidgeot nodded repeatedly.

"Then please wait here for a moment."

D smiled as he turned back to the house, retrieved a woven basket from the cabinet, and then took out a heap of snacks from the fridge.

Soft and sweet honey buns, chewy honey gummies, and honey lemon water in a milk bottle.

D filled the basket with these foods, put a lid on, and headed back outside.

"If you can find Teddiursa and Mew, I'd like you to deliver these to them. If not, you can keep them for yourself."

Pidgeot tilted its head to examine the woven basket, then nodded.

The next second, it spread its wings, picked up the basket with its talons from D's hands, and flew into the sky.

D nostalgically watched Pidgeot depart.

Pelipper, Fearow, Pidgeot...

One must say, Mew and Teddiursa are really good at making friends!

Wonder which Pokemon will come next.

"Perhaps I should buy a camera and give it to them, so they can send postcards by taking photos," D thought.

He planned to buy a camera when he went to town for groceries the next day to have it handy.

At that moment, Keri arrived at the ranch with the equipment.

D hurriedly greeted her and led her to the Salt House for an on-site inspection.

The floor of the Salt House had been damaged by a Diglett, which dug a large hole underneath.

"Is this Diglett a Pokemon you just caught?" Keri's eyes turned towards the Diglett resting in the corner.

D shook his head, "Not yet, it damaged my farmland, so it's working here to pay it off."

Keri realized, "I see, I thought it was hired like the Dragonite to help on the ranch!"

Since soil plowed by Digletts is ideal for plant growth, many farmers form partnerships with them, using them on their fields.

D had thought about this, but it ultimately depended on the Diglett's choice; he never forced Pokemon to do anything.

So he replied, "We'll see."

Keri didn't say much, examining the large hole below before taking out a Poke Ball.

With a flash of white light, a Pokemon with a serpentine appearance, yellow body in three segments, a blue belly, each segment with a pair of small wings, appeared before D.

Seeing it clearly, D was slightly stunned.

Noticing his gaze, Keri smiled and explained:

"This is a Dudunsparce, evolved from a Dunsparce, it can build nests underground with its sturdy tail and can use the Rock Type move Ancient Power to reinforce nests and prevent them from collapsing. We'll need its help to build the cellar!"

She said as she instructed the Dudunsparce, "I entrust this to you!"

The Dudunsparce, with a gentle disposition, nodded slightly, then dove into the large hole.

Immediately, dust flew, and the underground soil and Rock crumbled to dust under its efforts.

The nearby Diglett gaped in amazement at this scene.

So... impressive!

But then, it realized it might not be able to continue working here.

Because it was no longer needed.

Thinking it couldn't have those delicious foods anymore, the Diglett showed a disappointed expression.

D didn't notice the Diglett's reaction.

He was discussing the size of nests with Keri.

Keri: "How big of a nest do you want to build? Dudunsparce is excellent at nest building, wild Dudunsparce nests can be up to ten kilometers, quite large."

D thought carefully and then said, "Enough for five Garganacls would be fine."

"Five Garganacls…" Keri mentally calculated their size, "I understand, this construction should be finished by tomorrow. Dudunsparce will stay here today and I'll come for it in the morning."

"No problem, I'll take good care of Dudunsparce!" D nodded.

Keri smiled slightly, "I'll leave it in your hands then!"

Then, she called over Dudunsparce, explained a few things to it, and left.

D saw her off the ranch, and returning to the Salt House, noticed that the Diglett was nowhere to be seen.

He looked around, feeling somewhat puzzled.

"Diglett?"

D called out, but there was no response.

"Could that little guy have run away..."

For a moment, D didn't know what to say.

He searched the area but couldn't find it, only accepting it as fate.

It was getting dark.

D found the four Naclstack and Nacli to tell them about the ongoing construction of the Salt House, asking them to rest in their Poke Balls at night.

The Pokemons had no objections, so after dinner and some playtime outside, D returned them to their Poke Balls.

The night brought winds outside, making D somewhat uneasy.

Before bed, he went out to check the ranch facilities and gave Dudunsparce a supper for its overtime work, advising it to eat before continuing.

Once sure everything was alright, D returned inside and fell asleep cuddling a fluffy Pawmo.

---

---

---

---

# 158: Chapter 155: The Monster Arrives!

4 a.m., Paldea Western Sea Region.

The night is as serene as water, and a ship with lights on quietly anchors on the pitch-black sea.

This is a fishing boat with fifteen fishermen from the Paldea Region on board.

After a day of fishing, these fishermen went to bed early with anticipation, ready to transport their catch to the nearest city for sale the next morning.

In the duty room, two fishermen in sleeveless vests are dozing off.

They are the night watchmen for tonight.

Because the sea can always bring about unpredictable emergencies, like thunderstorms, rain, tsunamis, or attacks from wild Pokemon.

Therefore, every night, a few people are arranged to keep watch on the boat.

Suddenly, a crisp ding-dong sound echoed.

Ida, one of the dozing fishermen, was startled awake.

He blinked his eyes drowsily and let out a big yawn.

Surveying his surroundings, there was no wind or rain outside, everything seemed so calm.

"Another peaceful night!" Ida said, yawning mightily, as he patted the head of the sleeping Poliwhirl beside him, intending to go back to sleep.

But at that moment, his keen peripheral vision suddenly noticed the radar monitor in front of him.

The glowing green radar map displayed an enormous figure moving rapidly under the sea a few kilometers away.

Witnessing this, the sleepiness in Ida's mind vanished instantly, and he became fully alert.

He quickly shook his nearby companion awake, shouting, "Matsuboto! Wake up, it's urgent!"

"What's going on?" asked Matsuboto, the man who was enjoying his sleep, now stirred awake and slightly irritated: "What's the matter?"

Ida pointed at the radar map in front of them and exclaimed, "Something is rapidly moving underwater toward us!"

Matsuboto looked up and, upon seeing the giant mass, his eyes widened instantly.

"What is that?! Is it a Pokemon?"

"No!" Ida shook his head. "I don't know, the outline doesn't match! No Pokemon in the sea is that large except Wailord. No! Not even Wailord is this big!"

He quickly stood up and sounded the alarm: "We must get everyone prepared!"

The piercing alarm blared from the ship.

The crew members were awakened and gathered on the deck and in the radar room. Upon learning about the situation, their faces immediately showed a horrified expression.

"What on earth is that?!"

"It's so big! Dozens of meters long, could it be a sea monster?!"

"I heard my grandma say when I was young, terrible monsters live under the sea, and if humans don't respect the ocean, it will come to destroy them!"

"Stop saying nonsense! There's no such thing in the world! Everyone calm down! Prepare ahead! Ida, start the boat! We need to leave here!"

With his reminder, the crew gradually reacted.

They scrambled to start the ship and tried to leave the spot.

But soon, someone noticed that the thing was moving faster than the ship!

"Damn! There's no time! It's about to be underneath us!"

"Something's wrong, it's getting foggy! Such thick fog!"

"Damn it! That thing is here! The radar shows it's quickly moving upward, it'll break the surface in ten seconds! No, seven! Six! Five seconds!"

Suddenly, a layer of mist appeared in the air.

On the once calm sea, waves began to surge, crashing onto the ship's hull one after another.

Gazing at the mist floating over the dark sea, the crew felt a deep fear.

"Three seconds! Two seconds! It's coming up!"

"Splash!"

The sea erupted with massive waves, and a colossal shadow emerged from the depths, the sea churning. The unfortunate fishing boat, like a leaf, was tossed away by the waves.

The only solace for the crew was that the boat didn't capsize.

They collapsed on the deck in disarray, and gradually, the boat stabilized.

The fishermen instinctively looked over to that side.

The moonlit night mingled with the fog, and amidst the mist, they raised their heads to behold the gigantic shadow.

At that moment, everyone was awe-struck by the scene before them.

Fear gripped their hearts.

Someone shouted in a trembling voice, "Monster... it's a monster! The monster is here!"

His voice pierced the silent night with clarity.

The others said nothing, just stared blankly at that silhouette.

Suddenly, the shadow moved.

The giant Dragonite looked over the vast ocean towards the distant unfamiliar land.

It felt both anxious and excited.

After parting with the two little fellows from the Heroic Expedition Team, it started heading in this direction.

However, having never traveled here before, even with the route provided by Mew, it accidentally took the wrong path.

The first time, it ended up in a place full of ice and snow.

The second time, it found an isolated island, where it saw many unfamiliar humans and Pokemon.

Someone called Mustard told it this wasn't where it should be.

Now, after wandering lost for a whole day, it finally found this place.

Thinking of this, the giant Dragonite lifted its foot and began to step slowly but firmly forward.

"Roar——"

The long, lonely sound echoed from its mouth, drifting across the sea.

Hearing the sound, the people on the boat were slightly taken aback, then murmured:

"What on earth… is that creature?"

"Is its target the Paldea Region?!"

Soon, someone realized.

"This is bad! It's so large that if it reaches land, the cities will be destroyed!"

"But we can't stop it!"

"Quick, report this to Miss Jenny and the Paldea League, have them dispatch Trainers to stop this thing!"

"Yes!"

Someone hurriedly got up and found the radio equipment to contact the nearest police station.

Soon, the call connected.

"This is Casseroya Lake Police Station, Miss Jenny speaking!"

Upon hearing Miss Jenny's voice over the phone, the fisherman nervously stammered:

"Th-this is the Paldea fishing boat, we found a monster at sea! It's heading towards the Paldea Region! Please come to stop it! We can't let it ashore!"

Hearing this, Miss Jenny on the other end was momentarily stunned.

Her initial reaction was that this person must be joking.

Nevertheless, after a brief pause, she calmly ordered, "Tell me the exact location!"

"Paldea Western Sea, about 32 degrees east longitude, 127 degrees north latitude!"

"Got it!" Miss Jenny quickly located the spot on the map.

She donned her gear, climbed a nearby lighthouse, and with a telescope, gazed out at the distance.

In the next moment, she saw a massive silhouette shrouded in fog in the distance.

"This... is serious trouble now!"

This is not something their small police station can handle.

Miss Jenny frowned, calmly took out her communication device, and swiftly reported the matter to the Paldea League.

---

---

---

---

# 159: Chapter 156: It's Here to Find Someone!

5:00am, Paldea West Coast, Casseroya Lake Lighthouse.

Miss Jenny's expression was serious. Holding a walkie-talkie, she led the officers in orderly evacuations of the wild Pokemon nearby.

The Paldea League support hadn't arrived yet, so they could only do their utmost to prepare for the landing of that enormous creature.

Half an hour ago, they dispatched a helicopter for a close investigation of the creature's situation.

But until now, that helicopter still hadn't returned.

"…Evacuate the nearby wild Pokemon as quickly as possible and prepare for any contingencies!" Miss Jenny issued her commands methodically.

"Yes!" The officers quickly sprang into action.

Suddenly, fog rolled in.

Miss Jenny looked up and surveyed her surroundings, turning her gaze to the distant sea, and sure enough, she found that mysterious creature was closer.

From behind, the monitor officer reported rapidly: "Report! The target is ten kilometers from the shoreline! At its current speed, it will reach the target point in about ten minutes!"

"Ten minutes? That's not enough time!" Miss Jenny frowned, "Damn, where on earth did that thing come from!"

Just then, the roar of a helicopter engine suddenly came from the sky.

People looked up to see a helicopter with the Paldea League emblem landing slowly on the beach.

A tall, dark-skinned woman with long black hair and dressed in black attire stepped out from inside.

Her expression was stern and devoid of much emotion, although very young, she exuded an aura of awe-inspiring power.

Upon seeing the newcomer, Miss Jenny immediately saluted respectfully, "Chief!"

This was Geeta, the Chief Champion of the Paldea League, who also served as the President of the Paldea Pokemon League, recognized as one of the most powerful at the Champion Level, standing at the pinnacle.

As it happened, Geeta had been conducting an inspection at the Glaseado Gym just yesterday.

After the inspection concluded, she stayed there to rest for the night, planning to return to the Pokemon League the following morning, not expecting an incident to arise midway.

"Explain the situation here," Geeta said.

Miss Jenny nodded and reported:

"An hour ago, we received an emergency call from fishermen at sea. They had spotted a fast-moving mysterious shadow underwater. They initially thought they would be attacked, but the creature seemed uninterested in them, simply heading toward the Paldea Region."

Hearing this, Geeta silently nodded. She took the binoculars from Miss Jenny and walked to the shoreline being lapped by waves, gazing toward the distant sea.

Through the binoculars, she saw a massive shadow, shrouded in mist, approaching this way.

Geeta frowned slightly, "What kind of Pokemon is that?"

Miss Jenny shook her head, "The helicopter sent to investigate has not yet returned!"

Just as she finished speaking, another helicopter landed on the shore.

A young officer in uniform rushed out, looking shocked and flustered, "Re-reporting!"

Miss Jenny: "What's the situation?!"

The young officer's voice trembled, "Tha-that's not a monster, it's a giant Dragonite! It's estimated to be over thirty meters tall! If you include the part underwater, it's probably forty meters!"

Forty meters...a Dragonite?

Hearing this number, everyone present turned pale.

At this size, it was truly a sea giant! The thought crossed Geeta's mind that such a massive Pokemon existed in this world.

But she quickly realized now was not the time to dwell on it.

A Dragonite over forty meters tall, if it reached the city, the consequences would be unimaginable!

After a moment of contemplation, she asked, "When you approached, did it notice you?"

The young officer shook his head, "No, it didn't attack and seemed uninterested in us!"

Geeta acknowledged with a nod and then fell into thought.

Miss Jenny asked, "Chief, what should we do?"

Geeta, recalling the fishermen's report, replied, "Without malice, it likely doesn't intend to attack humans..."

Miss Jenny was slightly taken aback, "Should we stop the evacuation?"

Geeta shook her head without hesitation, "No, even if it has no malice, with its size, once it comes ashore, it could inadvertently cause destruction to the city!"

After speaking, she once more fell into contemplation.

Miss Jenny did not interrupt her.

After some time, Geeta suddenly asked, "Have you informed the leader of the Alfornada Gym?"
