If she remembered correctly, the Alfornada Gym Leader Tulip had a Gardevoir capable of telepathy.

If Tulip were here, they could attempt to communicate with the giant Dragonite.

Miss Jenny hesitated briefly, then nodded quickly, "We have! Gym Leader Tulip is on her way!"

Initially, she didn't know the Chief Champion was nearby, so she contacted the Gym Leaders in nearby towns to see who could come.

"Understood."

Geeta turned and boarded the helicopter, instructing, "I'll go ahead and try to communicate with it. When Tulip arrives, have her come to find me directly."

Miss Jenny: "Understood!"

Watching the resolute figure boarding the helicopter to approach the giant Dragonite, Miss Jenny felt a deep sense of admiration.

At that moment, she roughly understood why this chief Champion had at such a young age become the idol that all Pokemon Trainers in the Paldea Region looked up to.

————

A thick fog enveloped the area.

The helicopter continued forward through the mist.

Geeta calmly looked down at the sea, its surface disturbed by the influence of the giant Pokemon.

Suddenly, she heard the pilot in front shout, "Chief, we've arrived!"

The helicopter hovered in front of the dark silhouette, and Geeta stood up, her gaze fixed on the giant Dragonite.

Through the dense fog, she vaguely made out the true form of the shadow.

At just one glance, this chief Champion was stunned.

That ancient and formidable aura was something no ordinary Pokemon could exude!

Where did it come from? And where was it heading?

There were still too many things in this world undiscovered by humans, and Geeta didn't want to engage in direct conflict with it. So she stood at the open door of the helicopter, attempting to communicate.

"Stop now! Many humans and Pokemon live on this land, and if you continue, you'll destroy them!"

But her words received no response.

The giant Dragonite continued to move forward on its own.

Seeing this, Geeta frowned slightly.

She took out a Poke Ball with her right hand, and with a flash of white light, a large Avalugg appeared on the sea below.

"Avalugg, freeze the surrounding seawater!"

At the command, Avalugg began to move.

A terrifying surge of Ice Type Energy emerged from its body, frost spread rapidly, and the surrounding seawater quickly froze.

In the blink of an eye, the nearby sea surface was completely frozen, blocking the path of the giant Dragonite.

It was then that the giant Dragonite seemed to finally take notice.

It lowered its head slightly, gazing at the Pokemon trying to impede its progress.

It opened its mouth wide, a devastating energy gathering quickly within, then transforming into a massive beam that shot forth.

The frozen ice surface shattered instantly, the seawater surged, creating towering waves as the ice melted under the terrifying energy, the resulting white mist thickening the air with fog.

The Avalugg, of Champion Level strength, seemed as fragile as a lone boat adrift in the ocean against Dragonite's attack.

Geeta watched the scene with shock.

The strength of this giant Dragonite! It might have already reached Champion Level! Perhaps even surpassed Champion Level!

If such destructive power were unleashed in a city…

Images of buildings collapsing, a bustling city turned to ruins, flames rising high, crowds engulfed by infernos flooded Geeta's mind.

"I must not let it reach the shore!" Geeta thought.

Seeing her communication attempts fail, she resolved to use all her strength to stop this creature.

Just then, the helicopter carrying Psychic Gym Leader Tulip arrived.

She had already heard everything from Jenny and was a bit surprised to see Geeta like this.

She smiled warmly and said, "It's the first time I've seen you so serious! But leave it to me now!"

Geeta put away the Poke Ball she had just taken out and recalled Avalugg below.

Tulip took out a Poke Ball, and with a flash of white light, a Gardevoir appeared in mid-air.

"Gardevoir, it's up to you now!"

"Gardevoir."

Gardevoir responded softly, its eyes glowing with a blue light.

In the next moment, it vanished, reappearing further away.

The giant Dragonite halted its advance, looking up at this unfamiliar Pokemon.

Though Geeta and Tulip seemed calm on the surface, they were actually very tense inside, watching Gardevoir start to communicate with the giant Dragonite without blinking.

On the way over, Tulip had seen the scene of that giant beast unleashing a destructive beam.

Even she, with all her experience, couldn't help but be shocked.

That scene was comparable to a legend, leaving her at a loss for words.

At this moment, not only were they nervous, but even Miss Jenny and the officers on the shore were extremely anxious.

Time ticked by second by second; they didn't know how long it had been, maybe just a few dozen seconds, or maybe several minutes had passed.

Gardevoir finally returned to Tulip's side.

Tulip and Geeta looked over tensely, "How did it go?"

Gardevoir used its Telepathy ability, and its voice appeared in their minds:

"It said that it came here to find a companion."

---

---

---

---

# 160: Chapter 157: Who Is That Person?

Both of them were stunned immediately.

They had just imagined countless possibilities in their minds.

Such as losing its mind and coming to attack human cities, or being angered by humans fishing and coming to deliver punishment...

But they never considered this possibility.

After a brief moment of shock, they couldn't help but ask, "The companion... who is it?"

Gardevoir: "It's a Dragonite that lives with humans."

What?

Geeta and Tulip were slightly taken aback, but then quickly realized:

This powerful giant Dragonite came to find its companion. If they could find that human and their Dragonite, would it leave?

Thinking of this, Geeta continued to ask, "Does it know the name of that human?"

Gardevoir: "It says it can't remember."

Geeta and Tulip: "..."

This can't be blamed on the giant Dragonite.

After learning from Mew and Teddiursa that there were other companions in this world, it rushed over without delay, thus neglecting other information given by Teddiursa.

Although Mew had communicated the route to the Paldea Region to it in its mind, such a task was too difficult for a Dragonite that had slept for so many years.

Moreover, the route was sporadic and incomplete, and combined with the unfamiliarity of the world, it got lost several times before finding its way here.

It only knew its companion was in the Paldea Region.

As for other information, it ignored and didn't remember, only knowing its companion was with a human.

After hearing this from Gardevoir, Geeta and Tulip couldn't help but look at each other, a question mark rising in their minds simultaneously.

Who is that person?

What kind of Trainer and what kind of Pokemon could become friends with such a terrifying giant Pokemon?

After a brief silence, Tulip asked, "What should we do?"

Geeta couldn't help but hold her forehead, thinking, what a mess!

She let out a sigh and calmly said, "Let Gardevoir try to persuade this giant Dragonite to stay here and wait, while we go help it find its companion."

They didn't want a conflict with this terrifyingly powerful Pokemon.

So for now, this seems to be the only way.

Tulip agreed with this plan. She nodded and passed on the suggestion to Gardevoir.

Gardevoir nodded gently, then returned to Dragonite's side to continue communicating with it.

After a while, Gardevoir returned and informed the two of them of the result.

"It agreed."

Upon hearing this, Geeta and Tulip both breathed a sigh of relief.

Fortunately, the other party still had the rationality to communicate.

Now they only had to find that human and his Dragonite.

As long as they found them, this incident could be resolved perfectly.

After pondering for a moment, the young Chairwoman of the Pokemon League, the top Champion of the Paldea Region, spoke into the intercom and ordered:

"Contact major TV stations and notify the journalist media to broadcast this matter on TV to quickly find that Trainer!"

Miss Jenny, standing guard by the shore, nodded solemnly: "Yes!"

————

While undercurrents surged through the Paldea Region, there was a harmonious atmosphere at D Ranch.

At six in the morning, the old clock on the wall chimed the hour.

D opened his eyes, greeting the Pokemon beside him, and got dressed to start a new day.

After freshening up, D hummed a tune while preparing sandwiches for the Pokemon.

The Alcremie in the cupboard wasn't fully awake yet, drowsily watching D.

D patted its little head, getting a whiff of cream: "Sleep a bit more!"

"Ma~ma~" So the Alcremie closed its eyes, planning for an extra nap.

The morning at the ranch was always full of energy.

The Gogoats crowded around the trough, eating Grassy Seeds.

The Dolliv was basking in the gentle sunlight, enjoying it immensely.

Glaceon jumped off the Ice Stone into the grass outside, shaking its body with a face of comfort and tranquility.

The Combee and Beautifly gathered in the flower field, joyfully drinking the floral-scented morning dew.

Pawmo flew to the Chicken House, opening the door and herding the hens out to graze.

Koraidon, Dragonite, and Cyclizar also came under the corridor, stretching lazily before frolicking around the ranch.

Hearing the happy noises outside, the Alcremie in the cupboard found it hard to continue sleeping.

Frustrated, it jumped off the cupboard, intending to scold the three big fellows to keep it down, but was noticed by D.

While preparing breakfast, he turned to remind it:

"Oh, Alcremie, could you get the Poke Balls with Nacli and Naclstack from the desk and let them out for some activity?"

So, Alcremie had to comply, obediently climbing onto the desk, hugging the five small Poke Balls to the grass outside.

Upon landing, the five little Poke Balls automatically enlarged, shooting out beams of white light, forming the figures of Naclstack and Nacli on the ground.

Seeing them, the Pe retrieved Pokémon shrugged off its last bit of displeasure and joined one of the Naclstack in the 'lift-high' game in the ranch.

The sandwich preparation was simple and soon, over ten sandwiches were ready.

D placed them on the table, then took Berry Juice, MooMoo Milk, and Poffins from the fridge, and called out to the outside: "Food's ready!"

Hearing this, a group of Pokemon immediately ran over, gathering around the table from large to small, picking up the sandwiches and eating enthusiastically.

Seeing them enjoying their meal, D's appetite also increased.

He first turned on the TV, then sat down to eat.

The TV was broadcasting the morning news of the Paldea Region.

D didn't pay much attention at first, busy plucking dried grass from Pawmo's fur.

But soon, a news report on the TV caught his attention.

"In the early hours of yesterday, a fishing ship discovered a giant sea beast... Upon investigation, it was a giant Dragonite measuring over forty meters!"

"We still don't know where it came from. Currently, our top Champion Geeta and Alfornada Gym Leader Miss Tulip are trying to negotiate with it."

Then, a dark-skinned face appeared on the screen.

It was none other than Geeta, the top Champion of the Paldea League.

But compared to the game, Geeta at this time looked very young, around twenty years old.

Her expression was serious, her gaze fixed on the screen, her voice steady and calm:

"We have learned that the purpose of this giant Dragonite coming to the Paldea Region is to find its companion."

"Please, anyone familiar with this Dragonite, be it human or Dragonite, make your way to the Paldea West Coast by Casseroya Lake! It is waiting for you there!"

---

---

---

---

# 161: Chapter 158: Go Meet It!

D: "???"

Staring at the screen, at the giant Dragonite standing like Godzilla over the endless sea.

At that moment, D felt a sense of unprecedented bewilderment.

What's going on?

As everyone knows, there's only one giant Dragonite in the Pokémon World.

It appeared only once throughout the whole anime, in the original series at Bill's Lighthouse.

Bill tried to explore the mysterious creature loitering on the sea by using its call to make the giant Dragonite think it was the sound of a companion, luring it near the lighthouse.

However, the giant Dragonite didn't find any companion there. After destroying Bill's Lighthouse, it vanished into the ocean and was never seen again.

And now, it has appeared in the Paldea Region!

Is this giant Dragonite the same one from before?

If so, why on earth would it have crossed half the planet, relocating from the Kanto Region to the distant Paldea Region?

Moreover, it caused such a commotion, even alerting the Paldea League, which dispatched top-tier Champion-level personnel.

D's face was full of question marks.

D initially thought this had nothing to do with him.

But suddenly, as if remembering something, he abruptly stood up, opened a drawer storing postcards sent back by Teddiursa and Mew, and took out the latest letter.

The letter clearly depicted a lighthouse, a Dragonite, the ocean, and a Krabby.

Wait a minute!

D's eyes widened, and an unbelievable idea suddenly emerged in his mind.

Could it be that this giant Dragonite was tricked here by Teddiursa and Mew?

So, did he misinterpret what Teddiursa and Mew meant yesterday?

They didn't encounter a normal Dragonite, but happened to come across the giant Dragonite loitering near Bill's Lighthouse!

With this realization, D was shaken to his core.

"This is outrageous..."

How did Teddiursa even dare!

It actually managed to coax the giant Dragonite to the Paldea Region, seemingly to find him and the Dragonite, but he simply can't afford to take care of it!

The ranch would surely be destroyed, wouldn't it?!

D was a mess in the wind.

But soon, he calmed himself down, comforting himself: "Maybe it's not here for me."

After all, he couldn't think of any reason to link himself with the creature.

After pondering for a moment, he decided to stay calm and observe the situation first.

If he went and found out the giant Dragonite wasn't there for him, it would indeed be awkward.

After having breakfast, D first went to the Salt House to check on Dudunsparce's progress.

When he got there, he was surprised to discover the entire Salt House had undergone drastic changes.

This Dudunsparce seemed to have a talent for construction. It had dug through the original floor, and the extra earth had formed a cave on the ground.

Inside the cave, there was a passageway extending downward. Out of curiosity, D stepped inside.

The passageway wasn't steep nor as narrow as imagined. Even three people walking side by side could easily fit, fully accommodating a Garganacl.

Continuing forward, after about three minutes passing through the passageway, the view instantly broadened before him.

Gazing at everything before him, D was shocked.

In just one night, Dudunsparce had dug out a massive Underground Cave!

The interior of the cave was incredibly spacious, with sturdy soil pillars supporting the ground above at intervals, which D assumed were to prevent collapses.

Moreover, in the corner, there was a small underground lake. Whether it originally existed or was specifically dug out by Dudunsparce was unknown.

D was amazed. Truly a Pokémon worthy of Keri.

In such a large space, not to mention five Garganacls, even ten more could easily fit!

He walked around the place, finally spotting Dudunsparce asleep in a corner.

With the noise, Dudunsparce opened its sleepy eyes.

Seeing its appearance, D couldn't help but say with a laugh, "You're incredible!"

He placed the prepared food and juice in front of Dudunsparce.

Dudunsparce glanced down, opened its mouth, ate, and continued sleeping soundly on the ground.

D didn't disturb it; he returned to the surface, considering that with such a large space, he could perhaps capture some other Pokémon living in caves to coexist with the Garganacl.

Pokémon like Geodude, Drilbur, Golurk, Rhyhorn, to enrich the Pokémon ecosystem of the ranch while satisfying his collection habit.

With time still early, D started milking Gogoat, and together with Cyclizar Koraidon, moved the filled milk barrels aside, then took out two bottles and handed them to Dragonite for milk delivery.

After finishing this task, D went to the beehive, collected half of the Honey brewed by Combee, and set them aside, ready to make honey lemonade later.

In the blazing summer, savoring an ice-cold honey lemonade would be absolutely delightful.

Once everything was done, it was 9 a.m.

Keri arrived at the ranch, evidently anticipating the results of Dudunsparce's work, so when she arrived, she laughed and asked:

"How is it, are you satisfied?"

D nodded repeatedly: "Satisfied, I'm incredibly satisfied, your Dudunsparce is truly amazing!"

Keri smiled slightly: "Dudunsparce has always been a hardworking Pokémon, it's been learning construction with me for seven years now."

D realized: "Seven years... no wonder."

Keri: "Now that the work is done, I'll take Dudunsparce back then."

D nodded: "It's sleeping down there now."

Keri acknowledged, then went into the Underground Cave, and retrieved the sleeping Dudunsparce back into the Poke Ball.

"I have to work at other ranches, so I'll go now. See you later!"

"Mm."

Watching Keri's figure disappear, D finally stretched and flexed his body.

With all the work done, he had the rest of the day free to himself.

So he played with the proud Glaceon until it was thoroughly embarrassed before he stopped.

Seeing Glaceon hastily running back into its Ice Cave, D burst into laughter.

To make it up to Glaceon, he turned and took a large tub of creamy ice slush out of the fridge and placed it in the Ice House for Glaceon to enjoy slowly.

After all, the temperature in the Ice House was low, and with natural Ice Stone, he didn't have to worry about the creamy ice slush going bad.

At noon, after having lunch, D wanted to follow up on the news of the giant Dragonite.

He turned on the TV, switched to the news channel, and sure enough, saw the traces of the giant Dragonite there.

The Paldea League seemed intent on broadening the search for its companion by broadcasting this news 24/7.

Looking at the giant Dragonite on the screen, D found himself wondering.

No one's gone yet. Is it really here for him?

But why? He wasn't its companion.

D's mind was racing.

Soon, he suddenly noticed something.

IF the giant Dragonite was indeed lured by Mew and Teddiursa, then it might very well be searching for another Dragonite!

And Teddiursa had only met one Dragonite, which was the milk-delivering Dragonite at his ranch.

So, when encountering the giant Dragonite looking for a companion, it mistook them for companions and told the giant Dragonite about the milk-delivering Dragonite which it had seen.

Thus, the giant Dragonite, in search of a companion, traveled all the way to the Paldea Region.

Therefore, the actual purpose of the giant Dragonite wasn't to find him, but to find his Dragonite!

"Could it really be like that?"

D fell into deep thought.

If that's the case, then he really ought to go and take a look.

---

---

---

---

# 162: Chapter 159: The Guardian God of Dragon Island Is Born!

There's just one more problem.

The Giant Dragonite and the normal Dragonite, though both Dragonite, are completely unlikely to be companions.

After all, they have never interacted before; their lives are like parallel lines that can never meet.

The Giant Dragonite came to the Paldea Region on a whim. If it realizes this, it might be quite disappointed, right?

But no matter what, a visit will reveal the truth.

Since the decision has been made, it's time to act and get busy.

D informed all the Pokemon at the ranch about the trip he's taking with Dragonite.

"...we might return later than usual. Koraidon, remember to take care of Cyclizar and everyone at the ranch."

The reason for not bringing Koraidon along is that D isn't keen on exposing everything to outsiders.

After all, the place would be swarming with reporters, the top Champion, Gym Leaders, police, and it might even hit TV.

It's better to keep a low profile.

Upon hearing that it's not going along, Koraidon appeared completely downcast, and the colorful feather on its head drooped.

D raised a finger: "You're allowed to sleep on the bed tonight."

Upon hearing this, Koraidon's eyes lit up, and it let out a delighted growl: "Ah-gah-sis!"

D smiled and patted its head, "But before that, you have to help take care of the ranch."

Koraidon patted its chest, assuring, "Ah-gah-sis! No problem!"

D: "I'm counting on you then."

He turned back into the house, slung a backpack over his shoulder, and packed some emergency rations.

While doing this, D began pondering the possible outcomes of the meeting between the two Dragonite.

The first possibility is that the Giant Dragonite doesn't see Dragonite as a companion and leaves disappointed.

The second possibility is that the Giant Dragonite recognizes that Dragonite is not its companion but is happy to be friends with Dragonite.

The third possibility is that the Giant Dragonite realizes it's been deceived and flies into a rage.

But on second thought, the Giant Dragonite was shown on TV calmly waiting at sea and capable of equal communication with humans, suggesting it has a gentle, non-volatile temperament.

Therefore, the third possibility can be disregarded.

The first possibility doesn't matter much. If it's the second possibility, and the Giant Dragonite accepts the modern Dragonite, D plans on taking it to Dragonite Island.

Even though the Giant Dragonite is rare, he doesn't intend to capture it since the ranch is too small and has no space for it.

The best solution is to send it to Dragonite Island, where there's an ocean it can live in, possibly even becoming the Guardian God of the place.

Thinking about visiting Dragon Island, D packed a lot of food and Berries into his backpack, as promised to the local Dragonite, Dragonair, and Dratini.

If visiting again, he must bring tasty treats.

Seeing everything in order, D closed the fridge door and headed outside.

Dragonite was already waiting there. D mounted its back, determined the direction of the Paldea West Coast, and had Dragonite head that way.

Everything below gradually got smaller, with a blue glow appearing in Dragonite's eyes.

Agility activated, Dragonite's speed sharply increased, shooting forward like a jet.

————

Paldea West Coast.

Geeta stood atop a cliff, gazing at the Giant Dragonite over the distant sea.

Tulip stood beside her, gazing along.

"It's incredible that such gigantic Pokémon exist in this world."

Tulip couldn't help but remind, "Don't forget, the neighboring Galar Region also has gigantic Pokémon."

But Geeta shook her head. As the top Champion, she could tell the difference between the two.

"It's different. Galar Region's gigantic Pokémon rely on external forces. Once out of Galar, they can't grow gigantic anymore."

As for this Giant Dragonite, judging by its displayed strength, it is clearly its own power.

The power exceeds Champion Level, and even she couldn't guarantee dominance over this Dragonite.

Just then, Miss Jenny's voice came from behind:

"Leader! The companion the Giant Dragonite is looking for has arrived!"

On hearing this, Geeta's heart stirred.

She turned around and saw a young man with black hair in casual clothes approaching with Miss Jenny.

Beside the young man stood a very robust Dragonite.

From its determined look and strong physique, it was evident that it had been superbly raised.

"This person is quite adept at raising Pokémon." was Geeta's first impression of him.

As she was sizing up D, D was also observing the chief Champion.

"Her real age doesn't seem to be over twenty; she might even be younger than I was before I transmigrated."

One must admit, Geeta's accomplishment of becoming chief Champion and president of the Pokemon League at such a young age is remarkable.

While pondering, D heard the person in front introduce themselves.

"Hello, my name is Geeta, and I am the president of the Paldea League."

He also spoke, "My name is D, and... I am a Rancher."

A Rancher? Geeta was momentarily stunned. She had imagined many occupations for him but hadn't expected this one.

---

Truly simple and honest.

However, the excellent Dragonite beside her proves that this person is anything but ordinary.

Her mind was filled with countless thoughts, but she didn't show it and started to talk about serious matters.

"Do you know that enormous Dragonite? Can you get it to leave the Paldea Region?"

D wasn't sure: "I can try."

That was enough.

Geeta nodded slightly, she signaled to Tulip to release Gardevoir and said to D, "This Gardevoir can use Telepathy, it can help you communicate with that Dragonite."

After a pause, she asked again, "Is there anything else you need from us?"

D shook his head: "No, that will be fine."

He glanced at the Gardevoir, assessing its special ability to be Telepathy.

Telepathy, a magical power that can directly sense human inner thoughts and convey its own intentions through the mind.

"Then I'll leave it to you."

Geeta nodded, then fell silent. She watched as the young man skillfully mounted Dragonite's back and flew towards the ocean, thinking he truly was brave.

Even when she first saw the enormous Dragonite, it gave her a fright!

In fact, D's heart was far from as calm as it appeared on the surface.

He watched the sea dragon in awe, murmuring, "It's so big!" It's really like a monster from Ultraman!

The Dragonite was equally stunned: "Roar!"

It never thought there could be such a huge Dragonite in the world!

D felt a terrifying aura, his heartbeat speeding up, and asked, "Are you afraid?"

"Roar..." Dragonite's eyes showed shock.

Gardevoir, however, followed calmly behind them.

Soon, they reached the enormous Dragonite.

The enormous Dragonite noticed them.

Upon seeing the Dragonite beneath the human, it was initially delighted but soon became confused.

How did its kin become so small?

The enormous Dragonite couldn't figure it out.

It had slept in the ocean for ages, only to wake and find the world changed drastically.

Its companions and kin were all gone.

It searched for a long, long time but never found them.

"Roar?" The enormous Dragonite let out a puzzled sound.

Gardevoir translated, "It says, 'Why are you so small?'"

Dragonite was a bit confused: "Roar..."

It didn't recognize this big fellow!

Seeing it not respond for a while, the enormous Dragonite blinked its huge eyes, growing more puzzled.

Looking at the massive face, D cleared his throat and spoke:

"Hello, did Mew and Teddiursa bring you here?"

The enormous Dragonite's pupils moved slightly, noticing the human on Dragonite.

Mew and Teddiursa... Its mind recalled those small figures, then nodded and let out a Hyper Voice: "Roar."

Gardevoir: "Yes."

D observed this Dragonite, seeing how it seemed to regard Dragonite as a companion, but was puzzled why the companion became so small.

"Could it be that ancient Dragonites had such huge physiques?" D wondered.

He pondered deeply, having two directions to choose from.

One was to tell this enormous Dragonite the truth, saying that times have changed! This is not the world you knew, where Dragonites are all very small.

The second was to deceive this pure enormous Dragonite, trying to make it regard modern Dragonites as companions.

Which one to choose?

After contemplating, D tentatively asked, "Have you noticed any differences between you two?"

Hearing this, the enormous Dragonite carefully observed Dragonite and then said: "Roar."

Gardevoir: "Exactly the same, it is a Dragonite."

D: "..."

Oh well, instead of letting the enormous Dragonite continue to wander aimlessly in the sea alone, better to give it a home.

Thinking of this, D said, "There are other Dragonites in this world, I can take you to find them and live with them, would you like to go?"

Upon hearing this, a gleam of light sparked in the enormous Dragonite's eyes.

"Roar?"

Gardevoir: "There are other Dragonites?"

D nodded: "Yes, many many."

Like a child, the enormous Dragonite cheered, its eyes shining brighter: "Roar!"

This time, even without Gardevoir's translation, D understood its meaning.

He nodded, saying, "Then follow me, I'll lead you to them!"

The enormous Dragonite readied to follow.

D then thanked Gardevoir.

Gardevoir nodded gently, then turned and left, returning to Tulip by the shore.

"Let's go to Dragon Island and introduce it to everyone!" D said.

"Roar!" Dragonite responded and immediately led the way.

The enormous Dragonite turned around, following them and gradually leaving the Paldea Region.

The people on the shore, including Geeta, watched this unfolding scene, involuntarily breathing a sigh of relief.

The crisis was averted, time to wrap up and go home!

————

The Paldea Region behind them slowly faded away, disappearing from view.

D rode Dragonite flying in the sky, while the enormous Dragonite was entirely submerged in the sea, turning into a massive shadow swimming beneath.

---

If this scene were to occur in another world, seeing it would likely lead to rumors of a sea monster the next morning.

"Could it be that ancient Dragonites all lived in the sea?" D thought.

A picture formed in his mind of a large group of giant Dragonites, akin to underwater behemoths, constructing a Dragon Palace on the ocean floor.

He wondered if such Dragonites could still fly.

Looking at the small wings behind it, D thought it would be difficult.

An even bolder idea sprang into his mind; perhaps ancient Dragonites weren't Flying + Dragon Type, but Water + Dragon Type.

"Now Groudon has a companion."

The speed of the two Dragonites was very fast, whether swimming or flying, Dragonite as this Pokemon was truly versatile.

After some time, a familiar island finally appeared on the sea's horizon.

The Dragonites on the island seemed to sense the unfamiliar presence. One by one, they flew into the air to check the situation.

After seeing it was D returning on that silly Dragonite, they all breathed a collective sigh of relief.

But suddenly, an enormous figure broke through the water's surface, sending up a spray like a rainstorm.

The island Dragonites were startled, nervous expressions with eyes wide open.

Such a big Dragonite!

Seeing so many Dragonites at once, the gigantic Dragonite was even more delighted.

It stood guard at the island's edge, bowing down to overlook the whole island.

And D and the Dragonite landed by the lake at the center of Dragonite Island.

The grass here was still lush and vibrant, teeming with life; it seemed summer had arrived, with clusters of small yellow wildflowers growing in the grassy areas.

In the lake, young Dratinis and Dragonairs peeked their heads timidly above the water, cautiously gazing at the giant Dragonite overhead.

Their eyes met, and the Dratinis and Dragonairs shivered with fear.

Seeing this, D said to the Dragonite beside him, "Go tell everyone the news and tell them the giant Dragonite won't hurt them; it just wants to live alongside them!"

On the way here, he had already shared the story of this giant Dragonite with his Dragonite, saying that it was very lonely and wanted companions.

The Dragonite nodded, agreeing.

It flew into the air, letting out a howling roar that gathered all the adult Dragonites on the island, introducing this harmless Dragonite as instructed by D.

Meanwhile, D approached the lakeside with a grin, calling over the adorable Dragonairs and Dratinis.

"Alright, alright, don't be afraid; it won't hurt you!"

He soothed the little cuties' nerves as he opened his backpack, poured out the bottom.

Soon, a small mountain of snacks made of bread, Poffin, pudding, and berries was piled up in front of these little dragons.

As expected, they were attracted to the food, gathering around.

Seeing this, D smiled and caught a familiar Dragonair, asking, "Do you remember me?"

The Dragonair happily wriggled its body, "Woo~"

"Little one." D scratched its chin, smiling, "I promised last time I'd bring you food, here, eat together with everyone!"

He fed a piece of aromatic Baofen to the cute Dragonair.

The Dragonair swallowed the Baofen, a blissful expression on its face, then snuggled up to D, affectionately rubbing its face against his.

The other Dratinis and Dragonairs also joyfully surrounded D, enclosing him in the center.

Looking at them, D felt like the happiest person in the world, happily feeding them Poffin.

At this moment, the adult Dragonites also landed on the grass, showing empathetic expressions after hearing about the giant Dragonite's story.

No companions, wandering the sea in solitude, emitting sad cries at night...

So pitiful!

One particularly enthusiastic and bold Dragonite even flew into the air, opening its mouth wide, calling to the giant Dragonite.

It was saying, "Stay here and live with us from now on; we are all your companions!"

The giant Dragonite was deeply moved, tears like small pearls welled in its eyes, almost falling.

Half of its body emerged from the water, making a hugging gesture with its arms, cherishing the small island like it held the most precious treasure in the world.

---

---

---

---

# 163: Chapter 160: Beneath Dragon Island

The first encounter with the giant Dragonite was a very novel event for the group of Dragonite living on Dragonite Island.

Initially, they felt some fear in their hearts and only dared to watch from afar, not daring to approach.

But soon, they discovered that the giant Dragonite had no ill intentions and just wanted to befriend them.

Thus, a large group of Dragonite began to try to make contact with the giant Dragonite; some ran onto its back, while others flew around it several times, wanting to see how big it really was.

D, along with a group of Dratini and Dragonair, and a few Dragonite attracted by food, sat by the lakeshore watching the scene above.

"The giant Dragonite must be very happy right now, right?" he couldn't help but think.

The Dragonite's appetites were huge, and soon that pile of snacks was devoured completely by the group of Dragonite, Dratini, and Dragonair.

Dragonair, still wanting more to eat, looked over with hopeful eyes, uttering a soft growl: "Ooh..."

D: "..."

He shrugged helplessly: "There's none left. I brought all the food from home this time."

A look of disappointment appeared on the faces of the group of Dratini and Dragonair.

Seeing them like this, D thought for a moment and said, "I'll bring more next time I come over."

As expected, after he said this, the group of little ones let out a cheer, filled with joy and anticipation.

"Ooh~!"

"A bunch of little gluttons," D placed one Dragonair on his lap and tickled its belly.

Dragonair squirmed as he tickled it, letting out squeals to the point of tears almost coming out.

Once it regained its ability to move, it immediately dashed off, hiding in the lake, only exposing its head and refusing to let D touch its belly.

D chuckled, unbothered, and instead reached out to catch the somewhat timid pink Dratini.

The last time he came, this little one was still very afraid of him, but maybe the snacks had bridged the gap between them, as this time it was noticeably better.

D's mind wandered to a rumor he'd heard in his previous life.

It is said that Shiny Pokemon are very unwelcome in the community because their body color is an oddity to other Pokemon, possibly seen as ugly and thus isolated.

He looked at the other Dratini and Dragonair and asked:

"Do you think it's cute?"

Upon hearing this, the group of Pokemon all turned their attention to this unique companion.

"Ooh~"

A Dratini came over and affectionately rubbed the Shining Dratini's cheek, their relationship appearing very close.

The other Dratini and Dragonair did not show any signs of disgust but looked on cheerfully.

Seeing this scene, D couldn't help but feel emotional.

Obviously, in this group of Dragonite living on Dragon Island, that guess was not established.

Although their personalities differ, all of them are kind, gentle, and helpful.

D took a deep breath and then lay down on the grass, his gaze passing through the dense circle of trees, looking at the sky overhead.

In the azure sky, white clouds float, the golden sun shines, making it almost impossible to open one's eyes. When the sea breeze blows, the hot air spreads in all directions.

Luckily, tall and dense tree clusters grow around Dragonite Island, perfectly keeping the high temperatures outside, making the Lake of the Moone in the center of the island seem like an idyllic paradise.

With the shade of trees, fertile soil, perhaps influenced by the energy released by Dragonite, vegetation on this island grows particularly lush, and Berries are particularly large and plump.

The Dragonite really chose a good place to live!

Making him want to come here often for vacations in the future.

D lay on the grass while the Dratini and Dragonair around him played on their own.

They chased each other, ran into the lake with a splash, then left the lake, rushing into the dense tree clusters.

This might just be the daily life of this group of Pokemon?

D did not feel bored; instead, he found it very leisurely and interesting.

Suddenly, a sound of a giant creature entering the water came from outside the island.

D sat up abruptly from the ground, looked around, and then ran to the eastern cliff of the island with a group of equally curious Dragonite and Dragonair.

At this time, the adult Dragonite of the island were all gathered here, including his own Dragonite.

The waves continuously crashed against the steep cliff, and the Dragonite collectively watched the scene in the sea.

"Roar!"

The giant Dragonite showed a trace of fatigue on its face, it let out a melodious roar towards the Dragonite on the island, and then gradually submerged into the sea, disappearing from sight.

Seeing this, the Dragonite also plunged into the water one after another.

D, puzzled, also wanted to follow and take a look.

He looked up at the sky and called out to Dragonite, immediately, beams of light from all around focused on him.

D: "..."

"Awoo!" Dragonite proudly flew to D, boasting to its old mates: "Awoo!" He's calling me! Not you guys!

All Dragonite: "..."

"Take me down to have a look!" D flipped onto Dragonite's back, and immediately plunged into the sea with its guidance.

Luckily, he learned to swim in a previous life, which came in handy at this moment.

At the moment of entering the water, all surrounding sounds seemed to vanish.

Familiar things disappeared without a trace, replaced by a more expansive azure world before his eyes.

---

A large number of normal sea fish live in this area, and they gathered together, seemingly sensing the presence of predators, and immediately swam farther away.

Holding Dragonite by the neck, D dove into the sea with it.

The gigantic Dragonite was just ahead, like a giant sea monster, swimming towards deeper waters, eventually reaching beneath the island, where it lay down, closed its eyes, and began to sleep.

The Dragonites watched this scene, and only after the gigantic Dragonite fell asleep did they return to the shore one by one.

Although it was unclear what the gigantic Dragonite said, from its and the Dragonites' reactions, it seemed it was tired and wanted to get a good sleep.

D asked his Dragonite for confirmation, "Did it just say that it's going to sleep?"

The Dragonite nodded its big head, "Grrr!"

D remarked, "I thought so."

The Dragonite added, "Grrr, grrr." And that's not all!

In its mind, the words of the gigantic Dragonite appeared.

It said, "I'm tired, I'm going to sleep, and I'll play with you all after I wake up!"

It said, "If any baddies hurt you or threaten the island, just wake me up, and I'll give them a good lesson."

The Dragonites thought it would go to the depths of the sea to sleep, but unexpectedly, it went nowhere, just beneath Dragonite Island.

Unfortunately, D didn't understand what the Dragonite was saying.

He just pondered whether the gigantic Dragonite needed to eat.

Berries? Fish shoals in the sea? Or maybe, like Groudon and Kyogre, it doesn't need food and can survive on natural energy?

The answer to this question might have to wait until the gigantic Dragonite wakes up.

With the gigantic Dragonite issue perfectly resolved, D and Dragonite began to bid farewell to the dragons on Dragon Island.

The Dragonites, Dragonairs, and Dratinis all had faces full of reluctance to part, with many wanting to step forward and go home with D, but were glared back by Dragonite.

D noticed their little actions and couldn't help but smile, "Alright, we'll visit again next time; we'll stop here for now! See you later!"

D waved at the group of Dragonites, and under their reluctant gazes, rode Dragonite to begin the return journey.

Dragonite Island gradually disappeared behind them over the sea, and D reflected on everything that happened today, thinking he might well be considered a human friend of Dragonite Island by now?

After all, that group of Dragonites quite liked him.

However...

Today's events were really quite frightening.

The surprise almost turned into shock.

If the gigantic Dragonite's temperament had been a bit more aggressive and it had forcefully broken through the Paldea League's defenses to land, it would surely have resulted in a very fierce battle.

It's unknown how strong the real-world head champion Geeta is, and whether she could suppress the gigantic Dragonite.

After using Agility and Extreme Speed, Dragonite's speed reached a very outrageous degree.

Soon, they arrived in the Paldea Region.

D intended to return directly to the ranch but noticed someone waiting for him on the Paldea West Coast.

Compared to before, the League line of defense on this coast had been withdrawn, and Miss Jenny, along with a group of reporters and Tulip, had all left.

Only a helicopter was left on the entire beach, and Geeta stood by the helicopter, looking at the sky.

D intended to pretend not to see and fly away, but from a distance, he saw Geeta wave to him from the ground.

Seeing this, he had no choice but to land Dragonite there.

Geeta glanced up and down at the young man in front of her, noticing his clothes were half-dry and half-wet, most likely from going into the sea.

She spoke, "It seems the problem has been resolved. On behalf of the Paldea League, I thank you."

D casually replied, "It's nothing; since it started because of me, it's only right for me to solve it."

Hearing this, Geeta nodded, thinking this Trainer had a rather nice personality.

She then asked, "May I ask where that gigantic Dragonite went?"

"Somewhere it should be."

D saw the unease in Geeta's heart, paused, and continued, "Rest assured, it won't come to the Paldea Region again."

"That's all I needed." Then Geeta shifted the topic, "I guess you haven't participated in a Gym challenge yet?"

D was slightly taken aback, then shook his head, "Not yet."

Geeta: "Are you interested in attending the Paldea Academy? Start there by taking on Gym challenges and becoming the Paldea Champion."

Now D understood; Geeta was treating him as talent and thought of recruiting him.

He had no doubt that once he achieved these goals, Geeta would try to pull him into the Pokemon League to work.

Just like poor sales department employee Larry, who served as both a Gym Leader and an Elite Four, and from long-term exhaustion, always seemed somewhat out of breath when speaking.

He didn't want that kind of life; living peacefully was the real deal.

D declined without a second thought, "No need, I quite prefer being a farmer."

He didn't forget to pitch his farm products to this head champion:

"Oh, by the way, Champion, if you get the chance, you could buy some milk and vegetables from me; they're very fresh!"

When the Dragonite Delivery Company is established, he would use Geeta as a spokesperson; if the head champion eats his farm's carrots, would there be any worry about not selling them?

When the livestreaming industry takes off, he could start streaming to sell products, selling his farm products through online shopping via the Dragonite Delivery Company throughout the Paldea region.

The image of himself holding a big sign with "Farm Products Not Selling, Help Us" came to D's mind.

No, that's too embarrassing.

D shook his head internally, shaking off these messy thoughts.

Geeta: "..."

She had never encountered such a Trainer before and for a moment didn't know what to say.

After pondering for quite a while, Geeta hesitated and nodded once, politely saying, "If I have the chance, I'll give it a try."

Geeta was still quite young at that time, and her control over her facial expressions wasn't as good, far from the maturity and experience she showed in the games.

D found it amusing but said nothing more, turning to mount Dragonite and bidding farewell to her as he began the return trip.

When he returned to the ranch, it was already four in the afternoon.

D patted the heads of Koraidon, Cyclizar, and other Pokemon that came to greet him, intending to go inside for a drink of water, when he suddenly noticed Naclstack playing on the grass.

He went over and asked, "Have you checked out your new home yet? It's already built."

"Stack?"

A few Pokemon looked puzzled, seemingly unaware of this.

D couldn't help but facepalm, saying helplessly, "Alright, I'll take you to have a look, follow me!"

He led the Naclstack and Nacli into the Salt House, down a path leading to the Underground Cave.

Looking at the wide space, the four Naclstack and Nacli were full of surprise and excitement.

"Nacl! Nacl!"

"Stack～"

"Like it?" D laughed.

In the summer, when the surface is extremely hot, this cave remains cool, much loved by those cave-dwelling Pokemon.

He walked to the edge of the underground lake and said:

"There's water here, so you can drink if you're thirsty. I'll install feeder bowls here later, and I'll put your favorite Rock Salt Fried Soil and savory food inside."

Saying so, D went up with Koraidon to move the feed bowls originally installed in the Salt House down and placed them in the cave, filling them with fried soil and savory food.

The Naclstack and Nacli were not hungry yet, wandering around the Underground Cave, seemingly getting familiar with the environment.

Watching this, D suddenly felt they were like the little moles in fairy tales, establishing an underground kingdom.

He yawned, feeling a bit weary, and after bidding farewell to Nacli and Naclstack, turned and returned to the house.

D first took a sip of water, then glanced at the old wall clock and saw it was still early, so he flopped onto the bed and said to Koraidon, who followed him in:

"I'm going to take a nap; you go outside and play with Dragonite and the others!"

---

---

---

---

# 164: Chapter 161: Night of the Fireflies

While D was sleeping, a group of Pokemon were playing outside.

Koraidon felt something was not quite right with D's health, as if he was sick.

Worried, it occasionally went inside to check on D in between teaching Dragonite and Cyclizar how to practice their moves.

Only when it heard D snoring did it finally feel at ease.

Gradually, it got dark.

D hadn't woken up yet, and a few Pokemon whose stomachs were growling with hunger exchanged glances and, in harmony, decided not to disturb him, wanting to let him rest a bit longer.

Koraidon quietly tiptoed to the refrigerator, opened it, and took out several large cups of Berry Milk, then took a large bag of berries D had bought from town out of the cupboard.

It handed the milk to Cyclizar and Dragonite, who were waiting nearby.

The three Pokemon, holding the berries and milk, tiptoed out of the room, considerately closing the door for D.

On the clean and soft lawn, Koraidon, holding the berries, called out to the Pokemon playing outside: "Aa gaa sii!"

Hearing the call, Pawmo, Alcremie, and the other Pokemon gathered around.

Koraidon led Cyclizar and Dragonite in handing out the berries to everyone, indicating that this was dinner for today and that D should rest more, so he did not have to cook tonight.

The Pokemon agreed happily. Although the berries were simple, they still ate joyfully.

Unlike ordinary fruits, berries are rich in nutrients and energy. For a Pokemon like Pawmo, eating two berries would be enough to fill its stomach.

After dinner, the sky gradually darkened.

Koraidon, like a big child, took the leftover berries and milk cups back into the house.

When it came out again, it saw Dragonite and Cyclizar had run to the riverbank to watch the fireflies.

Every night, these little insects, emitting faint glows, would gather by the riverbank, accompanied by the gentle flow of the river, creating a beautiful scene.

An idea suddenly popped into Koraidon's mind

——These fireflies disappear during the day; if they catch them and release them into the room, could the beautiful scene be seen all the time?

Then, Koraidon found the bag used to store berries and called over Arcanine, Glaceon, Pawmo, and Dolliv to help catch fireflies and put them in the bag.

After hearing Koraidon's plan, the Pokemon's eyes lit up, and they all joined in the operation to catch fireflies.

Each displayed their divine powers, unleashing moves like Ice Beam, Thunderbolt, and Gust all at once.

As a result, although they caught the fireflies, none of them could glow.

Koraidon couldn't help but facepalm: "...Aa gaa sii."

It handed the bag to Cyclizar and personally demonstrated how to catch them for the group.

Slowing its pace, it focused and held its breath, its golden pupils locking onto a firefly quietly levitating.

In the next second, Koraidon moved. It lunged forward, clasping its paws together, trapping the firefly in its palms.

Dragonite, Glaceon, Pawmo, Cyclizar, and Arcanine gathered around.

Koraidon carefully opened its hand, and the Pokemon saw a firefly inside, flickering with yellow-green light.

"Aa gaa sii."

After finishing the demonstration, Koraidon tucked the firefly into the bag.

Witnessing all this, the Pokemon's heads all lit up with a bulb-like flash.

They imitated Koraidon's method and started catching fireflies accordingly.

And indeed, it was quite effective.

In no time, the bag was filled with fireflies.

Other fireflies along the river were also startled and flew away.

Koraidon, carrying the bundle, eagerly wanted to release the beautiful fireflies into the house and share them with D.

A group of Pokemon ran into the dim room, carefully closing the doors and windows before Koraidon opened the bag.

Immediately, one by one, the fireflies flew out from the bag, their faint glows forming a bright Milky Way in the darkness, illuminating the faces of the Pokemon with anticipation.

"Bamo..."

"Gao..."

So beautiful!

"Aa gaa sii!"

Once all the fireflies were in the room, Koraidon shouted excitedly, then ran to the bedside and licked D awake with its wet tongue.

——Although there were better ways to wake D, Koraidon was too happy at this moment so it used this more intimate method.

So, when D opened his eyes from his dream, he saw floating fireflies in the dark room, like a starry sky.

D was stunned for a second, then realized these were not stars, but fireflies!

"What's going on?" D said, his voice somewhat hoarse. He thought he probably had a cold, likely due to not drying his clothes properly after swimming in the sea this afternoon.

Koraidon smiled: "Aa gaa sii～"

Looking at the Pokemon gathered in the room, D realized: "You all did this?"

"Awoo～" Dragonite nodded repeatedly.

The Pokemon looked over with anticipation, hoping that D would like it.

Although there was a language barrier, after living with them for so long, D could still understand their intentions through their expressions, tone, and body language.

Just like now.

He looked up at the glowing fireflies in the room and thought of it as a present from everyone, feeling a bit touched inside.

"Thank you," D grinned: "I really like it."

The few Pokemon, who were already filled with expectations, immediately beamed with happy smiles upon hearing this.

"Aa gaa sii～"

"..."

D glanced at the time and saw it was getting late. He thought that these Pokemon might still be hungry and was about to get up to make them dinner.

However, upon learning his intention, Pawmo shook its head: "Bamo!"

Koraidon showed D the bag of berries and pointed to the milk bottle on the table.

"You've already eaten?" D was a bit surprised.

The Pokemon all nodded simultaneously.

D's eyes scanned their faces, seeing their looks, thinking this was probably Koraidon's idea.

"Still hungry?" D got up to prepare his own dinner, asking who hadn't eaten enough so he could make more servings.

But all the Pokemon shook their heads; after finishing that bag of berries, their bellies were full.

D: "..." It seems he'd have to buy a few more bags of berries from town tomorrow.

Since Koraidon and the others were full, tonight he only needed to prepare his meal.

D simply fried an egg and cooked a bowl of noodles, sprinkled with scallions and sesame oil, and placed the fried egg on top of the noodles.

After dinner, it was time for bed.

Koraidon eagerly squatted at the side of the bed.

D had promised it during the day that it could sleep on the bed with him tonight.

"Come on up!"

D patted the spot beside him, and Koraidon immediately jumped up, lying down next to him like a big golden retriever.

Cyclizar showed a look of envy.

Seeing this, D felt a bit helpless and called it to his other side.

"You all sleep first; I'll watch some TV."

D wasn't particularly sleepy, so he reclined against the pillow and started watching TV.

Koraidon lay on his left, Cyclizar on his right, Pawmo was nestled on his abdomen, and he was squeezed in the middle, unable to move.

In the dark room, only the sound of the TV and the glowing fireflies flickering remained.

D looked up at the fireflies for a while, then glanced at the few Pokemon beside him, and couldn't help but smile.

---

---

---

---

# 165: Chapter 162: Translating Sunflower Seeds! I Can Understand Pokemon Speak!

Year 198 of the League Calendar, May 6th, Summer.

The weather was getting hotter. Except for doing farm work, D spent almost every day at home under the air conditioner, watching TV and making desserts with Alcremie to share with everyone on the ranch.

As time passed, the crops in the fields began to grow and mature one after another.

The vines of the hops climbed the trellises, golden-yellow pumpkins and dark green watermelons lay quietly on the ground, and the corn grew tall, though not fully ripe yet.

The wheat and pineapples were also almost fully grown, but still needed some time before they matured.

Of course, what pleased D the most was the Sunflora Field full of sunflowers.

The golden flower heads faced the sun, with green stems and leaves below, looking extremely beautiful.

Looking at all of this, even though he had anticipated it, D still felt very miraculous.

The growth cycle of crops in this world was unbelievably fast.

These sunflowers had bloomed and grown seven days ago, and now, under the nurturing energy of Gogoat, they were starting to mature.

D braved the hot sun to inspect the crops in the field.

[Pumpkin: A crop used for cooking, will be ready for harvest in 3 days.]

[Watermelon: A cool and refreshing summer crop, already ripe and ready for harvest.]

[Corn: Not yet ripe, will be ready for harvest in 10 days.]

[Hops: A crop for brewing barley beer, not yet ripe, will be ready for harvest in 10 days.]

"Looks like the other crops will have to wait a bit longer," D thought to himself.

Right now, only sunflowers and watermelons could be harvested from the field.

Remembering the mutated giant watermelon from last time, D walked into the watermelon patch and Sunflora Field. Using his Golden Finger, he carefully checked each watermelon and sunflower, wanting to see if there were any changes.

However, the result was somewhat disappointing.

In the entire two fields, not a single mutated crop was found.

"The mutation probability is really low."

Feeling a bit disappointed, D quickly recovered, reminding himself that only two types of crops had matured, and many others remained.

Turning his gaze towards the nearby pumpkin patch, his eyes filled with renewed anticipation.

Time to start the harvest!

D called over Koraidon, Cyclizar, and Dragonite to lend a hand, picking the ripe watermelons and piling them in the shade.

He planned to sell part of these watermelons, send some to Glaceon's Ice House to chill, and keep the rest for culinary experiments.

The giant watermelon from last time was entirely consumed, so he hadn't had the chance to see what dishes could be made from watermelon.

By the time the work was done, it was already ten in the morning.

D split open five watermelons for the Pokémon, allowing them to sit in the shade and enjoy.

The three Gogoats and Miltank each received one, the three Combee shared one, and Naclstack showed no interest in watermelons; they preferred dirt and Berries sprinkled with salt.

Watching the Pokémon relish the treat, D felt the entire ranch was brimming with the joy of harvest.

He smiled, turned, and cut down a mature sunflower, plucking the almost bursting sunflower seeds to roast later for snacking during idle times.

In this age without advanced mobile networks, sunflower seed snacking was a great way to pass the time.

Gathered from one sunflower, the seeds filled a large basin, which D washed, dried, and borrowed a bowl of caramel cream from Alcremie.

He then added the sunflower seeds, a bit of Rock Salt, and caramel cream into a pan, stirring continuously.

About twenty minutes later, the sunflower seeds were fully roasted.

Once it was ready, D turned off the heat and poured the caramel-scented seeds into the basin.

Looking down, the information about the seeds appeared in his mind.

[Translation Nuts (B+): Roasted with caramel cream and Purifying Salt.

Cooking Effect: Language communication (minor). After consumption, able to understand Pokémon language, one nut translates one word, effect cumulative.

Evaluation: A very nice little snack, filled with the aroma of caramel cream. Eating it makes one feel happiness. It's the best choice for leisure time.]

Hmm? What is this?!

D widened his eyes in disbelief, staring at the basin of seeds in front of him.

To verify the effect of this dish, he nibbled two nuts, then ran outside to find the Butterfree relaxing under the eaves, shouting:

"Butterfree?"

Butterfree turned its head, and seeing D, a joyful expression appeared on its face: "Um-ee um-ee?" (What um-ee?)

D: "!!!" He really understood!

Returning inside, he held the bowl of nuts, grinning like a fool.

Undoubtedly, the practicality of this bowl of nuts far surpassed all the dishes he had made before!

Half a month ago, he was envious of the Gardevoir capable of Telepathy, and now he could directly communicate with Pokémon.

Enabling the eater to communicate directly with Pokémon…

Pairing this with fatigue-recovery dishes, the effect could rival the rare Power of Viridian!

Invincible!

D immediately began devouring the nuts voraciously, and after consuming an unknown number, he ran to the grass outside, finding Dragonite and other Pokémon enjoying their watermelon.

D shouted: "Dragonite!"

Startled, Dragonite turned its head: "Aoo?" (What's up?)

D chuckled: "Nothing much, just came over to chat with you."

Dragonite: "Aoo… Aoo?" (Why suddenly like this… haven't we always been able to chat every day?)

D grinned: "That's because, now I can directly understand you!"

"Aoo?!" (What?!)

"Gao?!" (What?!)

"..." (What?!)

Hearing this, the Pokémon looked over, faces filled with surprise and curiosity.

Dragonite continued to chatter, asking how it was possible since D couldn't understand before.

Then, they saw D mysteriously produce a small nut.

D: "It's because I made a snack that allows me to understand you!"

"Gao?" Cyclizar eyed the nut, puzzled.

D explained the effect of the nut: "…It allows me to understand you, each nut makes me understand one word."

Upon hearing this, the Pokémon were all surprised.

How magical?!

However, as they talked, D heard Dragonite's words revert to "Aoo, aoo."

D: "..." It seemed the accumulated word count had run out.

Looking toward the distant fields, he made an important decision:

From today, expand the planting of sunflowers!

---

---

---

---

# 166: Chapter 163: Watermelon Knight Dragonite—Summer Special (4k)

That afternoon, D went to the town and bought several big bags of sunflower seeds from the seed shop.

However, he didn't plant them right away; he planned to wait until the current crops all matured and were harvested before starting the planting.

Sunflowers are a crop that can be planted season after season, even in autumn, they can continue to be planted.

This way, before the cold winter arrives, he can accumulate a large batch of seeds, shell them, and when he wants to talk to the Pokemon, he can grab a handful and toss them into his mouth.

As for getting a sore throat from eating too much...

If he eats a bit more veggie berries to balance it out, it should be fine.

At five in the afternoon, Zack came to collect the goods.

D moved a few ripe watermelons, Gogoat's goat milk, and Miltank's milk in front of him.

Looking at those crops that were about to mature in the field, Zack grinned and said, "It's another good harvest season! I heard other farms have also had good yields."

D's heart stirred: "Has anyone's crops matured early?"

Zack shook his head: "No, not really."

He didn't know what he thought of, then turned to look at the Calyrex shrine placed not far away and said with a smile:

"Because of the huge watermelon you shared with everyone last time, people from nearby farms and the community started to believe in Calyrex, and they bought statues from Keri to worship at home. This harvest, everyone feels that the Calyrex might be secretly helping out; that Legendary Pokemon is really something!"

D: "..."

He suddenly became a bit worried that the townspeople might overly rely on the power of Calyrex, thereby abandoning their previous hardworking nature, and couldn't help but subtly remind him.

"It's also a result of everyone's effort."

Zack heard his point and laughed heartily: "Don't worry! Everyone knows just like the stories say, if people become too lazy, they might get annoying to the Legendary Pokemon and lose its favor."

"So, while everyone is grateful to Calyrex, we also rely on our own strength to work hard in farming."

Hearing these words, D was finally relieved.

Zack handed over today's money for shipments: "Here's today's money. I have to head off now, other places are waiting for me to collect goods! See you tomorrow!"

"See you tomorrow!"

D bid farewell to Zack, then turned and headed to Glaceon's Ice House to bring out an ice-cold watermelon.

Thanks to the power of the Ice Stone, the watermelon's surface was already covered with a layer of delicate frost.

D brought it and several other watermelons back to the house and began experimenting with making dishes using watermelon.

The first thing to be made was a refreshing and cooling ice-cold watermelon juice.

D cut open the ice-cold watermelon, threw the flesh into a juicer, plugged it in, and the juicer started humming.

Soon, cups of icy red watermelon juice were ready.

D looked down.

[Ice-cold Watermelon Juice (B): A summer drink made from ice-chilled watermelon.

Cooking Effect: Heat Resistance lv1, after consumption, can withstand hot weather, prevent heatstroke, duration 15 minutes.

Rating: Sweet and delicious watermelon juice, a perfect drink to tantalize taste buds, feeling the chill during the hot summer with just one cup.]

Heat resistance...

D recalled he had made several dishes with similar effects before, like hot soup noodles, whose effect was cold resistance.

Overall, it was decent and within his expectations.

Next, D also tried making shaved ice watermelon, fried watermelon rind, watermelon popsicles, watermelon jelly, and other delicacies.

However, the effects of these foods were not very ideal; they were either refreshing or restored stamina (small). The only slightly useful one was the watermelon jelly.

[Watermelon Jelly (C+): A jelly made from freshly picked watermelon.

Cooking Effect: Watermelon Knight, after being fed to Pokemon, if they wear a helmet made of watermelon, they can transform into Watermelon Knights, attracting the opponent's attention and lowering the opponent's attack and defense during battles.

Rating: Q-bouncy and smooth, soft and delicious, it's a snack suitable for all ages.]

D: "..."

Though useful, the effect seemed somewhat trivial.

Looking at the pile of leftover watermelon rind in front of him, D thought since he was idle now, he might as well give it a try and see the effect.

He cleaned one fairly intact watermelon rind, then cut away the excess parts with a knife, making it into a helmet styled after a motorcycle helmet.

He carried the watermelon rind outside and happened to see Dragonite lying on the grass, sleeping, so he called him up.

Dragonite heard the noise and sat up from the ground: "Roar?"

"Want something good to power up?" D whispered mysteriously.

On hearing this, Dragonite's eyes lit up and it nodded repeatedly: "Roar!"

D first tossed the watermelon jelly into Dragonite's mouth.

Watching Dragonite swallow the jelly whole and then take out the watermelon helmet he had hidden behind him, he placed it on Dragonite's head under his skeptical gaze.

With a muffled pop, Dragonite's horns pierced through the watermelon rind, fitting the watermelon helmet perfectly on Dragonite's head, making it look comical yet adorable.

D couldn't hold back, let out a chuckle.

"Roar..."

Dragonite raised its eyes to look upward but couldn't see its appearance.

---

Koraidon ran over at this moment, and upon seeing the watermelon perched on Dragonite's head, it burst into laughter with a "gagagaga" sound.

Dragonite widened its eyes, looking at D with an expression of disbelief.

How dare he play tricks on it!

Feeling mocked, Dragonite was devastated and about to cry, "Awoo..."

D refrained from laughing, shook his head repeatedly upon seeing Dragonite's look:

"No, it's not that I want to laugh, it's just that seeing you like this makes it hard not to laugh."

Dragonite: "..." Isn't that the same thing?

D continued, "You're aware that the dishes I make have magical effects, right?"

"Awoo." Dragonite nodded its head.

"The jelly you just ate also has effects; its purpose is that if you wear the watermelon helmet after eating it, you can gain an advantage in battle."

D explained in detail, "For example, lowering the opponent's attack and defense. Just imagine, your appearance makes them laugh uncontrollably, so they can't concentrate and expose weaknesses."

"In a way, it definitely reduces their defense, and when they're laughing, their muscles aren't fully functional, so their strength also decreases. See, I'm not wrong, right?"

"Awoo..." Dragonite thought it over carefully, looked at the laughing Koraidon, then nodded.

Seems like that's indeed the case.

It then carefully stored the watermelon helmet away, "Awoo!"

From now on, this helmet will be its treasured item!

"Alright, then I'll give it to you," D waved his hand casually.

Anyway, this thing will soon rot and can't be stored for long; it's pretty useless.

*

Tonight, the ranch had a feast of watermelon.

Stir-fried watermelon rind, watermelon juice, watermelon jelly, watermelon rind boiled with ribs, watermelon sandwiches...

By the end, a group of Pokemon collapsed weakly onto the ground, feeling they'll never want to eat watermelon again.

Seeing their state, D showed an apologetic smile.

He couldn't help it; he had sliced open many watermelons and juiced them, so if they aren't finished, they'd spoil.

After dinner, a group of Pokemon ran outside to bask in the moonlight; D wasn't sleepy either, so he grabbed a handful of seeds and prepared to chat with the Naclstack in the Underground Cave.

Yet before he reached the destination, the seeds he ate were used up.

Not far away, Dragonite lying on the grass, "Awuuwu!" (The moon is so beautiful!)

Cyclizar: "Gaao!" (It's really nice!)

Koraidon yawned, "Agaass..." (Hasn't the moon always been like this?)

Glaceon leisurely walking in the flower field, "Buuii!" (This flower smells so good!)

Butterfree dancing above, "Muuuii~" (Mmm mmm~)

Bird-type Pokemon flying over the ranch, "Gagaga!" (It's dark, time to go home, go home!)

Group of Psyduck across the river, "Kadaka!" (Really want to swim in the river!)

"Kada!" (You'll be washed away!)

"Kadakadaka!" (Lots of delicious food grows across the river, smells so good, really want to eat them, Kada!)

D was speechless.

How can even the ambient sounds of wild Pokemon be automatically translated?

From this perspective, the translation nuts are quite a pointless ability!

And what's with this group of wild Psyduck wanting to steal his farm crops!

D cast his gaze across the river at the group of Psyduck, as expected, several Psyduck were watching over this side.

As he approached, a few Psyduck promptly shifted their gaze to him.

D ate a few seeds, only to hear those Psyduck exclaim in panic, "Kada!" (We've been discovered, Kada!)

D: "..."

Pretending not to hear, he turned and walked into the Salt House, following the path to reach the Underground Cave where the Naclstack lived.

Upon reaching the bottom, he heard sounds of a fight.

D went closer to see the unevolved Nacli sparring with a Naclstack.

Witnessing this scene, he wasn't surprised, as this was his suggestion to Nacli.

Last time, after eating that pot of Rock Salt Fried Soil, Nacli still hadn't evolved and felt down for awhile, so D offered another method.

That is to spar with other Pokemon, acquiring evolutionary experience through battles, honing its body to meet the evolution conditions.

Seeing him arrive, Nacli and Naclstack stopped.

D cracked a handful of seeds, asking, "How do you feel?"

Nacli: "Yanyan~" (Feeling great~)

"That's good." D checked Nacli's body, then stated, "Very soon, you'll evolve into a more formidable Naclstack."

"Yanyan~" Nacli bounced twice on the ground, thinking about what D has done for it these days, it felt touched: "Yanyan!" (Love D the most!)

Hearing this, D was slightly stunned, then realized it was Nacli's genuine inner thought.

He smiled and said, "Thank you, I really like you too."

Nacli joyfully jumped around even more.

D reached out to pat its head, just as he was about to pick it up, he suddenly saw a dazzling light burst from its body.

---

In the midst of the glowing light, its appearance was rapidly changing.

That was evolution!

A moment later, the light dissipated, and a very strong Naclstack appeared before D.

With this, all five Nacli had fully evolved.

"Stack~!" (Evolved, so happy!)

"Congratulations, Naclstack." D stood up, looking at the five Naclstack in front of him with emotion.

He recalled the scene when he first encountered them.

To be honest, there's nothing more fulfilling in this world than watching a Pokemon evolve and grow up right before your eyes.

From little guys to big ones, from weak Pokemons with no self-defense ability to strong contenders.

Perhaps this is the joy of nurturing?

D chatted with the group of Naclstack for a while, and seeing one of them showing a sleepy expression, he decided to leave.

"I won't disturb your rest, see you tomorrow!"

"Stack~" (See you tomorrow~)

Just as he was about to leave, he suddenly heard a rustling sound from the corner of the cave.

Huh?

D was a bit surprised, thinking it might be another wild Pokemon attracted to this cave.

With curiosity, he stepped forward and soon saw a small mound on a flat piece of ground.

There were faint sounds coming from inside.

"Squeak squeak squeak!" (Can't see me, can't see me, squeak!)

D: "..."

Oh, he thought this Diglett had run away! Turns out it was hiding underground!

He called out, "Diglett, come out, I can see you already!"

With that, D saw the small mound, as if startled, turn and scurry deeper.

D said helplessly, "Diglett, don't run, stay and work for me. I will provide you with food every day, as long as you can help me till the land!"

With these words, the fleeing Diglett stopped.

It cautiously poked its head out of the mound: "Squeak?" (Really?)

"Of course it's true," D smiled, "Your past incidents of damaging the farmland will be forgotten. Now, I want to formally hire you to work at the ranch. As compensation, I'll provide you with three meals a day."

"Squeak?!"

Diglett was both surprised and delighted.

The word count is exhausted, D couldn't understand Diglett's words, but judging by its expression, it seemed to have agreed.

After a brief thought, he said, "Then from today on, you can live here!"

Digletts make their nests underground, and they rarely come to the surface because the strong sunlight makes their bodies weak.

D paused, then continued, "I'll come and call you when I need you."

Coincidentally, he was planning to expand the sunflower planting area in the next few days. With a Diglett good at tilling, the work would be much easier, and he could open up more land for farming.

This way, the more crops planted, the greater the chance for them to mutate under Gogoat's Grassy Terrain.

Diglett squeaked a few times in agreement.

D asked, "Where have you been these days?"

He took out a handful of sunflower seeds and started munching.

"Squeak, squeak squeak."

Diglett said it had been in the area all along because its nest is here.

Unseen by D, directly beneath the ranch, it had dug many small tunnels and usually didn't come to the surface.

There wasn't any food above ground before, until recently, when Diglett discovered that there were people living above its burrow and crops planted there.

It was attracted to the crops, and then everything else happened.

Hearing this, D nodded, understanding that they were actually the newcomers to this land!

"No problem, then you can stay here!" Seeing Diglett glance at the Naclstack, D reassured, "Rest assured, those Naclstack won't harm you, they're very gentle Pokemon."

"Squeak." Diglett agreed.

---

---

---

---

# 167: Chapter 164: Diglett's Home

The next day, after breakfast, D called for Diglett, planning to carve out a piece of land in the southeast corner of the ranch for planting sunflowers.

After devouring the sandwich D offered, Diglett felt brimming with strength.

Its eyes were filled with determination, and as soon as D designated the area to be cultivated, Diglett began to move.

It tucked its head underground and moved swiftly with astonishing agility.

D was very interested in Diglett's plowing, so he didn't go anywhere and simply stood on the lawn, observing.

As Diglett passed by, mounds of earth rose on the once flat surface, resembling neat rows of field ridges.

The surrounding soil became loose, making it perfect for cultivation.

Time ticked by, and after about forty minutes, the entire field had been plowed.

Exhausted, Diglett emerged from the ground, sweating and letting out a breath of relief, with an excited look on its little face.

It's done!

Well, not bad, right?

Diglett looked hopefully at its employer.

Seeing its expression, D couldn't help but smile: "You've worked hard, Diglett. Here, have an Oran Berry first, and leave the rest to me!"

Diglett happily accepted the Berry, then sat to the side, munching on it while watching D busy at work.
