Planting sunflowers is different from planting wheat; each sunflower needs to be spaced further apart to absorb enough soil nutrients for healthy growth.

With experience from planting, D had accumulated some knowledge.

He first judged the distance between each ridge with his eyes, then used his hand to dig into the soft earth, planting a sunflower seed each time, repeating the process.

By the time everything was done, it was already half-past ten in the morning.

The sun was shining brightly, and the temperature in the air was growing hotter.

In such weather, the crops in the field can easily dry up and lack water.

D called for Dragonite to bring down rain to nourish the field, ensuring the sunflowers grow healthily.

Dragonite used Rain Dance.

Before long, a gentle drizzle began falling from the sky.

Diglett stood in awe.

It looked at the sky, then at the field, its furry little face full of confusion.

It's raining? Why is it only raining in that one spot?

D moved to stand beside Diglett, watching Dragonite control the weather.

He noticed Diglett's expression and explained, "This is a Water Type move called Rain Dance, which causes it to rain."

"Chirp chirp," Diglett nodded absentmindedly.

Then, suddenly recalling something, it squeaked at D before turning around to lead the way.

"What's the matter?" D raised an eyebrow in surprise, then followed it.

Soon, with Diglett leading, he arrived at a field of golden wheat.

D planned to use this wheat for malt, brewing beer alongside hops and other materials.

Diglett stopped by the wheat field, and D noticed a small hole in the grass.

Diglett burrowed into the hole, poking its head out: "Chirp chirp chirp!"

Seeing this, D quickly went back inside to get some sunflower seeds and started snacking on them.

"Say it again."

Diglett, though puzzled, repeated: "Chirp chirp chirp!" (You can... call me... from here... I'll hear... and come out!)

D understood that Diglett was letting him know he could call it from this hole; it would hear and come running from underground.

"Got it," he nodded.

"Chirp chirp~," Diglett chirped twice more, then retreated back into the hole.

With the weather so hot, staying outside was making its body weaker under the sunlight.

"Then you should head back first!" D understood.

Then, he went to the storage room, using wooden planks, nails, and a hammer to craft a small wooden sign, on which he wrote "Diglett's Home" before placing it by the hole.

Watching this scene, D clapped the dust off his hands in satisfaction.

The farm work in the field was mostly done for the day, and with the heat, he didn't feel like staying outside. Instead, he returned inside to watch TV and enjoy the air conditioning.

Dragonite, Cyclizar, Koraidon, and other Pokémon who disliked the cold but were fine with heat weren't affected by the hot weather at all, still playfully running and chasing each other on the lawn.

Gogoat and Dolliv stayed in the pen with a rain shelter, so they didn't feel hot for a while.

Moreover, the ample sunlight benefits Grass Type Pokémon with Synthesis, aiding their growth.

Alcremie followed the five Naclstack into the cool underground cave to escape the heat, not forgetting to bring along Glaceon.

As a result, D was left alone in the room.

He ate watermelon with the air conditioner on, reclining in a chair and watching a Pokémon movie on TV, enjoying his leisure time.

But this leisure was soon interrupted.

Dragonite came running in from outside, holding a wilting watermelon rind, looking at D with a wronged expression.

At first, D was puzzled, but upon seeing the rotten watermelon rind in Dragonite's claws, he was speechless.

"I thought something serious had happened; this thing is bound to rot."

Dragonite's eyes widened: "Roar roar!"

D laid back in his chair: "Don't worry, I'll order a plastic watermelon helmet for you online later."

Realizing afterward, however, that in this world online shopping wasn't a thing yet, D couldn't help but facepalm: "I'll take you to the toy store in town later this evening to see if they have any watermelon helmets."

Dragonite was satisfied: "Roar~"

Seeing its expression, D added, "But I'm not sure if a plastic watermelon helmet would still give you the same help."

After all, the description in the recipe was for a helmet made of watermelon, not a helmet styled after one, and there's a significant difference between the two.

"Roar?" Dragonite was stunned, unsure of what to do.

It looked down at the rotten watermelon rind in its claws and then back at D, contemplating it before saying, "Roar..."

It decided against it, thinking it didn't need the helmet since it lived on a ranch and didn't usually battle.

"Really don't want it?" D raised an eyebrow.

Dragonite nodded earnestly, "Roar roar," explaining the reasons to D.

"Alright then," D thought of the large harvest of crops yet to be collected in the field, some of which might have mutated to help Pokémon grow stronger.

He told Dragonite, "But perhaps soon, I'll find other ways to help you grow stronger."

D casually said this to comfort Dragonite, not expecting that by the next morning, there would actually be a mutated crop in the field.

---

---

---

---

# 168: Chapter 165: Glowing Pineapple and Size-Shifting Pumpkin

The whole affair began with Cyclizar.

Early in the morning, the morning dew dripped down the petals onto the grass.

After finishing breakfast, D went out to milk the Gogoat.

Koraidon, as usual, took Dragonite and Cyclizar to the grass to play with a ball that D had bought for them at the toy store yesterday afternoon.

The three Pokemon stood in a triangle, tossing the ball to each other in turn.

When it was Cyclizar's turn, it didn't control its strength well, causing the ball to fly into the pineapple field.

It let out a "Gao" sound, and then quickly chased after the ball into the pineapple field, finally finding the ball beside a pineapple.

Cyclizar stepped forward to pick it up. Just as it was about to turn away, it was suddenly attracted by something glowing not far away.

Cyclizar focused its gaze and saw an incredible sight.

The glowing thing was actually a matured pineapple!

Cyclizar had never seen such a crop before, and in its confusion, it quickly reported the news to D.

Upon receiving the news, D hurried over and immediately saw the glowing pineapple.

At that moment, an exciting thought popped into D's mind:

"Gogoat's Grassy Terrain has once again spawned a mutant crop!"

He quickly went up to inspect, and the information about this strange pineapple immediately surfaced in his mind.

[Glowing Pineapple: A type of special crop, born under the influence of Gogoat's Grass Energy. When used to cook dishes, it can grant them a "flash" effect.]

Flash? Could it be the Flash Move he was familiar with?

After a Pokemon uses it, their body emits a glow that can dispel darkness and illuminate the surrounding area, proving to be very useful in cave adventures and pitch-black environments.

D couldn't wait to try it out.

Just then, he suddenly thought, could there be other mutant crops in the other fields as well?

With that thought, D quickly suppressed his impulse and patiently wandered in the field, starting to inspect each plant.

As his gaze swept over them, a stream of information appeared.

[Normal Corn], [Normal Corn]… [Normal Wheat], [Normal Wheat]… [Normal Hops]… [Normal Pumpkin]…

"Nothing?"

D couldn't help feeling a bit disappointed, but looking at the pineapple in his hand, he began to comfort himself internally: "Having one is already great, one shouldn't be too greedy."

He planned to take this pineapple back to his room and make it into a dish to see the effect.

However, just as he was about to leave the farmland, his peripheral vision suddenly caught a bizarre sight.

Amid a pile of shiny yellow, golden pumpkins in the pumpkin field, there was an especially peculiar pumpkin.

Under D's gaze, that pumpkin began to change at a visible pace!

It first slowly shrank to the size of an Oran Berry, then started to expand, ultimately growing as large as a tire.

D widened his eyes, watching this scene in considerable amazement.

The next second, the information about that magical pumpkin appeared in his mind.

[Size-Shifting Pumpkin: A type of special crop, born under the influence of Gogoat's Grass Energy. When used to cook dishes, it can grant them a "size-shifting" effect.]

"Size-shifting?"

What kind of bizarre effect is this?

Holding a feeling of wonder, D handed the glowing pineapple to Cyclizar beside him, then stepped forward to pick the pumpkin.

Once detached from the vine, the strange pumpkin still didn't stop its size changes.

Even more interestingly, its weight also altered, becoming lighter and heavier with each fluctuation.

Holding it in his arms, D felt the unique sensation more strongly.

He took the pineapple and pumpkin with Cyclizar to the kitchen. D watched the pumpkin lying on the chopping board, continually changing size.

Once it reached its largest form, D picked up a knife, and with a muffled "thud," he plunged half the knife into the pumpkin.

Only then did this special pumpkin finally stop changing, though its size didn't remain at its largest, instead returning to a normal appearance.

"What a pity, I thought I could make several more dishes," D regretted mildly for a moment.

Next, it was time to try and make dishes with these two vegetables and fruits!

According to the descriptions, it seems that no matter what dishes he made with these two ingredients, the effects would always be fixed as flash and size-shifting.

Therefore, he first needed to consider how to make the most out of them, to create the most dishes with the least ingredients, without wasting a single bit.

"The pineapple can be used to make sweet and sour pineapple pork, pineapple sticky rice, it can also be made into pineapple cake, or juiced…"

But pineapple cake seems a bit wasteful. After a brief moment of contemplation, D made a decision in his mind.

—Cut the pineapple in half, use most of the flesh to make sweet and sour pineapple pork and juice, and use the remaining pineapple shells to make pineapple sticky rice.

As for the pumpkin…

Various pumpkin dishes flashed through D's mind, finally settling on pumpkin pancakes.

This was the dish he could think of that would make the largest quantity.

Once decided, D immediately got busy.

He first cut the pineapple in half, then used a spoon and a fruit knife to scoop out all the flesh, and finally put the soaked sticky rice and diced fruit into the two halves of the pineapple, setting them in the rice cooker to steam.

While steaming the sticky rice, D took out tenderloin, eggs, and other ingredients from the refrigerator.

He cut the tenderloin into cubes, stirred them evenly in egg liquid, then coated them with starch, and fried them in oil until golden brown.

Next, D made a sauce using tomato juice, sugar, vinegar, and other seasonings.

In the final step, he tossed green pepper, red pepper, pineapple chunks, and the fried tenderloin into the pan for a stir-fry.

Seeing it was about done, D turned off the heat and poured the colorful, fragrant, and tasty sweet and sour pineapple pork onto a plate.

As he looked down, the information about this dish surfaced.

[Sweet and Sour Pineapple Pork (B+): A dish made with freshly picked pineapple and pork tenderloin. Sweet and sour, nutritious and delicious.

Cooking Effect: Flash. After being fed to a Pokemon, the Pokemon has a chance to learn the Flash Move, while its entire body shines with light, lasting for 30 minutes.

Appraisal: A very good dish, a favorite of those who love sweet and sour flavor.]

Looking at the description, D couldn't help but say: "Just as I thought."

Flash…

It's quite a nice Move, capable of playing a remarkable role when exploring pitch-black caves.

---

---

---

---

# 169: Chapter 166: Big and Small

Soon, the pineapple sticky rice was also ready.

D took it out of the rice cooker, and as he looked down, he noticed its effect was identical to that of the pineapple sweet and sour pork.

[Pineapple Sticky Rice (B+): Made with freshly picked pineapples and sticky rice.

Cooking Effect: Glow, when fed to a Pokemon, the Pokemon has a chance to learn the Glow move, and their entire body will light up, lasting for 30 minutes.

Evaluation: The fragrance of the pineapple is well integrated into the sticky rice, sweet and soft, it's a dish full of tropical flavor.]

"Not bad."

D planned to distribute it to a few Pokemon later.

As for what's next, it's time for the pumpkin.

Making pumpkin pancakes is much simpler than making pineapple sweet and sour pork.

While recalling the recipe for pumpkin pancakes in his mind, D peeled, seeded, and washed the large pumpkin, sliced it, and steamed it until cooked.

During the wait, D kept the pumpkin seeds separate, planning to roast them later so as not to waste them.

Soon, the pumpkin was steamed, and D found a mixing bowl to mash the soft pumpkin into a puree with a rolling pin.

Next, he added some sticky rice flour and white sugar to make dough. Once fully blended, he could proceed to the next step.

— Take some pumpkin rice dough, roll it into balls, then flatten them; this entire pumpkin made a total of thirty-two pumpkin pancakes.

Finally, fry these pumpkin pancakes in oil until golden, then remove and let them cool on a plate.

Looking at the golden-crispy pumpkin pancakes lying on the white porcelain plate, information about this dessert appeared in D's mind.

[Pumpkin Pancake (A): Made with fresh pumpkin, sticky rice flour, and white sugar.

Cooking Effect: Size Adjustment, after consumption, you can change your body size at will, not exceeding the limit, with only three transformation chances, after which you'll return to your original size.

Evaluation: Golden and crispy on the outside, soft and tender inside, with the fragrance of pumpkin, it's a very soft and sticky snack.]

Hmm? Size Adjustment?

Seeing this effect, D couldn't help but imagine the scenes of Koraidon, Cyclizar, Gogoat, and other Pokemon turning into giant monsters dozens of meters tall after eating this pumpkin pancake.

The next second, D opened his eyes wide.

What does this mean?!

It means he can now artificially create Dynamax Pokemon!

Even more astonishing, the description doesn't specify it's only effective for Pokemon, does this mean humans can use it too?

D's heart began to race.

With curiosity, he picked up a still-warm pumpkin pancake, blew on it a bit, and ate it in a couple of bites.

"Enlarge."

D silently commanded in his heart.

Immediately, his body began to grow larger, nearly touching the ceiling. D quickly shouted to stop.

He awkwardly glanced down at his current self, estimating in his mind that he was now about two and a half meters tall.

Fortunately, there were no Pokemon in the room, otherwise, they might have been startled.

"It's not just the height that increased, it seems that all parts scaled proportionally with the height."

While D was experiencing his current form, he pondered the pumpkin pancake's effect.

Looking down at his body, D's old face blushed, and then he silently thought: "Shrink."

Quickly, he returned to his normal size.

D breathed a sigh of relief, moved his neck, and internally evaluated: Ridiculous effect!

However…

D suddenly thought of that gigantic Dragonite beneath Dragonite Island.

If fed these pumpkin pancakes, could it become a normal-sized Dragonite?

"We could try it out in the afternoon."

But for now, it's time for lunch.

D went outside to call the Pokemon and brought the pineapple sweet and sour pork, pineapple sticky rice, and pumpkin pancakes to the table.

He also made a few sandwiches.

As lunch was served, Koraidon and the other Pokemon started to dig in heartily.

However, as the first piece of meat was eaten, all the Pokemon began to glow with a dazzling white light.

"Vee?!"

Glaceon was shocked, Cyclizar looked confused, Dragonite showed a happy expression, and Koraidon remained indifferent.

Seeing their reactions, D explained with a smile:

"Eating the meat and pineapple here will make your bodies glow, for about half an hour."

Dragonite was surprised: "Rawr?"

D nodded: "That's right, it's a dish made from those glowing pineapples."

"Rawr?"

Dragonite suddenly remembered there seemed to be a pumpkin that could enlarge and shrink.

It glanced at the row of golden, crispy pancakes on the white porcelain plate in front of it.

What about this? What magical effect does it have?

"Uh…" Recalling what had just happened, D suddenly didn't know whether to explain.

He hesitated for a moment, then said: "I'll tell you after you finish eating."

At least not during a meal, otherwise if any Pokemon suddenly wants to grow large and tears off the roof, that would be troublesome.

Upon hearing this, led by Dragonite, the Pokemon quickly sped up their eating.

Seeing them finish, D led them to a reasonably spacious field in the ranch.

Looking at the expectant Pokemon in front of him, he cleared his throat and said: "Now, in your mind, think: [I want to grow bigger]!"

"Rawr?"

Initially, Dragonite was confused, but soon it discovered its body was starting to grow larger!

Dragonite's face showed a shocked expression.

D shouted: "You can control it! As long as you think to stop, the change will automatically stop!"

Indeed, as he said, Dragonite's form quickly ceased changing and stabilized at roughly six meters, as tall as a two-story building.

Seeing its posture, Alcremie followed suit, marveling at its transforming body.

Cyclizar and Pawmo followed closely, while Glaceon hesitated a bit.

Yet Koraidon was the most surprising.

Because it managed to shift to a size similar to Pikachu on its own!

D raised his eyebrows in surprise.

Koraidon seemed quite pleased: "Aga Sss!"

It ran up to D, looking up at him.

"What do you want?" D asked.

Koraidon stretched out its arms as if asking for a hug.

D: "..."

He reached out and picked Koraidon up, letting it stand on his shoulder like Pikachu.

Then, he looked up at the other enlarged Pokemon and said:

"Be careful not to get too big and wreck the ranch, and remember, you have three transformation chances, you can grow or shrink as you wish, once the last one is used up, you'll revert to your original form."

---

---

---

---

# 170: Chapter 167: Island Vacation

Two o'clock in the afternoon, on the way to Dragonite Island.

D rode atop Dragonite, glancing sideways at Koraidon perched on his shoulder, momentarily speechless.

The sea breeze brushed against his face.

Soon, little heads poked out from the backpack D carried strapped to his chest, clustering together to gaze at the scene ahead.

"...Are you really not planning to return to your usual forms?" D looked down at the Alcremie, Glaceon, Cyclizar, and Pawmo in front of him and asked.

Earlier at noon, after witnessing Koraidon's actions, all the Pokémon became tiny and hung onto him.

Especially upon hearing he was heading to Dragonite Island, their faces all showed expressions of longing.

There was no other choice. D could only ask Gogoat to watch over the ranch, and then brought them along.

All the Pokémon gazed emotionally at the vast blue sea below, their eyes sparkling brightly.

This was the first time they'd seen the ocean since growing so big.

Pawmo murmured softly: "Bamora…" How beautiful...

Glaceon's eyes reflected the shimmering sea surface, and it had never imagined the world could be so vast; aside from icy mountains and snowy fields, such stunning scenery existed beyond.

Living in a small town since childhood and having come to the ranch at just a few months old, Cyclizar shared the same sentiment.

So beautiful! Even more than the moon as seen by Dragonite! Cyclizar thought.

"Mama…" A glistening tear flashed in Alcremie's eyes; it suddenly felt very blessed.

D originally planned to say something, but before he could open his mouth, he saw the Pokémon in front of him displaying this behavior.

Oh well, he wouldn't say anything; today, he would just let them have fun for the whole afternoon!

Dragonite's speed was swift, and before long, they reached the familiar Dragonite Island.

It landed in the center of the island, catching the sharp eyes of several Dragonair playing in the lake.

Their eyes brightened instantly, and they all flew out of the water to greet him.

Dragonite initially thought this group of Dragonair were there to welcome it, since during its time living on the island, it helped take care of them, doing things like finding berries for them.

Seeing them like this now, Dragonite felt quite gratified.

It spread its arms wide, wanting to embrace these Dragonair and Dratini.

However, in the next moment, these Dratini and Dragonair bypassed it and ran over to D standing behind it.

Dragonite widened its eyes, fuming, and pointed at them loudly: "Roar!" (No manners!)

Then, it huffed angrily and came over, hugging D right in front of the Dragonair and Dratini, indicating that this was its good partner, its good trainer.

The Dragonair and Dratini blinked, looking at the childish Dragonite before them, with one even making a funny face at it.

Dragonite:!!!

D: "..."

He helplessly patted Dragonite's claw: "Alright, stop fussing, don't forget we're here for serious business today."

Only then did Dragonite remember that matter, obediently letting go of D.

D opened his backpack, placing Alcremie, Pawmo, Glaceon, and Cyclizar on the ground, and then let them play with the Dragonair and Dratini of the island.

As for himself, he took Dragonite and Koraidon to the cliff edge.

Gazing at the waves crashing against the cliffs below, D turned and asked: "Do you know how to dive?"

Koraidon appeared a bit troubled: "Agassi…"

It could swim, but couldn't dive into the sea for long periods.

"I knew it," D smiled: "If that's the case, you stay on the shore and wait for us, we'll be back soon."

Koraidon didn't plead or act cute to make D take it into the water.

It nodded, then obediently jumped down from D, shifting back to normal size and standing at the cliff's edge to wait.

D bid farewell, then pulled out the pre-purchased diving gear from his space backpack, donned it, and followed Dragonite into the blue sea.

Guided by Dragonite, they quickly swam toward the ocean floor.

Soon, they saw the huge sleeping figure directly beneath the island.

D gently patted Dragonite's back.

Dragonite got the message, guiding D to the giant Dragonite's presence, then let out a melodious cry, starting to call the giant Dragonite.

Once, twice, three times…

Just as D thought the giant Dragonite had fallen into a deep sleep, the massive Dragonite suddenly opened its eyes, revealing those ancient golden dragon eyes, and a terrible aura immediately spread out from the seafloor.

D and Dragonite were startled.

But quickly, the giant Dragonite retracted its aura, staring at the human and Dragonite before it, its sleepy eyes revealing doubt.

"Roar?" A dull growl emerged from its throat.

Unable to speak underwater, D asked Dragonite to convey his message.

After listening, the giant Dragonite glanced over.

D gestured upward to indicate talking above.

The giant Dragonite understood.

It stood up and swam toward the sea surface, stirring the water with its massive body.

D and Dragonite were startled by its movement; as they were about to be separated by the hidden torrent beneath the sea, a pair of dragon claws grabbed them, lifting them out of the sea to the surface.

D removed his oxygen mask, and at this moment, Koraidon, holding the backpack, flew over as it saw them surface.

D didn't say much, directly taking a pumpkin pie from the backpack, and said to the giant Dragonite: "Open your mouth?"

The giant Dragonite immediately opened its mouth wide.

Gazing at the abyss-like giant maw, D silently swallowed his saliva, then threw the pumpkin pie inside with all his might.

He thought the pumpkin pie was so small, probably having no effect on the giant Dragonite, not knowing if it would work.

"Can you try thinking about becoming smaller now?" D tentatively asked.

The giant Dragonite closed its mouth and began thinking.

Soon, a miraculous scene unfolded.

Its massive body, like a mountain, began shrinking at a visibly rapid pace, eventually becoming approximately three meters tall.

Fortunately, D had prepared beforehand; once the giant Dragonite transformed, he immediately mounted Dragonite, preventing any fall.

He looked at the "Dragonite" before him in surprise and asked: "How do you feel?"

"Roar!" The giant Dragonite was also filled with curiosity about its current appearance.

It looked down at its body, then turned to look at the familiar yet strange surroundings.

At this time, the Dragonites of Dragon Island noticed the scene and astonished, gathered around after seeing the giant Dragonite's appearance.

---

---

---

---

# 171: Chapter 168: Became Friends with the Giant Dragonite (Fifth Update)

Dragon Island, central lake.

A group of Dragonite surrounded the larger Dragonite in the center, looking at it with curious and innocent eyes.

"Awoo?" (How did you transform like this?)

"Awoo awoo?" (Amazing! Does this mean you can play with us now?)

"Awoo!" (Come with me to pick Berries! And catch fish in the sea!)

"Awoo～"

D sat on the grass, leaning against Koraidon, munching on sunflower seeds while listening to the group of Dragonite chatting.

The ambient noise here was overwhelming, and after quite some time, he only caught a few phrases that he couldn't understand.

The large Dragonite seemed to quite enjoy the goodwill shown by the group of Dragonite.

After becoming smaller, it didn't look as frightening as before, and its aura was much less imposing, becoming rather ordinary.

But D could guarantee that its strength hadn't diminished at all, and if provoked, the large Dragonite would probably unleash its fury without hesitation.

Not sure what the group of Dragonite said to the large Dragonite, D soon saw the group flying away with it, not knowing where they went.

D didn't bother with it, assuming they wouldn't go off to destroy the world, right?

Then, he turned his gaze to the normal-sized Alcremie and the other Pokemon nearby.

They were playing happily with a group of Dragonair and Dratini.

But...where did Pawmo go?

D stood up and looked around, but couldn't find any trace of Pawmo.

At that moment, a loud thunderclap suddenly came from the other side of the island.

D and the nearby Dragonite froze, then flew into the air to check the situation.

D also mounted Koraidon, following the Dragonite to the source of the sound.

From a distance, he caught sight of Pawmo on the sea surface.

Pawmo was charging up.

The once-clear sky quickly became covered with storm clouds, and terrifying electric energy gathered within them, ultimately forming a blue bolt of lightning that struck Pawmo.

With an enthusiastic cry of "Ba-moh!", a blue electric spark surrounded Pawmo's fur, and its eyes were completely filled with light.

A powerful surge of electricity erupted from it, concentrating downwards towards the sea.

"Kaboom!"

A thunderous noise followed, raising massive waves on the sea.

When everything calmed down, Pawmo grinned, revealing two sharp little teeth.

Nearby, several Dratini looked at the lightning-wielding Pawmo with admiration.

If they had hands, D had no doubt they'd be clapping fervently.

"You scared me..."

D breathed a sigh of relief; he thought Pawmo had gotten into a fight with a wild Dragonite.

At this moment, Pawmo noticed him too.

"Ba-moh!" it called happily, flying over.

Seeing this, D thought Pawmo was a bit too reliant on flying, since gaining the ability to fly, he rarely saw Pawmo run on the ground.

"This won't do!" D said.

"Ba-moh?" Pawmo paused, a bit puzzled.

D asked, "Do you want to evolve into Pawmot?"

Pawmo nodded repeatedly, its eyes full of determination: "Ba-moh!"

D explained to it, "Continuing like this, you won't be able to evolve into Pawmot."

While Pawmo was in a daze, he continued, "As far as I know, for Pawmo to evolve into Pawmot, it needs to run hard on the ground, and after covering a certain distance, it can evolve into Pawmot while running."

Pawmo immediately understood D's meaning: "Ba-moh..."

D chuckled: "So, starting today, you need to return to basics, not rely too much on flying, and try to start running."

"Ba-moh!"

"That's the way!"

D patted its head: "Now go play with the Dratini, and after we get home, we'll run together."

He needed some exercise as well.

Having been eating lavishly every day and with Pokemon helping with farm work, there wasn't much he really needed to do, which led to significant weight gain.

D pointed to the lake behind him: "I'll be over there, so come find me when you're done playing."

"Ba-moh!" Pawmo nodded, indicating it understood.

D rode Koraidon back to the central lake.

As soon as he landed, the group of Dragonite that had flown off earlier returned.

Each Dragonite was carrying a pile of Berries in their arms, with a few holding some sea bass.

Seeing this, D raised an eyebrow slightly.

Then, he saw the large Dragonite carrying a pile of Berries, walking towards him, and placing the Berries before him.

"Roar!"

The large Dragonite sounded quite pleased.

D looked at it for a while, then took out some sunflower seeds and resumed munching.

The large Dragonite's voice automatically translated.

"Roar...roar!" (My...friend...thank you...food...gift...for you!)

Upon hearing this, D was stunned for a moment.

So, does this mean the large Dragonite considered him, a human, a friend?

He hesitantly asked, "Friend?"

Then, he heard the large Dragonite speak haltingly: "Roar, roar...roar!" (You...Mew, Bear...Help me...find companions...I thank you...you are friend, mine!)

It seemed to be quite new to this unfamiliar world and still not quite adapted, so its ancient Dragonite speech was unclear.

But despite this, D understood the large Dragonite's meaning.

Honestly, he felt quite happy about it.

"Do you plan to live here from now on?" D asked again.

The large Dragonite nodded: "Roar!"

"That's great!"

D sincerely felt happy for the large Dragonite.

The large Dragonite ran off.

Watching its back, D suddenly thought of Bill, who was investigating the large Dragonite.

He wondered how Bill would feel upon learning about the large Dragonite's current situation.

Given Bill's personality, if he learned that the large Dragonite found its desired companions, he would likely be happy for it.

D picked up the Berries given by the large Dragonite from the ground, handed one to Koraidon beside him, and took one himself to start munching.

"How wonderful!" he said with emotion.

"Ah gah sss?" Koraidon turned to look at him.

D said to it, "I never thought there would be a day where I would personally participate in this world, helping Pokemon like some... like some people."

Koraidon didn't quite understand: "Ah gah sss."

D chuckled: "You can sort of understand it as, I'm very happy about helping Pokemon and receiving appreciation from them."

If his journey to this world had any meaning, this might be why he exists here.

——To meet more Pokemon, help them, and heal himself.

---

---

---

---

# 172: Chapter 169: Dragonite's Earth-Shaking Form

This family gathering concluded perfectly, but before returning to the ranch, there was one more thing D needed to do.

He wanted to test the upper limit of a Dynamax Pokémon after eating pumpkin pie and see if there were any changes in the moves of the Dynamax Pokémon.

So, he summoned his Dragonite and once again fed it a piece of pumpkin pie.

The Dragonite understood D's intention. After eating the pumpkin pie, it left the island and flew above the ocean.

D and Koraidon stood on the cliff edge of Dragonite Island, watching Dragonite from afar.

Once it flew about over a hundred meters away, it stopped.

Then, Dragonite's body started to grow visibly.

Three meters, five meters, seven meters… fifteen meters… twenty-five meters… thirty meters…

Finally, it transformed into a massive Dragonite rivaling a Gigantic Dragonite!

Its colossal body was like a towering mountain, hitting the sea with a huge splash, creating a tidal wave like a giant sea beast.

"It seems there's a slight difference compared to a Gigantic Dragonite. Is this the upper limit mentioned in the pumpkin pie description?" D murmured to himself, watching the scene.

After thinking for a moment, he shouted loudly, "Try using a move!"

Dragonite responded with a cry, focusing intently. With a thunderous roar, large storm clouds rolled in from all directions.

Their scope was vast, covering the sky and even overcasting the area on Dragonite Island below it.

Then, a lightning bolt struck, followed by torrential rains engulfing the world in a curtain of rain.

As the storm poured down unexpectedly, Dragonites on Dragonite Island flew up to check what was happening.

Upon seeing the gigantic Dragonite in the sea, they couldn't help showing expressions of shock and confusion.

"Hoo?" How has another gigantic Dragonite appeared?

Even Dragonite was quite shocked.

It's shocked differently than the Dragonites on Dragonite Island because it was surprised by its own astonishing move!

Looking at the cloudy sky, Dragonite couldn't help but recall D's words from before—let the whole world be enveloped in your rain.

Now it had done it!

Dragonite was extremely excited, and D felt an equal thrill in his heart.

"It seems that once Dynamaxed, the Pokémon's moves are also enhanced. I wonder if there's a usage limit; I'll have to let Dragonite try again."

After a brief consideration, he rode Koraidon to fly over to Dragonite's side, prompting Dragonite to stop and test other moves.

Dragonite nodded, then called out to Koraidon, signaling it to take D a bit further away because Dragonite could sense the next move's power would be very terrifying.

Koraidon didn't say much and turned to fly away.

Once they reached a safe distance, Dragonite started to act.

All around it, purple Dragon Type energy gathered as its massive body swirled in the sea, generating a powerful storm that turned into a massive Twister.

The ocean water was drawn into it and turned into a waterspout, connecting the cloudy sky with the ocean, seemingly possessing a power capable of devastation.

"Hoo…" So scary…

The young Dratinis on the island shivered in fright, staring at the scene in terror.

The other Dragonites also looked grim.

Fortunately, Dragonite didn't maintain this waterspout for too long before dispersing it, its eyes full of excitement, then unleashing a third move—Dragon Rage.

Purple Dragon Type energy spewed from its mouth, turning into a massive flame that roared forward.

With a loud explosion, the sea hadn't even calmed before being stirred into tumult once again.

Just then, after completing three moves, Dragonite's body suddenly popped, reverting to its original form in a puff of smoke.

Seeing this, D was slightly taken aback, then pondered, "It seems the Dynamax Pokémon can only use three enhanced moves and will revert after completing them, similar to Dynamax."

Now it could be called a bootleg Dynamax.

At this moment, Dragonite flew back, and D asked, "Can you transform again?"

"Hoo!"

"Shrink down and show me."

Upon hearing this, Dragonite's body swiftly shrunk, eventually becoming small and cute like a Pikachu.

D scanned this small Dragonite up and down and said, "Show me your moves again!"

Dragonite nodded, then began to demonstrate.

Compared to Dynamax, when it shrinks, the moves used by the Pokémon also become very small, but the number of uses increases accordingly.

To verify his theory, D had Dragonite use Thunderbolt consecutively until after the thirty-eighth use, Dragonite returned to its normal form.

D began to summarize, "Growing larger enhances Pokémon moves, but there's only three attempts. After using them up, it'll revert to its original form."

"And it seems that shrinking can help Pokémon conserve energy, allowing for more uses of moves, though with reduced power. If no moves are used, it seems they can maintain their changed size indefinitely."

This is quite similar to the principle behind the Poke Ball, which also utilizes the tendency of wild Pokémon to shrink and hide when extremely weakened.

"That's enough. Let's leave it at that for now!"

D had grasped the basics and turned around to relay his conclusion to the giant Dragonite.

The giant Dragonite blinked its eyes.

D added, "Unless absolutely necessary, try not to make a move. If you use up your three chances, you might not be able to return to this form again."

Since the pumpkin pie couldn't be preserved for long, once consumed, it was gone. Besides, he couldn't guarantee that he could grow another size-changing pumpkin next time.

"Roar." The giant Dragonite nodded and kept this in mind.

"Alright then, let's call it a day. It's getting dark; we should head home," D smiled as he looked at the big fellow in front of him, instinctively wanting to reach out and pat its head like he did with other Pokémon.

In response to his gesture, the giant Dragonite's gaze shifted to his restless hand.

D stopped his hand in mid-air, awkwardly retracting it and silently cursed his habits for getting him in trouble.

Feigning as if nothing happened, he turned to bid farewell to the giant Dragonite and then found the Pokémon that were playing with the Dratini and Dragonair.

"Time to head home. We'll come back and play next time!"

Cyclizar, Pawmo, Alcremie, and Glaceon reluctantly bade farewell to the Dragonair and Dratini on the island, then turned back to D.

D then recalled each of them back into their Poke Balls one by one.

In an instant, only Koraidon and Dragonite remained outside.

Dragonite was engrossed in answering questions from the other Dragonites on Dragonite Island, basking in their astonished and admiring gazes, wearing a proud expression.

D figured they must be discussing the matter of gigantification.

He chuckled to himself, then turned to find his backpack on a patch of grass. Slinging it over his shoulder, D mounted Koraidon, and shouted to Dragonite, "Let's go, Dragonite, we're heading home!"

"Rawr~"

Dragonite responded enthusiastically, rushing over to give D a hug in front of the other Dragonites, then obediently followed behind Koraidon, taking off into the sky.

"Bye-bye, see you next time!"

D waved cheerfully to the group of wild Dragonites.

As Dragonite Island slowly disappeared from view, D pondered why the Dratini and Dragonair, with whom he was quite familiar, hadn't come to see him off today.

Earlier, he hadn't seen them by the lake. He wondered if they had gone somewhere in the wild to pick berries or had dived into the lake to sleep.

Previously, they had always said goodbye reluctantly. Their sudden absence left D feeling a little disappointed.

However, he didn't dwell on it.

Koraidon's speed was impressive, taking just about an hour to return to the ranch.

They landed in the ranch, and D scanned around, seeing the Gogoat lounging leisurely on the grass, which assured him that nothing major had happened on the ranch.

D felt relieved and released his Pokémon from their Poke Balls, letting them play on their own.

He casually tossed his backpack onto the bed, thinking of grabbing a bottle of water from the fridge, when he suddenly heard Allen's voice.

Seeing this, D grabbed a bottle of juice and, while sipping, walked outside.

He hadn't noticed that the backpack he'd placed on the bed moved slightly.

The zipper was pulled open by a tail, and then heads of various sizes peeked out, curiously surveying the unfamiliar surroundings.

In the ranch.

Allen handed D a letter, looking quite excited:

"Hey D, have you heard? The fishing tournament is about to begin!"

D raised an eyebrow, "Fishing tournament?"

Allen nodded, "That's right, it's on the 20th of this month, at Lake Verity on the east side of the town."

He nudged D with his shoulder, "I heard from Zack that you're really good at fishing! A few months ago, he took a lot of fish from you, so maybe this time you could get a good ranking!"

D: "..." Could he say those fish were actually caught by Koraidon?

Allen continued on his own, "I heard the first place winner can get a collector's edition fishing rod! They say it's a rod that can even catch a Pokémon like Gyarados!"

D thought to himself, so what? He had seen a rod that could catch a Kyogre!

"Is this letter about the fishing tournament?" he asked.

Allen shook his head, "No, Mayor Thomas said he'll notify you about the fishing tournament separately. This is about the fireworks celebration."

D was a bit surprised, "Fireworks celebration? So soon!"

Allen: "Indeed, it's on the beach in Porto Marinada Town at the end of this month, May 31st. I won't go into details; it's all in the letter. It's a very lively festival with countless fireworks blooming in the night sky simultaneously. It's quite a spectacular sight! Be sure to bring your Pokémon to enjoy it thoroughly!"

---

---

---

---

# 173: Chapter 170: Dragonair: Uncle Dragonite, Is This How You Got Caught Too? (4k)

After delivering the letter, Allen left.

Watching his silhouette gradually disappear, D finally opened the letter in his hand.

[To D:

This is the Porto Marinada Town Beach Management Office. We cordially invite you to attend a summer festival at the beach on the 31st of this month at 8 PM.

You will spend a wonderful evening with the townsfolk and Pokemon, watching splendid fireworks bloom in the night sky. As summer reaches its peak, we are eagerly looking forward to your arrival.

——by Porto Marinada Town Beach Management Office]

"A summer festival, huh..."

D murmured softly, picturing the scene in his mind, then smiled, tucked the letter away, and turned to go back inside the house.

Just as Allen mentioned, sure enough, as soon as he returned home, he received a call from Mayor Thomas, telling him about the fishing competition.

"D! The town plans to hold a fishing competition on the 20th of this month. You must come and participate! It will be at Lake Verity on the east side of town, starting at 8 AM. You can come with Allen, as he'll be participating too!"

D: "..."

It seems he needs to brush up on his fishing skills a bit; otherwise, his title as the fishing king might be exposed.

Thinking this, D replied, "Alright, I'll be there."

Mayor Thomas nodded with a chuckle, "Then good luck!"

At this moment, D suddenly remembered something: "By the way, Mayor, do we need to prepare our own fishing rods and bait, or will they be provided?"

Mayor Thomas: "Oh right, I almost forgot to tell you, my memory is getting worse with age. You need to prepare those yourself, fishing rods and bait and all!"

"The competition is about who can catch the most fish within the given time, and the one with the highest number will win the champion title."

Prepare them myself?

D nodded, "Okay, got it."

After hanging up, he calculated the time in his mind.

Today is May 7th, and the 20th is 13 days away. In these days, he needs to harvest the crops in the field and then plant the second batch.

D stood by the desk, pondering while looking at the calendar.

He didn't notice at all that a few tiny figures had already crept up behind him, stealthily levitating on his shoulders.

Suddenly, D felt something was off.

He quickly turned around and saw several Dratini and Dragonair with big white noses had appeared behind him without him noticing.

"Ah!"

The sudden scene startled D, making him bump into the desk behind him.

On the other hand, the Dratini and Dragonair greeted him enthusiastically, seeming rather pleased: "Woo~"

"When did you all sneak over?! No, how did you even get here?!" D was shocked.

Among the three Dratini, there was even a pink Shiny Dratini, and they were flying around D.

The other two Dragonair, looking delighted, fixed their gaze on the Space Backpack placed on D's bed.

Only then did D notice that the zipper of his Space Backpack, which he had zipped up, had somehow been opened by someone and was lying quietly on the bed, with a few bottles of Berry Milk rolling out.

So these Pokemon sneaked into his Space Backpack when he wasn't paying attention and followed him to the ranch?

That's... quite bold! What if he was a bad person?

D couldn't help but hold his forehead. His first reaction wasn't happiness but fright. With a serious expression, he said:

"Sneaking over here will make everyone on the island worried!"

Seeing him upset, the three Dratini and two Dragonair immediately lowered their heads, showing a pitiful appearance.

"Woo..."

D: "..."

His heart softened, but he still asked, "Does anyone else know you came along?"

Dragonair shook its head. To avoid being scolded by Dragonite, they sneaked into the Space Backpack when all the Dragonite on the island were distracted.

"Forget it." D sighed helplessly, then as he walked outside, he said, "Come with me. I need to figure out a way to inform the Dragonite on the island about your arrival."

The five Pokemon obediently followed him out to the grass.

D's gaze swept around and finally spotted Dragonite near the riverbank, so he called Dragonite over.

When Dragonite saw the Dratini and Dragonair following D, it widened its eyes and rushed over, clearly upset.

Upon learning from D that these fellows had sneaked over, Dragonite started scolding them like an elder.

However, Dragonair wasn't afraid of Dragonite's scolding.

It secretly raised its eyes and softly said, "Woo..." (You used to run outside every day too, then one day you just never came back, and everyone thought you had been caught by bad people...)

Dragonite was momentarily taken aback. It thought carefully and realized its behavior wasn't exactly exemplary either.

Dragonair's words left Dragonite at a loss for what to say, and it could only scratch its head sheepishly, seeking help from D.

---

D thought for a moment and said, "The most important thing now is to tell the Dragonites on the island about their arrival."

He then looked at the Dragonair and Dratini: "As you can see, this is where we live. The environment is not as good as the island you lived on."

"There are no Berry trees here, no ocean, and there aren't many places with water."

The Berry trees have not yet grown and at least won't bear fruit until autumn, and the young Dratini can't leave the water for too long.

Although there are lakes on the ranch, compared to the lakes on Dragonite Island, the lakes here are still too small.

Hearing this, the Dragonair and Dratini turned their heads and curiously observed the surrounding environment.

The ranch was bare, with only a few scattered large trees, green lawns on the ground, a few unknown buildings not far away, and on the other side, fields of various flowers and bountiful crops.

In addition to the Glaceon, Alcremie, Koraidon, Pawmo, and Cyclizar they had already seen, many other kinds of Pokémon also lived here.

Some were curiously observing, while others were leisurely basking in the sun, exuding a sense of ease and comfort all over.

After they finished looking around, D continued to ask:

"Do you want to go back, or do you want to stay here? If you want to go back, I'll have Dragonite take you back. If you want to stay and play for a few days, I'll send you back after a while."

To be honest, he hasn't had the opportunity to meet breeders and learn how to make nutritious Pokeblocks from them, so for now, he can't raise too many Pokémon.

Dratini and Dragonair are fine, but if they evolve into Dragonite while staying here, it might really eat him out of house and home...

Stay a few more days?

Hearing this, the Dratini and Dragonair decisively chose the latter.

D knew they would make this choice.

He helplessly nodded at Dragonite.

Dragonite, as if resigned to fate, gave a glare to the Dratini and Dragonair, then flew into the sky and headed towards Dragonite Island.

D then brought the Dratini and Dragonair to the circular lake in the ranch.

When he first came to this world, he had caught two Magikarp and put them here, but later found feeding them troublesome, so he released them back into the river.

He thought this lake would just be abandoned, but he didn't expect it to come in handy at this time.

The water in the lake was fairly clear, with a few fallen leaves and petals floating on top, probably blown over by the wind.

"You guys can settle here for now!"

The Dratini and Dragonair splashed into the lake, leaving only their little heads peeking out onto the shore.

Seeing them like this, D felt like he was entertaining the neighbor's kids visiting.

He shook his head, dismissing the strange thought, intending to fetch some food to feed them, when he suddenly remembered something and reminded them:

"Oh right, you can roam freely within the ranch, but you must remember, never go outside the ranch, understand?"

"Huh?" Dratini looked puzzled.

D poked its big nose, deliberately scaring it:

"That's because outside, there are many bad people. If they see you, they will capture you and lock you in a tiny Poké Ball, and you won't be able to return to Dragonite Island."

Hearing this, the Dratini shivered in fear, pulling their heads down until only half was exposed.

Dragonair, however, was curious and asked, "Huh?"

D couldn't understand, so he went back into the house, cracking open a handful of sunflower seeds and stuffing them into his mouth.

"What did you say?"

Dragonair repeated: "Huh?" (Was Uncle Dragonite caught by you like that?)

D almost choked: "I made an agreement with your Uncle Dragonite. It became my Pokémon, and I helped it become stronger. Would you say it got stronger?"

Dragonair thought about it. Their Uncle Dragonite was initially a dumb Dragonite who couldn't learn any moves, but one day it left the island and didn't come back.

When it returned, its strength had vastly changed. It had become one of the most powerful Dragonite on Dragonite Island.

Thinking of this, the Dragonairs smiled happily, "Huh~" (If that person is like you, we wouldn't mind being captured just like Uncle Dragonite~)

D was taken aback for a moment, then chuckled, "Not everyone is like me, and in some people's eyes, you're as precious as diamonds."

"Although some people might take good care of you, there are also those who would capture you for profit, sell you to others for breeding or collection."

Dragonair didn't quite understand.

D gave them an example, "Take me and your Uncle Dragonite, for instance. If I were a bad person, I would sell it to someone else for ten thousand Berries."

The Dragonair let out a gasp, a lot of Berries!

But soon, a Dratini couldn't help but ask, "Huh?" (Then why don't you capture us?)

D winked at it, "Though I'd love to, doing so would make your Uncle Dragonite jealous and angry, and I don't want to upset it."

---

"So, I can take care of you, feed you delicious food, or help you become stronger and learn moves, but I can't keep you in a Poke Ball."

Dratini listened, half-understanding.

The other Dragonair's eyes lit up: "Woo?" (Do you mean that even if we're not in a Poke Ball, we can still live with Uncle Dragonite like you?)

D smiled, a bit surprised: "Smart, but you guessed that yourself. I never said that."

"Woo~"

The Translation Nuts were all eaten, and D couldn't understand Dratini and Dragonair's words anymore.

He stood up and glanced at the distant sunset, taking a deep breath: "Alright, you guys play here for now. I need to prepare dinner for your Uncle Dragonite."

D didn't start cooking right away but instead found Pawmo, who could fly, and gave it a new task.

"Pawmo, for a while, help me watch over those Dragonair and Dratini. Make sure they don't leave the ranch's boundaries."

"Bamo!" Pawmo patted its chest in agreement.

Only then did D feel relieved to prepare dinner.

Tonight's dinner was once again Secret Sandwiches and grilled meat, paired with a few Berries and milk, with dessert being aromatic Poffins and cream smoothies.

The preparation method for these foods was simple and quick. After finishing them, Dragonite hadn't returned yet. Using this time, D stir-fried a whole pot of Translation Nuts.

Looking at the steaming, caramel-scented nuts, D murmured: "Seems like I need to buy an automatic shell opener."

Cracking nuts one by one during translation is too troublesome. It would be much easier if shelled nuts were available, allowing for a big handful of nut kernels to be shoved into the mouth.

Or if there were Psychic Type Pokemon, that would work too. They could use Psychic to crack open a bunch of nut shells at once.

Thinking of this, D's mind flashed to the Indeedee siblings in that manor.

The manor owner said it would take another two months to send them over. Now nearly a month had passed, so it would still take some time.

But that's just right.

D checked his finances, and with all the Nacli evolving into Naclstack, the yield of Rock Salt from the ranch increased significantly.

He initially thought it would take until the month's end to save enough, but he was unexpectedly nearing his goal.

Once the ripe crops in the field are sold mid-month, he would have enough to reach two million.

Then he could ask Keri to come over and expand the house, which would take about one to two months.

By the time the house is built, the Indeedee siblings would arrive just in time.

At 7 PM, Dragonite finally returned to the ranch from Dragon Island.

It landed in the ranch, and D approached, asking: "What did the Dragonites on the island say?"

Dragonite: "Awoo!"

D blinked. From its expression, the Dragonites on the island seemed to agree to let the Dratini and Dragonair play here for a while.

He smiled and said: "Come in for dinner!"

Dragonite hurriedly ran inside and grabbed a sandwich to eat.

There was actually another thing it hadn't mentioned.

When it went to the island, some mischievous Dragonites, upon hearing about the Dratini and Dragonair, also wanted to come over and play, even stealthily following it.

Those were the same two persistent Dragonites that wanted to come back with D initially.

Luckily, it noticed in time and managed to chase them away.

---

---

---

---

# 174: Chapter 171: Too Happy to Leave

Dragonair and Dratini settled down in the ranch, including that Shiny Dratini.

D initially thought their temperament would be similar to Dragonite, being gentle and naive.

However, while they were indeed naive, they were also quite mischievous, except for the Shiny Dratini.

In just a few days, the Pokemon in the ranch were all messed with by them.

They would stick to the Gogoat to eat their Grassy Seed, cling to Pawmo and have them chase each other while flying, and even crawl into the Diglett Hole only to get stuck at the entrance...

They even sneaked into the water while he was fishing and hung Magikarp on his fishing hook.

He thought he had caught something big, only to reel it in and find a Magikarp using Splash.

Not only that, but they also got creative, hanging glass bottles, old boots they somehow found, and even themselves onto the hook.

One evening, Zack came to collect goods and happened to see him fishing up a Dratini.

Zack was astonished and exclaimed that his fishing skills had reached an extraordinary level, being able to catch even a Dratini, and said he would definitely do well in the upcoming fishing competition.

D explained the situation to Zack, who became even more astonished, exclaiming he had never seen a rancher who could raise Dratini, such rare Pokemon, in droves at home.

D was speechless.

To prepare for the upcoming fishing tournament, D had been diligently practicing his fishing techniques these days.

Every day after finishing his work on the ranch, he would take his fishing rod and bait to the water to start fishing.

Besides watching TV, there wasn't much else to do for leisure on the ranch.

In such a situation, fishing became a great way to pass the time, so he would often sit there all day long.

Today was another new day.

D carried his fishing rod, ready to go fishing by the river.

Seeing the eager Dratini and Dragonair wanting to follow, D felt a headache and said through gritted teeth:

"No way, if any of you secretly hang a Magikarp, a glass bottle, or an old boot on my hook again today, I'll send you back to the island!"

The Dratini and Dragonair indeed stopped their mischief plans to sneak into the water to help with the fishing.

D pinched Dratini's white big nose, pointing at the pink Dratini swimming leisurely in the lake, saying to it:

"Why can't you be like that one, see how obedient and well-behaved it is!"

Dratini glanced at the Shiny Dratini, then at D, and secretly made a funny face.

D immediately shouted for someone: "Dragonite, come over and keep an eye on your nephews and nieces!"

Dragonite looking innocent, thinking it couldn't manage these mischievous ones either, but still walked over honestly: "Roar..."

Leaving the Dratini and Dragonair in Dragonite's care, D confidently carried his fishing rod to the lakeside.

Koraidon thoughtfully set up a small folding stool, while Cyclizar held a transparent bottle filled with red liquid in its claws.

D sat on the small stool, took the bottle, opened the cap, and immediately a rich, fragrant aroma of red tea hit him.

Indeed, it was the specialty red tea from Kitakami.

D took a sip; he had been feeling a bit warm inside from eating too many sunflower seeds these days, and this red tea had the effect of relieving heat and thirst. Plus, it tasted quite good, so he would drink several cups every day.

"Alright, don't just stand here, you might scare my fish away, go play!"

After giving his instructions, D waved to Koraidon and Cyclizar.

The two Pokemon happily ran off, basking leisurely on the lawn not far away.

D glanced back, watching Dragonair and Dratini starting to pester Dragonite, thinking that though they were mischievous, their presence did bring more liveliness to the ranch.

And these Dragonair and Dratini had a vibe of being so happy where they were that they didn't think about going back.

What was Dragonite Island? They probably already put it out of their minds.

D smiled and shook his head, then began to seriously fish.

On the grassy land on the other side of the river, a group of Psyduck were selling cuteness in the shade of the trees.

Seeing humans looking at them from across the river, the Psyduck suddenly became flustered and uneasy.

D was puzzled, was he really that scary?

Meanwhile, on the other side of the ranch, Dragonite, who was looking after the little Dratini and Dragonair, quietly sighed in relief.

Dragonite's eyes fell on D who was fishing by the river.

Truthfully, it initially feared that the arrival of these Dratini and Dragonair would steal D's affection, and he would put them into Poke Balls and raise them into Dragonite.

It wasn't very smart and couldn't charm and act cute like these mischievous little ones, so it was worried about being replaced during this time.

Every time it saw D interacting with the Dratini and Dragonair, its mind would imagine a group of new Dragonite surrounding D while it was abandoned, drawing circles alone in a corner.

Only new dragons would make D laugh, ignoring the old one's tears.

Luckily, D didn't do that, and he continued to care for every Pokemon on the ranch as before.

This made Dragonite feel both sweet and bothered.

The sweetness was that in D's heart, it held a higher place than those Dratini and Dragonair.

The bother was D seemed to favor Koraidon and Pawmo more, even sleeping with them in his arms every night.

Sigh...

Dragonite silently sighed.

Today's weather wasn't great, with gloomy skies. D glanced up at the sky while fishing, thinking it might rain soon.

Sure enough, as he caught the third fish, a sudden rainstorm poured down, so fast it caught everyone off guard.

It started raining.

D hurriedly grabbed the iron bucket and ran back, taking shelter with everyone under the eaves.

The Pokemon big and small stood in a row under the eaves, making the space appear quite cramped.

Seeing this, D thought to himself, "It looks like expanding the house is really necessary."

He looked at the fields covered by the rapid rain.

After this batch of crops is harvested, he should have enough funds to expand the house.

He plans to call Keri over to expand the house; coincidentally, the hops have also matured. Once the house is built, he can officially start his brewing business.

However, there is still one problem at the moment.

Judging by Keri, the old constructor, and Machamp's work speed, building a functional two-story house would take at least a month.

Where would he live during that month?

Stay at the inn in town? It's obviously not realistic.

After thinking about it, D decided to buy a big tent and set it up on the ranch, not only for living but also to experience camping under the stars with his Pokemon.

Yes, perfect!

---

---

---

---

# 175: Chapter 172: Pokemon Tavern Opens!

This rain came quickly and left just as fast.

The grass was wet, and the riverside was muddy everywhere. D didn't want to get his shoes dirty, so he lost interest in fishing for a while.

He decided to return to his room and started processing the three fish he had caught, preparing to cook them: one braised, one steamed, and one fried.

In the afternoon, D went to Porto Marinada Town and bought an outdoor camping tent from the department store.

After bringing the tent back, he set it up on a clean lawn with his Pokemon. Although the location wasn't close to the house, it wasn't too secluded either.

Pawmo looked puzzled by D's tent setup.

"Bamo?"

D explained, "That's because, in a few days, I plan to renovate our house. We'll have nowhere to live, so we need to prepare in advance."

Then he began to paint a lovely picture of camping:

"On summer nights, the ranch will be breezy, and we'll lie in the tent, watching the stars in the sky and listening to the cicadas chirping in the mountains. When we're sleepy, we just sleep."

"Bamo..."

Hearing this, all the Pokemon were quickly drawn in, and couldn't help but show anticipation.

As D looked at the tent in front of him, his mind began to wander to another thing.

It was the game Animal Crossing.

In this game, players can also set up a tent on their island, and occasionally small animals will come to camp.

If both parties like each other, the player can invite the small animal to live on the island long-term.

So, the question is, can he invite wild Pokemon to camp like this?

D felt it was doable, but not entirely so.

Because wild Pokemon's habitats are fixed, rarely do Pokemon leave their habitats to travel or live elsewhere, let alone camp in an unfamiliar place.

But... the experiences of Teddiursa and Mew showed him that wild Pokemon also have information networks and relationships.

If, if he puts the word out, would it attract some interested Pokemon?

It's well known that there are many Pokemon in this world, and lots of them have unique personalities and interests.

Perhaps some special Pokemon would be attracted to come camping.

The more D thought about it, the more feasible it seemed.

Or going further, he could open a Pokemon Tavern here, using Berry Juice and MooMoo Milk as drinks.

Then, he'd release the message that at D Ranch, there's a human who can help Pokemon solve their problems. Pokemon in need of assistance could come here for help.

For example, he could ask traveling Teddiursa and Mew for help, asking them to spread the word about the establishment of the Pokemon Tavern as they travel the world.

He could offer them healing services or companionship, guiding their hearts, transitioning from a rancher to a priest, pointing lost Pokemon in the right direction.

The main reason he thought of this was that staying alone on the ranch every day was too boring.

If such a place existed, he could eavesdrop on their little gossip while learning more about Pokemon.

If he met a good-natured, eye-catching furry Pokemon, maybe he could even pet them a bit.

This way, even if he didn't leave the house, he could meet more Pokemon.

D was lost in thought, even when Cyclizar called him, he didn't hear.

It took him a while to come back to his senses and realize Cyclizar was calling him. "What is it, Cyclizar?"

Cyclizar looked at him filled with anticipation, "Gao,"

Not just Cyclizar, several Pokemon nearby showed the same expression.

D glanced at them and then at the tent in front of him, instantly understanding.

This group of Pokemon was captivated by the wonderful image he painted earlier, eager to sleep here tonight!

D grinned, "Of course you can, I'll go get a blanket, and we can all camp here tonight!"

The Pokemon cheered happily, shouting "Long live D!"

Without hesitation, D turned around, fetched a blanket, and rummaged through the storeroom for an old camp lantern to place in front of the tent.

Just then, a clear chirp came from the sky.

D was slightly startled, then looked up.

Soon, he saw a Pokemon that resembled a sparrow with dark blue feathers hovering over the ranch.

It was... Swellow?

D wondered, but then he noticed something clutched in Swellow's talons.

D instantly understood, Teddiursa had sent him something again.

As luck would have it, he had just thought of Teddiursa, and now it was delivering a message.

D smiled and thanked Swellow before reaching out to take what Teddiursa had sent.

It was another crayon drawing, but this time the illustration depicted a basket with snacks and two happy faces beside it.

Seeing this, D knew Teddiursa and Mew had received the food he sent last time.

He breathed a sigh of relief and took a look at the item Teddiursa found, which turned out to be a broken Great Ball, still worthy enough to collect.

With its task done, Swellow turned and prepared to fly away.

D quickly called out, then went inside to fetch some food and a camera he had purchased in town.

This camera was like a Polaroid, one that prints photos as soon as you press the shutter, perfect for Teddiursa.

"These snacks are for you, and I'd like you to take this and deliver it to Teddiursa and Mew," D said.

Swellow tilted its head and after a glance, nodded and grabbed the camera's strap with its beak.

D continued, "And one more thing."

As Swellow was about to fly away, it looked back again.

D bashfully shared his earlier idea of a "Pokemon Tavern" with Swellow.

"...If you find them, could you help me tell Teddiursa and Mew about my plan to open a Pokemon Tavern here? Tell them it will provide treatment services and psychological counseling for Pokemon.

Besides, Pokemon that encounter unsolvable problems are welcome here for fun, camping, anything—I'll welcome them all."

Hearing this, Swellow looked at him in surprise.

It took a few firm looks at the human before finally turning away from the ranch.

Finally, the camera was delivered! D breathed a long sigh of relief in his heart.

*

Three days later, in a dense forest of the Kanto Region.

Since receiving D's last food delivery, for fear of missing anything from D, they stayed longer at each new place they visited.

And now, watching Swellow return with something, Mew and Teddiursa both showed expressions of happiness and anticipation.

"Ima!"

"Miu!"

Soon, Swellow landed in front of them.

Three Pokemon drooled over food in the basket, while Mew and Teddiursa shared the treats generously with Swellow.

Once finishing the goodies, Swellow handed them the camera and conveyed D's message to Teddiursa and Mew.

Teddiursa paused while fiddling with the camera, hanging it around its neck. Upon hearing the news, its disbelief showed, "Ima?"

Mew tilted its head and looked at the Swellow in front of it.

Under their gaze, Swellow gently nodded.

Teddiursa and Mew exchanged looks, feeling both touched and joyful.

Just days ago, they met a Pokemon in need of help.

It was an ancient Ninetales.

Back then, Mew sensed its life force slowly fading, appearing very listless, as if on the brink of death.

Teddiursa really wanted to help, but they didn't know how to assist Ninetales. In the end, they sadly watched as Ninetales went into a deep sleep in the wild.

At that moment... if there had been D, maybe he could have helped that Ninetales!

Whether it was guiding or healing, D could have provided support.

After all, D was an incredible human, kind, smart, and capable of creating wondrous food. There was nothing he couldn't do in this world.

Looking at the camera in its hand, Teddiursa was deeply moved.

"Ima..." (D must have known about the troubles we faced and thought of this solution...)

Mew blinked, agreeing with Teddiursa's words.

It was very happy, "Miu!" (In that case, D will also be a member of our Heroic Expedition Team from now on!)

---

---

---

---

# 176: Chapter 173: Stealing Melons on a Moonlit Night

In the evening, D went camping under the starry sky with a group of Pokemon.

During the summer, while the daytime weather is hot, the nights are quite cool. Stars twinkle in the sky, and unknown bug buzzes echo from deep within the grass.

D lay on his back with his arms behind his head, gazing at the sky. A group of Pokemon lounged around him, and a few Dratini curled up on his stomach.

They were dozing off, evidently tired from playing, unlike their lively and mischievous selves during the day.

"Did you know?" D slowly began sharing a tale he had heard, while gazing up at the starry sky. "It's said that there are Pokemon living on those stars. They're called Clefairy, and long ago, they came to our planet in spaceships."

Dragonite was very interested, while the other Pokemon were less enthused but still politely listened.

Cyclizar: "Gaaaw?"

Dragonite: "Awooo?"

"I also don't know if it's true, just something I heard," D said. "But what I can confirm is that besides Clefairy, there are other powerful Pokemon residing in the cosmos."

Like Deoxys, for instance.

Dragonite eagerly inquired, "Awooo?"

D nibbled on some shelled sunflower seeds.

Dragonite repeated, "Awooo?" (Can I see it?)

D thought for a moment, then said, "Maybe someday!"

The era was still nascent, with technology not yet advanced, and humanity's exploration of the cosmos had only just begun.

Like the Mossdeep Cosmic Center in the Hoenn Region, it was the starting point for humanity's exploration of the universe.

Dragonite was getting excited.

Beside them, Pawmo was utterly amazed at human capabilities. For a creature of the wilds, exploring the vast, boundless cosmos was something unimaginable.

"Buzzle..." Its tone was openly astonished.

D chuckled, saying, "Don't underestimate humanity!"

Seeing the Pokemon look back at him, D continued,

"Humans may not be able to use moves like Pokemon, but they have smart minds and nimble hands to build all sorts of incredible machines."

"In fact, many Pokemon have fallen into human hands, like Sea God Lugia, who controls currents and monsoons, and Lei Gong, the embodiment of thunder..."

D thought of Mewtwo: "Not only that, humans can even create incredibly powerful Legendary Pokemon using certain technologies."

"Batte?" Butterfree quickly asked about this.

"That one?" D said. "It's called Mewtwo."

*

As night deepened, the buzzing from the bugs in the grass stopped, leaving only the gentle night breeze and the bright moonlight over the meadow.

Across the river, the gaze of six Psyduck was drawn by the farmland opposite.

The tempting aroma of fruits wafted over, and the Psyduck drooled with desire.

The scent seemed to condense in the air, forming a large white hand, beckoning them.

Finally, one Psyduck couldn't resist.

It leapt into the water with a sway, crossing the river to the other side. The other Psyduck, seeing this, tilted their heads curiously before mimicking and following suit.

They tiptoed into the farmland. One Psyduck found a watermelon, tilting its head at it, then rammed its head into it with force.

Instantly, the ripe watermelon split in two, and the Psyduck gleefully buried its face inside to feast.

The other Psyduck were not to be outdone. Some grabbed corn, others took on pineapples, and some got lost in the vast Sunflora Field, unable to find their way out, letting out pitiful growls.

The penetrating sounds broke the slumber of D and the Pokemon around him.

He jolted awake, quickly rising to investigate.

But when he saw everything before him, he was momentarily speechless.

Moonlit night, melon patch, sneaky fruit thieves...

Should he take out a pitchfork to show respect to these melon-snatching intruders?

Upon being discovered, the munching Psyduck panicked, clasping their heads and running around, trampling many flowers and crops.

D furrowed his brow at the sight and gestured broadly, prompting the Pokemon beside him into action.

In no time, the little intruders who raided the farmland were all caught.

Looking at the six Psyduck standing in a line, hands on heads, appearing slightly dumbfounded, D wondered if they could understand him.

After some thought, he asked expressionlessly, "What are you doing?"

"Quack, quack, quack, quack, quack!"

His question seemed to alarm them, causing the Psyduck to huddle and spin around in circles.

During this, one Psyduck bumped into another.

The bumped Psyduck turned, puzzled, to look at its companion.

They tilted their heads at each other for a moment, then one of them slapped the other.

The other mimicked its action and slapped back, with two crisp sounds ringing out as they tilted their heads again, looking puzzled as if forgetting what had just happened.

D: "..."

Couldn't they be a bunch of dimwits?

But recalling the information on Psyduck, he felt it was understandable.

---

Psyduck's heads occasionally generate psychic powers they can't control, leading to headaches and tears, constantly plagued by pain.

The stronger the headache, the more powerful the force becomes, and when it becomes unbearable, it explodes as telekinesis.

At such times, the headache seems to be temporarily relieved, but perhaps the brain is influenced by psychic powers, and memories of the time are not retained.

This issue has troubled the Pokémon academic world for years.

Because using psychic powers gives them headaches, Psyducks usually try not to do anything, just idling away, coupled with their silly faces, making them appear not very smart, kinda silly.

What should he do with such a Pokémon?

Teaching them isn't right, not teaching them isn't right either.

After pondering for a moment, D still had Koraidon grab them one by one and send them to the other side of the river.

Koraidon obediently did so, while D went to the field, picking the crops damaged by the Psyducks, and directly handing them to Koraidon to give them to eat.

The Psyducks were naturally extremely grateful, sitting on the other side of the river, munching into the watermelons with their heads bowed.

*

The next day early in the morning, all the crops in the field matured.

After milking the Gogoat, D put on a straw hat, braving the blazing sun up in the sky to start harvesting the crops.

The last time he farmed, he expanded a large area of farmland, and now with all the crops matured, he and Koraidon Cyclizar felt somewhat overwhelmed.

Just then, D suddenly noticed the group of Psyducks across the river looking longingly over.

A bold thought popped into his mind.

What if… he hired the Psyducks to help with the harvest?

Just like he previously hired the Digletts.

And he would only need to provide some food to the Psyducks.

There were a lot of crops growing in the field, which a few Psyducks couldn't possibly finish eating.

The more D thought about it, the more he felt it was a good idea, so he called over Koraidon and whispered in its ear.

After listening, there was not much surprise on Koraidon's face.

It nodded, and then went straight to the riverside and began communicating with the group of Psyducks.
