D nodded slightly, watching the young Trainers run off.

Then, D turned his gaze towards the Gogoat.

During this period, to help the Gogoat improve their physique so they could better contain the Grass Energy within, he would take them for morning runs every day.

Pawmo also participated.

After returning from Dragonite Island, in order to return to its essence and evolve into a more powerful Pawmi, it began running on the ground again, rarely flying to get around.

These hardworking Pokemon made D feel very gratified.

Although he wasn't a Trainer and the Pokemon didn't have to battle daily, continuous growth was beneficial for them.

Soon, Dragonite and the others got tired of playing and ran back, gathering around the picnic cloth.

D was about to say something when he suddenly saw people starting to gather on the beach, even the uncle who sold Magikarp went there.

With the sound of a loud explosion, a huge firework bloomed in the night sky.

In an instant, brilliant light illuminated the faces of everyone attending the festival.

Then, as if a switch had been flipped, more fireworks soared into the sky, blooming under the night sky and over the sea.

This scene was stunningly beautiful, as if announcing the peak of summer had arrived.

"Awooo..." Dragonite beside him couldn't help but murmur.

D turned to see its bright black eyes reflecting the splendid fireworks.

Although he couldn't understand its words, D thought it might be saying, "How beautiful!"

Under the starry sky and blooming fireworks, D looked at every Pokemon beside him.

Cyclizar, Koraidon, Dragonite, Pawmo, Alcremie...

He felt he would never forget this moment.

Suddenly, Cyclizar and Koraidon seemed to sense something.

They withdrew their gazes from the fireworks and simultaneously turned to look over here.

Their eyes were equally clear and bright, their expressions relaxed and joyful, and D saw himself in their eyes.

"Aga-snarl~"

"Gao-oo!"

D's lips curved up, and he reached out with both hands to hold the two Pokemon's paws.

One person and two Pokemon continued to watch the fireworks festival.

Under the starlit sky and blooming fireworks, D's lips moved, silently saying in his heart:

"I'm so glad to have met you all!"

---

---

---

---

# 184: Chapter 181: New Home Completed

League Year 198, June 28th.

With the completion of the new house, D's first summer in the Pokemon World officially drew to a close.

That morning, after nearly two months of hard work, Keri formally handed over the new house to D.

She instructed Machamp and Conkeldurr to load tools and surplus building materials onto the truck, and then led D on a tour of the house.

As they pushed open the door, D was greeted by a spacious living room.

At that moment, however, there wasn't a single piece of furniture, making the entire house feel very empty.

Leading the way, Keri introduced:

"This is the living room. As per your request, we've installed a new-style fireplace here. Light it in the winter, and the whole house will warm right up."

Following her gaze, D saw a brand-new fireplace on the opposite wall, with clear glass installed on it.

Keri said, "But you'll need to have enough compressed wooden logs; that's the fuel for the fireplace."

She smiled, "If you need any, my shop sells them, you know!"

D: "..." As expected, Keri never misses a chance to promote her goods.

But he really did need them.

"Alright, I'll check it out later," D said.

"Besides that method, you can also use a Pokemon's powers," Keri continued, "If you have a Slugma, for instance, you can place them inside the fireplace."

"Slugma would treat the cozy fireplace as their nest and reside there. They release heat from their bodies, which can effectively replace the role of wooden logs."

"Slugma, huh?" D thanked Keri and made a mental note of it.

However, Slugma's habitats are mostly in hot volcanic areas, and it's difficult to find them elsewhere.

Besides, Slugma doesn't seem to inhabit the Paldea Region, meaning he'd have to travel to other regions to find them.

But D currently had no plans to travel elsewhere.

As the season was changing, he needed to prepare in advance by buying some autumn crop seeds and planting them.

Additionally, he needed to purchase a bunch of barrels since the hops stored in his utility room could hardly wait to turn into beer.

"There are a total of five rooms on the first floor." After touring the living room, Keri led D to see the rest: "The main bedroom is on the right of the living room, the open kitchen and dining room are on the left, and the bathroom is furthest left."

She opened the door to let D see inside.

"You can buy a bathtub of your choice later," Keri said, "After finishing farm work every day, you can have a comfortable soak to relieve physical and mental fatigue."

D nodded, indicating his satisfaction.

Seeing his response, Keri chuckled and led him upstairs to the second floor: "There are more rooms on the second floor, totaling five: guest rooms for hosting visitors, a study for work and reading, and a children's room. If you have children in the future, they can stay here."

Children… that was too far off. He hadn't even thought about it, D mused.

After all, he was just twenty years old and didn't even know if he could find a marriage partner.

But there was no rush, and he wasn't planning to get married right now; he'd leave it to fate!

If he didn't find a suitable partner, staying single for a lifetime wouldn't be a problem.

They walked through the small hallway upstairs, and Keri opened the doors to the remaining two rooms: "These are two empty rooms. You can decorate them according to your preferences and needs."

D nodded, understanding, and then asked about what he cared most about: "What about the cellar?"

Keri: "The entrance to the cellar is on the first floor."

Back downstairs, D finally found the entrance to the cellar in the southwest corner of the living room.

It was a small wooden door.

Keri opened the door, revealing stairs leading down, the cellar was very dim, almost impossible to see one's hand in front of their face.

"The cellar lights are on the right wall of the stairs." Keri flicked the switch, and with a crisp click, retro wall lamps brightened up.

Soon, a warm orange glow filled the space, illuminating the entire cellar.

"The temperature here is suitable for brewing beer!" Keri said, showing a familiar smile that D recognized.

D: "... I know, I'll buy barrels from you later."

He had saved a lot over the past month and could buy everything he needed in one go.

Keri: "I'll give you a discount."

Having finished the introductions, Keri was ready to leave.

Before getting in the car outside, Keri said, "I'll be at the shop all afternoon, so feel free to come by if you need anything."

"Okay." D considered when he might move his things over.

He planned to convert the old house into a Pokemon Tavern but needed to make some preparations first.

After thinking for a moment, D asked, "By the way, Keri, do you sell bars?"

Keri was slightly taken aback: "Bars?"

"Here's the thing," D explained, "I'm planning to convert the old house into... a tavern for Pokemon gatherings."

"A tavern? That's simple." Keri didn't think much of it, assuming he meant for Pawmo and a few other Pokemon to play house: "After you pick out furniture at the shop this afternoon, I'll deliver everything to you."

D nodded: "Sounds good."

Keri drove away with the truck.

D led several Pokemon, excited about the new house, inside.

"This will be our new home from now on," D said with a smile, "How is it? It's much bigger than the old one, right?"

"Graoh!" Cyclizar nodded repeatedly.

Alcremie and Pawmo explored the room, while Koraidon showed little surprise.

With Alcremie's small size, climbing stairs was difficult, so Pawmo carried it upstairs.

Watching the two little guys disappear at the staircase, D started getting busy.

He opened every window in the room to let air circulate, picked up a mop to clean the floor, and tidied the new home inside and out.

After everything was done, it was noon.

Sore and tired, D returned to the old house and flopped onto his bed.

From this perspective, having a big house wasn't all good; cleaning was exhausting, far beyond what one person could manage.

Staring at the ceiling, images of the Indeedee Sibling flashed in D's mind.

Almost two months had passed since that day; he wondered if they had grown, what they had become, and if they remembered the person they had met once.

---

---

---

---

# 185: Chapter 182: Gifts (Double Release)

The new house is built, and next comes the time to furnish the rooms.

In the afternoon, just after lunch, D rode his Cyclizar to town.

When he reached his destination, Keri was washing dishes. Seeing him approach, she smiled and said, "Is everything ready?"

"Yes!" D felt quite emotional; after working hard for six months, he finally had his own house: "The rooms have been cleaned, and as soon as the furniture is moved in, people can live there."

"That's good." Keri nodded, washed her hands in the sink, and then took D to the workshop, handing him a furniture catalog: "The furniture is all at the factory for now. Take a look at the designs, and if you find one you like, I'll deliver it to you later."

Upon hearing this, D nodded slightly. People in this world are quite straightforward, so there's no need to worry about the "illustrations are for reference only" situation.

He opened the catalog and started choosing from the dazzling array of furniture inside.

Occasionally, Keri offered suggestions from the side: "The overall style of that house leans towards a natural wood theme. Wooden and fabric sofas and natural wood coffee tables are very suitable for this style."

"The dining room and living room are fully open, spacious and bright, with a few potted plants inside; this nature-loving style should be well-liked by Pokemon."

At that moment, D turned to a light-colored handmade fabric sofa. Imagining what it would look like placed at home, he nodded thoughtfully: "This is it."

"You have a good eye." Keri smiled slightly.

Hearing these words, D felt quite proud.

Next, he chose other furniture from the same series.

Coffee table, tatami bed, desk, TV cabinet, curtains, floor lamp, dining table, wardrobe...

And a large, light-colored carpet with green leaf patterns.

He thought he could spread it in the living room.

Besides this, D also bought a wooden display shelf.

He planned to place all the things Teddiursa had sent back on it to display.

Although Teddiursa was not here, it was still a part of this home.

Keri noted down the items he selected and then asked, "Do you also want to buy oak barrels for wine making?"

"Of course." D nodded.

In the past two months, by selling milk, Rock Salt, and crops, he had saved up quite a bit of money. It was convenient to buy everything at once on this trip, saving him from having to come back later.

"I see..." Keri thought for a moment: "Based on the size of your cellar, it can hold about thirty oak barrels for wine making. Well, since you're buying so much, I'll give you a 20% discount this time."

"Great!" D grinned widely.

Although he wasn't short on money, who didn't like a good deal?

Seeing his expression, Keri chuckled: "Alright then, if you have no other business, head back to the ranch first. I'll go to the furniture factory later to help deliver your things."

D acknowledged, about to agree when he suddenly remembered there was something else he needed to do.

"I still need to buy a bar counter and chairs; bring them along when you deliver the other items."

Once he moved the stuff away, the old house would be empty, and he could take this opportunity to renovate it.

"Alright, add the bar counter and chairs then." Keri, knowing he planned to make wine and open a tavern, couldn't help but be curious: "Are you really opening a tavern? Your ranch is so remote; there may not be any customers."

"That's not something you need to worry about." D showed a mysterious smile: "I've got my own tricks to bring guests to the tavern."

Though the guests wouldn't be humans, but Pokemon instead.

Seeing his demeanor, Keri didn't ask more: "Alright then, let's leave it at that!"

Since D Ranch is there, she'll know the answer one day.

After paying, D left the carpenter shop.

However, he didn't return to the ranch immediately; instead, he went to the town's department store with Cyclizar.

Since he arrived in this world, Pokemon had always worked for him, and he had yet to give any Pokemon a gift.

This time, with the house move, he planned to buy a gift for everyone.

As he pondered what gifts the Pokemon on the ranch would like, he stepped into the department store with Cyclizar.

This was a very large department store.

Located by the seaside, Porto Marinada Town is commercially well-developed and trades with other regions, so you can find all sorts of items here.

The mall has three floors with a clothing area, a Trainer supplies area, and a daily necessities area—everything you could wish for.

As the name implies, the clothing area sells clothes, the daily necessities area sells various daily items, and the Trainer supplies area sells things that Trainers might need.

Things like Poke Balls, Berries, Fire Stones, and so on.

D wandered between the shelves, starting to consider what the Pokemon might like.

"Koraidon is lively and flamboyant, yet still has a childlike side; perhaps a ball toy to play with Dragonite would be fitting."

"Dragonite, although initially intent on becoming stronger, now seems to be slowly changing its mindset, no longer purely pursuing strength."

Thinking of Dragonite's adorable demeanor, D wondered if he should get it a Pokemon doll.

---

There's just the right place here that sells dolls, and the dolls look exactly like the way Pokemon appear after using the Substitute Move in the game.

"As for Cyclizar..."

D glanced at the Cyclizar beside him.

Noticing his gaze, Cyclizar happily looked over, "Wrow~!"

D smiled and patted its head, then took a red bandana from the shelf in front and put it in the small cart.

People always have a special attachment to the first thing they receive in life.

D is no exception.

Simple-minded Cyclizar, giving it this red bandana signifies that to him, it is one of a kind in this world.

Watching D continually place things into the small cart, Cyclizar showed a puzzled expression, "Wrow?"

But D did not explain, and continued walking forward.

Seeing this, Cyclizar lowered its head, feeling very down.

D: "..."

He then said helplessly, "I was planning to surprise you later! These are gifts I'm preparing for you to give later on."

Hearing this, Cyclizar's eyes lit up immediately, "Wrow!"

D: "You can't wear it yet, I haven't paid for it. I'll give it to you once we're outside!"

"Wrow!" Cyclizar nodded repeatedly.

Spending twenty minutes, D picked a gift for every Pokemon in the ranch.

A ball for Koraidon, a doll for Dragonite, a red bandana for Cyclizar, a Light Ball for Pawmo, and a box of delicious ice cream for Glaceon.

A cream processing machine for Alcremie, a Flower Bracelet for Dolliv, and a jar of specially-made nectar for Bug-type Pokemon for Butterfree.

The three Combee got a big bag of sweet soft candies, each Naclstack received a small stone sculpture in a different pose, and the Miltank got three buckets of strawberry-flavored yogurt.

The Diglett received a large grain gift pack, and the Dratini and Dragonair received fish-shaped toys.

Even the Psyduck across the river received gifts. They seem to regard that place as their habitat, so adhering to the idea that distant relatives are not as good as close neighbors, D bought each of them a straw hat.

As for the three Gogoat...

D prepared three Miracle Seeds for them.

Miracle Seed: A seed that nurtures life. When a Pokemon holds it, it enhances the power of Grass Type Moves.

Without a doubt, the Gogoat that receives this item will become even stronger.

Apart from the Pokemon in the ranch, there were also the traveling Teddiursa and the soon-to-arrive Indeedee Sibling.

D didn't know when Teddiursa would return, or when the Indeedee Sibling would arrive, but it's always good to be prepared in advance.

Teddiursa received a hat full of a heroic aura, while the Indeedee Sibling got a pair of silk scarves.

With these gifts in hand, D returned to the ranch.

He called over a group of Pokemon and distributed the gifts to everyone one by one.

D observed each Pokemon's expression and only relaxed a bit when he saw that they all wore happy expressions upon receiving their gifts.

Great, it seems everyone likes his gifts a lot.

Except for the group of Psyduck across the river treating the straw hats as food and biting at them...

D twitched his mouth, rode Dragonite across the river, picked up a Psyduck, and placed the straw hat on its head.

The other Psyduck looked at their companion with wise eyes, and then started to imitate, clumsily putting on the straw hats.

The straw hats shielded them from the scorching sunlight, and judging by the looks of the Psyduck, they seemed to find it quite pleasant.

Seeing this, D finally felt at ease as he returned to the other side of the river.

At this moment, Keri happened to drive the truck over to deliver the furniture.

D hurriedly stepped forward to help, assisting Keri and Machamp in moving the furniture into the new house.

In the ranch, the Pokemon who received gifts were all very happy.

Except for Koraidon, who didn't seem too pleased.

It looked at the ball in its hand, then at the red bandana around Cyclizar's neck.

"Arghhh..."

Koraidon's feelings were complicated. It stood there for a while, then let out a quite mature sigh.

Hearing its sigh, Dragonite was very puzzled, "Woo?" (Are you unhappy with the gift from D?)

Actually, it was quite happy. Koraidon glanced down at the ball in its hand and, remembering it was from D, carefully put it away.

Dragonite: "..."

What's going on? Dragonite didn't understand, so it decided not to think about it anymore.

It then moved toward the lakeside to see what gifts D gave to the three Dratini and two Dragonair.

Initially, Dragonite felt a bit uneasy, worried that its place in D's heart wasn't higher than those annoying little dragons, and that their gifts would be better than its own.

But upon seeing the five fish toys floating in the lake with dead fish eyes, it immediately felt relieved.

The arrival of Dragonite puzzled the Dratini and Dragonair, "Woo?" (Why are you here?)

Dragonite waved its claws, "Woo!" (Nothing, you guys continue playing!)

Under the confused gaze of the Dratini and Dragonair, Dragonite left without any reluctance, hugging its doll.

With the doll in its arms, it wandered around the ranch, seeing what kind of gifts D had given to the other Pokemon.

Beside the flower field, the three Combee, who rarely went out to collect nectar, were together with Butterfree, sharing the special nectar and sweet candies that D had given them.

---

On the roof of the Chicken House, Pawmo was lying there playing with the Light Ball in its hand.

As it channeled electricity into the Light Ball, seeing it light up, Pawmo's eyes instantly revealed a sparkle, just like a child who got a new toy.

Three Miltanks sat in the meadow, leisurely drinking yogurt from a bucket.

In the sheep pen, Dolliv wore a Flower Bracelet, happily basking in the sun.

Behind it, three Gogoats were pawing at the grass with their hooves, and then carried three seeds in their mouths, planting them in the ground.

Dragonite saw the Gogoats nurturing the seeds with Grass Energy after planting them.

Dragonite didn't think much of it, believing that D must have wanted the Gogoats to expend their extra energy, so he specifically bought seeds for them to plant.

Not interested in farming, Dragonite turned and ran off.

Underground with Naclstack and Diglett, Dragonite didn't like going underground, so it didn't bother to look.

And as for Alcremie, it was tinkering with some strange machine in the wooden cabin, which didn't interest Dragonite either.

So next, there's only Glaceon left.

Dragonite had just noticed that Glaceon had sneakily carried something back to the Ice House.

So it tiptoed to the Ice House, didn't open the door, but instead pressed its head against the window to observe what was happening inside.

What it saw next was an unbelievable scene.

The usually cold and aloof Glaceon was hugging an iron bucket like Cyclizar, laughing silly!

Every now and then it would stick its head into the iron bucket, and when it came out again, the corners of its mouth were smeared with something white and creamy!

Glaceon licked it clean with its tongue, just like a little greedy cat.

Dragonite's pupils shook as if in an Earthquake.

At this point, it seemed that Glaceon inside the Ice House sensed something and looked up.

Then, its gaze met Dragonite's outside in mid-air.

The two stared at each other through the window, Glaceon's face turning bright red.

The next second, D, who was setting up his new home, heard a scream coming from the Ice House.

"Bui—!"

Keri commanded Machamp to move the bed into the room, and hearing this sound, she looked surprised: "Is that... Glaceon's Growl?"

D looked through the window on the second floor towards that direction and saw Dragonite hurriedly running out from behind the old house, looking as if it had been frightened by something.

"Did Dragonite upset Glaceon?" Keri also noticed this scene.

"No way?" D was a little surprised, after all, both of these Pokémon didn't have the type of personality that would bother other Pokémon for no reason.

He watched for a while longer, not seeing Glaceon chase after, and Dragonite was already out of sight around a corner.

"I'll go down and take a look." D said.

Keri nodded.

D walked out of the house and eventually found Dragonite on a patch of grass.

He approached and asked, "What's up? Did you have a disagreement with Glaceon?"

"Grrr..." Dragonite shook its head repeatedly, thinking of the earlier scene, feeling a bit embarrassed.

Dragonite opened its mouth: "Grrr..."

It was about to say something when it suddenly heard Glaceon's embarrassed voice behind it: "Bui!" (Don't you dare tell anyone!)

Dragonite quickly covered its mouth with its Dragon Claw.

D turned his head to see Glaceon exuding an aura of coldness warning off strangers, warily looking at Dragonite.

He squinted, looked at Dragonite, then at Glaceon.

Ah, so Pokémon have little secrets too!

---

---

---

---

# 186: Chapter 183: Oranguru Tavern (4k)

Seeing that Dragonite hadn't mentioned what it just saw, Glaceon snorted softly, turned its red face away, and ran off.

Looking at its back, it had quite the appearance of fleeing in panic, seemingly afraid D would keep pressing for answers.

D: "..."

Alright then!

Although not sure what happened, he had a feeling asking would make Glaceon upset.

D glanced at Dragonite and prepared to go back inside to continue decorating the room.

Passing the goat pen, he noticed three Gogoats gathered around, infusing a small mound of earth with Grass Energy.

Fresh soil and peeled-off grass were visible around, clearly indicating something just happened here.

D looked surprised. He stepped forward, bent down to look at the small mound, and curiously asked, "What is this?"

The three Gogoats turned their heads in unison and cheerfully replied, "Meh!"

D dug into his pocket, pulled out a handful of shelled sunflower seeds, and tossed them into his mouth: "What?"

Gogoats: "Meh, meh~" (Digging the soil, planting seeds~)

"Seeds?" D was momentarily confused. What seeds? He had stored all the crop seeds at home in the storage box, so the Gogoats wouldn't have had a chance to get them.

Noticing his confusion, the Gogoat in the middle explained, "Meh~" (The seeds you just gave us~)

D suddenly remembered the three Miracle Seeds he bought from town as gifts.

He widened his eyes and couldn't help but say, "Those are the items for you to carry, not actual seeds!"

But the three Gogoats looked puzzled, "Meh?"

Those are clearly seeds!

They could all sense the life force contained within. Once planted in the soil and nurtured with Grass Energy, the seeds would be able to sprout life!

D: "..."

Seeing the Gogoats looking so convinced, D started doubting whether he remembered wrong.

Could it be that Miracle Seeds could actually be planted?

He carefully recalled information about this carrying item in his mind.

Miracle Seeds... when given to Grass Type Pokemon to carry, it boosts the power of Grass Type Moves.

Besides this function, the seeds also seemed to have medicinal value, used for mixing with herbs to create healing potions and also for making the famous Akala Curry.

D couldn't figure it out, but seeing the Gogoats' determined expression, he decided to let them try.

"Then, you should take good care of these three seeds!" D said.

"Meh~"

The three Gogoats nodded happily and continued tending to the seeds buried in the soil.

D turned back to the house to continue decorating the room.

Seeing him return, Keri asked, "Is everything okay with Dragonite and the others?"

"It's fine." D shook his head and guessed, "Probably because Dragonite saw Glaceon eating ice cream, making Glaceon feel socially awkward."

"Oh?" Keri was even more surprised.

D explained with a smile, "Glaceon's usually quite reserved and aloof, but when it eats something it likes, it shows an adorably clumsy side."

Previously, he noticed that Glaceon would become utterly engrossed when eating cream ice slush, naturally becoming silly in appearance.

However, he didn't mention it and pretended not to know.

Hearing this, Keri couldn't help but chuckle: "That Glaceon's personality is quite interesting."

"Yeah!" D nodded in agreement.

With the efforts of the two and several Machamps, the whole house was set up in less than an hour.

A soft green leaf carpet was laid in the living room, with a light-colored sofa placed on it, and in front of the sofa was a wooden coffee table.

The overall decor style of the living room leaned towards a forest-like, pastoral feel. The warm-toned wallpaper and furniture conveyed a tranquil, natural, and welcoming vibe, with a touch of lively warmth.

Seeing this scene, D imagined himself wearing an apron cooking, and the Pokemon gathered in the living room waiting for dinner.

He couldn't help but feel a sense of home just thinking about it.

At this moment, the Machamps came out from the cellar.

They stood next to Keri, who understood and smiled slightly: "Looks like the cellar is set up too, shall we go take a look?"

"Of course!" D nodded.

The two turned and headed to the cellar. Accompanied by a "click," the soft light illuminated the whole cellar.

Thirty oak barrels were arranged in six rows, with five barrels in each row.

Though numerous, the cellar didn't feel overcrowded; rather, it seemed just right.

"It really looks a lot like the cellar in Stardew Valley!" Seeing this scene, D suddenly had this thought.

And Keri remembered something: "Wait! You should know how to make wine, right?"

"I don't." D admitted honestly, saying he had only made wine in games, never trying it in real life.

Keri: "???"

Seeing her expression, D laughed: "But don't worry, Mayor Thomas's ranch manual has detailed records of the winemaking process, and I can slowly try it out."

Hearing this, Keri relaxed: "Then there's no problem. I see you've grown a lot of hops and fruit trees, all of which can be used for winemaking. I'll come by to sample it when the time comes. In any case, good luck!"

---

D nodded, "Don't worry!"

Keri had intended to leave directly, but seeing that some things in his old house hadn't been moved to the new home, she stayed to help with Machamp.

Once all the furniture from the old house was moved out, Machamp carried the bar counter and tables and chairs from the car into the house.

The bar counter faced the entrance, with storage cabinets underneath for placing utensils and food.

There were wine racks and cabinets installed on the wall behind the bar, where juice and beer could be placed for guests to choose from.

D had purchased a total of six sets of tables and chairs of different sizes for the tavern.

The smallest tables and chairs accommodated Pokemon like Pikachu, while the largest ones were for bigger Pokemon like Incineroar, Machamp, and Urshifu.

The fireplace in the old house was not dismantled so that in winter, it could be lit to provide warmth for the Pokemon visiting the tavern.

After everything was prepared, Keri drove away from the ranch.

Watching her figure fade into the distance, D didn't rest immediately but casually cleaned the tavern.

Only when he was sure that the floor was spotless and the walls were as clean as new did D feel satisfied and stop.

Looking at the empty wooden walls around him, D thought, "When Teddiursa sends back photos, I can frame them and hang them on the tavern walls."

These are memories of their travels, and besides being commemorative, they can let visiting Pokemon know that a helpful Teddiursa exists in this world.

"Finally done!"

Watching the beautiful sunset outside the window, D couldn't help but feel completely relaxed.

Next, there was just one final step.

With that in mind, D closed the tavern's doors and windows, took out a string of Wind Chimes from his backpack, and hung it under the eaves with the help of a group of Pokemon.

Coincidentally, a gust of evening wind blew by, and the Wind Chimes under the eaves emitted a pleasant ringing sound.

Cyclizar, Dragonite, Alcremie, Dratini, and other Pokemon were attracted by this sound.

Their eyes gleamed as they looked at the gently swaying Wind Chimes, uttering puzzled growls.

"Gao?"

"Owu?"

D smiled and replied, "That's the sound of the wind."

He led the Pokemon into their new home, "Come in and have a look!"

The Pokemon poured into the house with anticipation, and at just one glance, their eyes lit up with surprise.

Looking at the clean, spacious living room, the soft carpet, and the large floor-to-ceiling window behind the white window curtains.

The dim yellow sunlight streamed in from outside the window, adding a touch of hazy color inside, making it look very cozy.

"Do you like it?" D asked.

The Pokemon nodded repeatedly.

D smiled, turned on the lights, and turned on the TV, letting Koraidon and others play in the living room, while he put on an apron and went to prepare dinner for everyone.

The kitchen was open-plan, allowing D to see the Pokemon in the living room while he cooked.

The TV was broadcasting the evening news from the Paldea Region.

In this lively atmosphere full of life, Pawmo and Alcremie hopped around on the sofa, amazed at the elasticity and softness beneath them.

Seeing them, Dratini and Dragonair also flew over and did the same.

Cyclizar sat on another single-seater sofa, watching their antics with a grin.

Finding the sofa a bit cramped, Dragonite sat on the spacious carpet instead.

Koraidon stood nearby, happily watching this scene.

*

After dinner, the exhausted D planned to go straight to sleep, but lying in bed, he found himself unable to drift off.

—The excitement and thrill of living in a new home kept him awake.

So, D got up and went to the living room.

Looking at the wooden display shelf placed by the wall, he suddenly remembered there was something he hadn't done.

D turned and went back to his room, taking out the things Teddiursa had sent back and his previously saved possessions, arranging them on the display shelf one by one.

A Water Stone, an Everstone, a broken Poke Ball, a transparent glass bottle, pearls...

The commotion D made woke up Cyclizar sleeping on the carpet in the living room.

Cyclizar opened its eyes, looking slightly puzzled, "Gao?"

"Did I wake you?" D asked somewhat apologetically, "Sorry."

Cyclizar yawned, rubbed its eyes, feeling less drowsy, and ran over to watch D busy himself.

D explained, "These are things Teddiursa sent back. Since there aren't many ornaments at home, I plan to fill up this display shelf with them."

"Gao." Cyclizar listened, somewhat understanding.

Soon, the wooden display shelf was filled with various props and rare items.

D still wasn't sleepy, and Cyclizar seemed quite energetic too.

After thinking for a moment, an idea sparked in D's mind.

He said to Cyclizar, "Will you accompany me to the tavern?"

"Gao!"

Cyclizar nodded happily in agreement.

"Great."

D smiled and patted its head. Then, he opened the refrigerator, took out bottles of Berry Milk and some Berries, handed some to Cyclizar, and carried the rest himself.

The two quietly left the house, heading to the old house under the cover of night.

They pushed open the door, switched on the lights, and the bright light illuminated the whole room.

---

D placed the juice and milk on the wine rack behind the bar, and looking at the adorable Cyclizar in front of him, he suddenly fancied himself as a bartender in an inn.

"Would you like something to drink?"

Cyclizar shook its head, indicating it wasn't thirsty.

"..." D patiently explained, "Starting tomorrow, our pub will officially open for business. I'm worried about providing poor service, so I want to simulate situations that might occur. Right now, I'm the bartender, and you're a customer coming for a drink."

Now Cyclizar understood. It blinked its eyes, cooperatively pointing to a bottle of juice, "Gawo!"

D grabbed a bottle of juice and handed it to Cyclizar, pointing to the table and chairs nearby, "You can sit there to drink."

Although Cyclizar was a bit confused, it obediently sat on the long bench and gulped down the juice.

Watching this scene, D nodded in satisfaction and then pondered about the shortcomings in the process.

"Yes, this is the process."

"Aside from drinks, maybe I should also prepare some food for Pokemon, so they can sit down to eat and chat happily."

"The ranch work can be quite busy sometimes, and I often have to leave. It would be nice to have an assistant."

The assistant can't just be any ordinary Pokemon; it needs to be very intelligent, a Pokemon with a high IQ.

For instance, the Pokemon known as the 'Wise One,' Oranguru.

D remembers there's an Oranguru-run tavern in the Alola region.

That tavern is located deep in the forest, with a beautiful environment and is built on a tree, crafted by Oranguru itself.

Many Pokemon residing in the forest visit it, and even the Island Guardian Tapu Koko from the Alola region goes for a drink.

"Oranguru... there seems to be such a Pokemon habitat in the Paldea region too."

However, capturing such a Pokemon isn't ordinarily easy, even more difficult than the powerful Desert Tyrant Tyranitar.

Because Oranguru is extremely smart.

This type of Pokemon likes to live quietly alone deep in the forest.

It rarely moves around, spending most of its time meditating on the trees, using its developed Psychic abilities and wisdom, making it intimately familiar with every corner of the forest, even able to direct Pokemon freely by waving its fan,

This appearance led people long ago to think Oranguru was a human living deep in the forest, thus it was also called the "Man of the Forest."

Moreover, Oranguru is very adept at using human tools, and it's said someone has seen it throw a Poke Ball to capture wild Pokemon.

It's precisely because of this intelligence surpassing ordinary Pokemon that making Oranguru a human-owned Pokemon is extremely difficult.

They also rarely accept the kind of relationship involving capture, command, and obedience.

Compared to Oranguru, D even feels Calyrex might be easier to befriend.

After all, he, as a missioner, brought the legend of Calyrex to the Paldea region.

A few days ago, he heard from Zack that Porto Marinada Town had officially set a gratitude festival date, to be held on the last day of autumn, at which time people would gather to thank Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests.

D was dumbfounded when he heard this.

He thought humans in this world are quite straightforward, aren't they?

Then he heard from the town mayor that throughout summer, the nearby ranch's harvest had been excellent.

They believed it was due to Calyrex, thus the festival was established.

But whether it was due to human's own hard-working farming, or Calyrex's blessings, or both?

D is also unsure; logically, Calyrex's power is still quite weak now.

Which one it really is, perhaps only Calyrex knows.

---

---

---

---

# 187: Chapter 184: The Mutating Miracle Seeds

In the stillness of the night, when all is silent, the tavern glows with dim lights.

D leans against the bar counter with his arms crossed, gazing at Cyclizar drinking Berry Juice in the soft candlelight, his expression growing increasingly gentle.

The old-fashioned wall clock ticks away, and before he knows it, the time has reached 9:30.

Yet D feels no trace of sleepiness, as he wipes away the dust on wine glasses while pondering his plans for the future.

Today is June 28th, with only two days left until the end of summer.

Next will come autumn, when the maple leaves turn yellow.

The season change is usually a busy period, not only requiring plowing and sowing but also watering and fertilizing.

When Zack came to collect goods in the evening, D heard him say that ranchers at other ranches were nearly overwhelmed.

In comparison, his ranch was considerably more relaxed.

For plowing, there's Diglett; for watering, Dragonite; Cyclizar and Koraidon also help him sow seeds, so fertilizing is the only task that takes a bit of time.

Besides this, there's the matter of brewing to be busy with.

Brewing doesn't simply involve throwing hops into oak barrels; the steps are complex and tedious, impossible to complete in a short time.

Not to mention, he's clueless about the brewing process and still has to learn gradually.

The beginning is always difficult, the start is hard.

Perhaps, once he becomes familiar, things will be much easier.

The ranch handbook given by Mayor Thomas records the entire brewing process in detail.

Besides ordinary barley beer, there are all sorts of flavored fruit wines.

Fruit wine can be made with normal fruits or Berries.

Speaking of Berries, D remembers the Berry Garden on his ranch.

Over the summer, they've grown significantly, transforming from tender saplings into sturdy fruit trees.

At this growth rate, by mid-autumn, he'll be able to harvest a large basket of Berries.

The fruit trees he planted in spring should bloom and bear fruit even earlier.

But the sale price of Berries is too low, and D is reluctant to sell them, thinking it's better to keep them as snacks for the Pokemon on the ranch.

At that moment, several small figures flew in from outside.

D looked up to see Dratini and Dragonair entering the tavern.

He was surprised by the sight: "Why aren't you sleeping?"

"Woo~"

The Dratini and Dragonair glanced at each other, blinking.

They had been sleeping but noticed the lights on here amidst their drowsiness, so they curiously came to check it out.

"Alright." D said with a smile: "Since you're here, have a drink!"

Upon hearing this, three Dratini and two Dragonair approached the bar.

D introduced: "There's MooMoo Milk, freshly squeezed Berry Juice, and various flavored Berry Milk. Take a look and see which you like?"

The little dragons peered at the juices laid out on the bar, then lightly curled their tails to choose their preferred drink.

D pointed at the table and chairs over there: "You can drink while chatting over there."

Dratini and Dragonair happily agreed, clutching their juices and heading to a small round table, where they sat around drinking juices and transformed into little chatterboxes.

Cyclizar had finished drinking, placed the empty glass down, and stood beside D, looking at him with pure eyes as if asking if there was anything it could help with.

"Nothing," D said with a smile as he patted its head: "It's just a trial opening, no need to be nervous."

Even in a real opening, not many wild Pokemon would come here.

After all, their tavern was still largely unknown.

Early on, most of those coming here to relax and drink were the Pokemon from the ranch.

Because Dratini and Dragonair were drawn to the place and seemed to enjoy it a lot, D stayed in the tavern for a while longer.

Unexpectedly, Koraidon also came running over shortly.

Upon waking up, Koraidon discovered D's presence was no longer in the room.

It panicked a little and hurried outside to find him.

Neither the living room nor the study nor the bathroom had him...

Koraidon glanced outside and saw the lights on in the tavern.

When it arrived, it found D chatting with Cyclizar there, a gentle smile on his face.

Koraidon: "..." Feeling a bit sour inside.

Shortly after Koraidon arrived, Pawmo and Alcremie also came looking for him.

D dutifully played the role of barkeeper, ensuring each Pokemon got a drink they liked, and then directed them to sit there and chat.

In the midst of drinks clinking, the small tavern became lively.

There were even Pokemon perfectly role-playing as wild Pokemon coming to drink.

D: "..." Just felt like he was playing house.

He shook his head, feeling amused and helpless.

Having spent so much time with these childish Pokemon, he too had unknowingly become more childlike.

*

The following day, after finishing work at the ranch, D went to the seed shop in the town and purchased a batch of corn seeds, pumpkin seeds, and sunflower seeds.

These crops are summer-autumn plants and can be planted continuously.

Though the residence has been fully expanded, it doesn't mean he has nothing left to do.

---

He had to build the watermill before winter arrived.

This way, the harvested corn would have new uses: one for grinding into cornmeal in the mill, the other for making chicken feed to feed the chickens.

And the sunflower seeds could be roasted into Translation Nuts, there's never enough of these consumables.

Not to mention, in winter you can't plant things in the fields, you have to rely on the stock from summer and autumn to live.

Although a greenhouse could be built... the cost of a greenhouse is not low, and right now there's not a big demand for one.

The sunflowers in those plots have already been chopped down, and the peeled seeds have been turned into seed crunches, one piece can translate many words, convenient and hassle-free.

As for pumpkins, they can quench thirst and are a very good ingredient, they're great for making soup and porridge.

Allen mentioned, in autumn there's something called Halloween, people make mature pumpkins into jack-o'-lanterns, and apparently this festival happens at night and attracts many wild Ghost-type Pokemon to participate!

Besides, D had another idea.

Which was to see if he could grow a few more pumpkins of any size.

If he could, this time he would store the pumpkins and make pumpkin pies for Pawmo in winter, turning it into a two-meter-tall big Pokemon, then he could sleep on it.

After leaving the seed store, D went to Keri's woodworking shop.

Because he found out the floor of the old house was damaged and he planned to replace it.

Upon hearing his intention, Keri said she would go over in a bit.

Thus, D returned to the ranch first, and before he could sit down, Keri followed quickly.

She and the Machamp beside her replaced the wood flooring, and upon seeing the traces of use in the tavern, Keri looked very surprised:

"Already open for business?"

"Yes."

Keri was even more surprised, she couldn't help but speculate: "Is this tavern meant to entertain traveling Trainers?"

However, D gave a negative answer.

Seeing Keri's puzzled expression, D did not hide but explained with a smile:

"It's a tavern only for Pokemon."

Keri's tone carried a bit of shock: "Only for Pokemon?!"

"Yes." D nodded: "Whether they are wild Pokemon living in the back mountains, or ranch Pokemon, or..."

He glanced at the Machamp beside Keri and continued: "Pokemon working and living with humans."

"For example, after a day's work, busy Pokemon can come here to have a drink, chat with other Pokemon, relax, it's just for Pokemon, no other humans will disturb them."

Upon hearing this, Keri blinked: "I think I understand."

And the two Machamp behind her showed surprised expressions.

Does such a place really exist in this world?

The two Machamp started to stir.

In response, Keri was very willing to respect their wishes, and she smiled: "You guys can also come here to relax later!"

The Machamp's eyes lit up.

Keri asked again: "What's the operating hours of this tavern?"

D had already thought it out: "7 PM to 10 PM, closed on weekends."

Keri nodded, then she couldn't help but curiously ask: "What about the charges? Some wild Pokemon might not be able to pay like humans..."

D: "I'll accept whatever they give me, of course, if they give nothing that's okay too." The main thing is freedom.

Keri was dumbfounded: "Aren't you afraid of losing money?"

D shrugged: "Don't forget my main occupation! Providing this tavern for Pokemon is just a hobby."

Only then Keri remembered D is the owner of a big ranch, the daily revenue is more than enough to open a tavern, even ten would be no problem.

Not to mention the ranch where Berry trees are planted, Miltank are raised, and soon a winemaking business will start.

All this provides ample material reserves for the tavern, even without purchases, self-sufficiency can be achieved.

"Really an enviable life!" Keri said: "In that case, we will also come to visit!"

This 'we' referred to her Pokemon partners, like the two Machamp and the Conkeldurr staying at home.

D smiled and nodded: "You're welcome anytime."

So at 7 PM, the tavern officially welcomed its first guests apart from Ninetales.

——The two Machamp.

The first visit here had their hearts filled with anxiety.

But amidst the anxiety was a small anticipation.

With this feeling, the two Machamp came to the ranch together and entered the tavern.

They instinctively looked around.

The not-so-big tavern appeared very cozy, the first thing upon entering was seeing the bar, and on the shelves behind the bar, various drinks were placed.

A red square carpet was laid in front of the bar, and candle holders were hanging on the walls around, with candles lit, and the decoration was very retro.

Further inside, you could see tables and chairs arranged neatly.

At this moment, the tavern was bustling.

Dratini, Pawmo, Miltank, Glaceon, Alcremie, and a large group of Pokemon were gathered in the tavern, drinking and chatting happily.

Seeing this scene, one of the Machamp whispered: "Ni cough...?" (It's so lively here! Is everyone here to drink?)

Having eaten a dozen seed crunches, D naturally understood Machamp's meaning.

He glanced at the comedic ensemble over there, looked at the two Machamp who walked to the bar, and asked with a smile: "Want something to drink?"

The two Machamp poked around here and there, finally choosing a glass of spicy Berry Juice and a glass of sweet-and-sour Berry Juice each, sitting at an empty table.

Then, D heard them start complaining.

Machamp No.1: "Ni cough, Ni cough!" (Work is so tiring! Haven't had a chance to rest, finally there's a place to relax!)

Machamp No.2: "Ni cough." (Yeah! But Keri is as tired as we are, building houses these days is indeed too busy, it will be much easier in a few days.)

Machamp No.1: "Ni cough, Ni cough Ni cough!" (Yes, Keri is hardworking, she's really nice, I still enjoy working with her.)

Saying that, it took a sip of the spicy Berry Juice, and felt comfortable all over: "Ni cough!" (So tasty! This place is really nice!)

The two Machamp murmured while drinking up the Berry Juice, and after sitting here for a while, they left satisfied as the sun set.

Just before leaving, the two Machamp gave D two small wooden dolls in the shape of Machop.

D was slightly stunned, then realized it was the Machamp's payment for drinks.

A smile appeared on his face.

Let's say, compared to money, he preferred these heartfelt little items.

D placed the Machop wooden dolls on the top layer of the shelf behind the bar.

The Machamp had left, and there should be no more guests coming.

D handed over his position to Cyclizar and Koraidon and went back to the house to prepare dinner for everyone.

*

League year 198, June 29th.

The farm welcomed another new day.

That morning, while milking the Gogoat, D suddenly noticed that the state of the three Gogoat was not quite right.

Like their bodies were drained, spiritless, and very weak.

D: ???

"What's going on?" He immediately frowned.

These days no one has come to challenge the Grass Trial at the farm, and usually, Gogoat are very sensible in taking care of crops and fruit trees, nothing like this has ever happened before.

Upon hearing his words, the three Gogoat weakly said: "Mwah..."

They turned their heads to the culprit who drained their bodies.

At first, D was still a bit confused, and couldn't help but follow the gaze of the three Gogoat.

He then noticed the small soil mound excavated by Gogoat two days ago had sprouted a shoot.

[Mutant Seedling: A seedling that absorbed a large amount of Power of Grass, due to Gogoat causing a mutation in its growth process, there is immense Life Force brewing within, finally breaking ground, it needs another 100,000 years to mature.]

D was dumbfounded on the spot.

Unit, ten, hundred, thousand, ten thousand, hundred thousand...

What kind of thing is this? It needs 100,000 years to grow?

How many 100,000 years does a person have in life?

D's first reaction was probably he would never see the day this tree matures...

---

# 188: Chapter 185: Calyrex: Move Out! Move Right Now!

Looking at the little sprout in front of him, D furrowed his brow, thinking calmly.

Clearly, it was the Grass Energy from Gogoat that caused those three strange seeds to mutate.

Grassy Seed must have contributed as well.

But... why is this thing's growth time so long?!

Is it ten thousand years since Arceus created the world from its inception to now?

What plant would need such a long growth period?

D quickly reviewed the famous plants of this world in his mind.

Revival Herb, Eternal Flower...

No, Eternal Flower is Floette, and shouldn't be classified as a plant.

Apart from these, the only thing left seems to be the Tree of Beginning.

In D's mind appeared the ancient tree depicted in movies, nestled amidst towering mountains, tall and majestic, reaching to the sky.

The tree's massive trunk had a unique internal environment, like a complex labyrinth, nurturing a distinct ecosystem where many Pokemon resided.

No one knew where it was born or how many years it took to grow into its current form; Mew called it home, and Regice, Registeel, and Regirock willingly became its guardians, driving away humans who trespass.

Some believed it wasn't a real tree but was composed of rock layers shaped like a tree; others thought it was the representation of tree boughs petrifying over its long growth.

D's brow twitched; could Gogoat have planted Mew's home?

Good gracious, no wonder there hasn't been any mutated crop in the field for over a month; it's secretly brewing something big for him!

D thought about the appearance of the Tree of Beginning again, then looked down at the seedling in front of him, which was no bigger than his little finger, unable to reconcile the two.

He was eager to know what on earth this plant was.

But clearly, unless special measures were used, he wouldn't see it in his lifetime.

After pondering briefly, D's eyes fell upon the shrine to Calyrex that he had been worshipping.

If anyone had this kind of power, it must be Calyrex, right?

D immediately raced to Calyrex's shrine and offered incense.

Calyrex sama! Your poor little follower needs help! I will bring you endless Green Grass Cakes as offerings!

Far away in the Galar Region, Calyrex: "..."

Another heavy snow fell on The Crown Tundra.

The world was a vast stretch of whiteness, with the snow on the ground piling up deeply.

At this time of year, Calyrex generally spent it in slumber because its body was very frail.

But in the past few months, things have been different.

With the help of that devout little follower, a continuous stream of Power of Faith had surged into Calyrex's body. Though far from its peak period, it was enough for Calyrex to muster some powers.

In response, Calyrex's heart was filled with gratitude.

Calyrex thought, this little follower was truly devout, doing so much for it alone when mankind had forgotten about it.

Not only that, the little follower continued to sing its praises silently, without asking for anything in return.

The surrounding temperature was very cold, yet Calyrex's heart was very warm.

This was the first time the little follower had requested its help.

The sovereign would certainly fulfill it, Calyrex thought.

It gathered its spirits and concentrated its Psychic powers, focusing on the matter over there.

It saw the little follower placing a mountain of Berry before its shrine, nearly to the point of overflow, until some Berries rolled onto the lawn, at which point the little follower quickly picked them up and put them back.

"Do you truly like the sovereign so much?" Calyrex muttered.

If so, let the sovereign do something for you!

Seedling? Want to make the seedling reach maturity?

This was still very simple for Calyrex.

Calyrex gathered its spirits, finally locating the seedling its follower mentioned on a small mound of earth in the pasture.

It saw three sheep Pokemon gathered around the seedling.

Calyrex ignored them, elegantly raising its right hand, using its power to start facilitating the seedling's growth.

At first, Calyrex didn't notice anything amiss.

But soon, as the energy stored in its body drained, and the seedling remained unchanged, Calyrex finally sensed something wrong.

Hmm?

What exactly was this thing? It consumed such... such energy!

A trace of astonishment appeared on Calyrex's stern face.

Meanwhile, D was unaware that Calyrex had responded to his prayers.

After giving offerings to Calyrex's shrine, he wandered back to Gogoat's side.

Incidentally, he looked down at the seedling, and to his surprise, discovered something significant.

The maturation time had shortened! Its name had changed!

[World Tree Seedling: A seedling from a seed that absorbed a significant amount of Power of Grass; due to Gogoat, it mutated during growth, brewing massive Life Force within, ultimately breaking through the soil, and requires 99996 more years to fully mature.]

It suddenly shortened by four years!

D was dumbfounded, blurting out, "Damn, it's really the World Tree!"

Wait a minute! Can a world have two World Trees simultaneously?

However, clever as he was, D still noticed the difference between the two.

---

The one in Cameran Palace, Kanto Region, is named [Tree of Beginning].

And the one grown by Gogoat is called the World Tree, without the word 'Beginning'.

The difference between them might be like the difference between the Great Leiyin Temple and the Little Leiyin Temple?

D began to analyze calmly.

The Tree of Beginning is bound to Mew's life.

If the tree dies, Mew dies, and if Mew perishes, the tree is also affected.

But this World Tree grown by Gogoat clearly doesn't have such worries.

It is very likely to grow to be like the Tree of Beginning! With a fully-fledged ecological system inside!

What's even more delightful is that Calyrex's power has reduced the growth time for this tree by four years.

When he brings Calyrex and its two beloved horses to the ranch, supplemented with all kinds of meals that replenish Grass Energy, maybe he can really see this tree grow to full size within his lifetime!

Thinking of this, D was filled with excitement and suddenly had new motivation.

He looked at the trio of Gogoats beside him.

When this World Tree matures, the Gogoats which nurtured it will have their merits recognized by the tree and obtain the right to reside within the World Tree.

Perhaps the three of them could become Guardians of the World Tree, like Regice, repelling all invaders, achieving legend status.

D was overjoyed, thinking of moving his home there to experience living in a treehouse and farming within the World Tree.

A ranch on the World Tree, a mansion on the World Tree, a tavern on the World Tree…

D turned and offered a pile of Berries to Calyrex.

Although it could not eat them, the sentiment was felt.

The Crown Tundra, Galar Region.

Seeing his devotee so happy, Calyrex felt speechless.

This devotee of his was good in every way, just a bit naive.

It seemed Calyrex would need to look after him more in the future.

As the once ruler of the Galar Region, Calyrex thought about many things at that moment.

Every time he thought of how the people of this place had forgotten him, Calyrex felt very disappointed.

Then, he thought of the region where his devotee was located.

Not only was agriculture well-developed there, but the resources were abundant, and both humans and Pokémon residing there were very simple and kind.

Happiness is all about comparison.

Calyrex: "..."

Move! Move now!

Giving up the title of King of Galar!

However, it was impossible.

Even though he could transfer territory on a large scale, he couldn't achieve such feats with his current physical condition.

Therefore, he could only pin his hopes on his devotee, hoping he'd quickly come to find him and help him retrieve those two ungrateful horses.

But now, the energy accumulated in his body was depleted, and he had to sleep for a while.

As the snowflakes fell, Calyrex closed his tired eyes, hoping that by the time he opened them again, his devotee would be standing before him.

D was unaware of Calyrex's expectations of him.

Seeing Calyrex manifest again, he began to contemplate how much he had improved the affection level over the past few months.

Clearly, it was not a small number.

Otherwise, why would Calyrex grant all his requests? Even if only twice.

But to the current Calyrex, it might already mean everything to him.

Thinking of this, D's inclinations slightly shifted from the two horses to Calyrex.

He returned to the living room and glanced at the calendar hanging on the wall, finally marking November 2nd with a pen.

He planned to set out for the Galar Region on that day to try to find Calyrex, Glastrier, and Spectrier.

As for why not leave in October...

This has to do with one matter.

The seasons in this world are very distinct, so to speak.

January through March is spring, with the awakening of all things and snow slowly melting.

April through June is summer, getting increasingly hot.

July through September is autumn, with the heat slowly diminishing in early July, the weather turning high and refreshing, and maple leaves starting to yellow.

October, November, and December fall within the scope of winter.

In October, temperatures gradually drop, frost begins to form outside, and by mid-November, snow starts to fall, continuing until the end of December.

D pondered that being his first year in this world, the first winter when the weather turns cold, the animals and Pokémon on the ranch might not adapt well.

So he needed to stay, arrange everything before leaving with peace of mind.

The trip to Galar was unpredictable in terms of time, and if it started snowing halfway, the Pokémon on the ranch might be affected, which wouldn't be good.

However, Koraidon felt that D was worrying unnecessarily.

Because all the Pokémon on the ranch had experienced winters and seen snow.

But who made him D?

As long as it's D, whatever he says or does is right.

If D tells it to go west, it absolutely won't go east.

Koraidon felt there was no Pokémon more obedient than itself.

It lazily yawned and lay in a corner of the living room, enjoying the familiar life atmosphere.

Meanwhile, D embarked on his brewing endeavor.

He brought out all the hops and malt he had stored earlier, and started following the steps recorded in the ranch handbook given by Mayor Thomas.

---

Brewing is actually quite complicated.

Things like saccharification, filtration, boiling, cooling, then putting it into oak barrels to start fermentation.

Generally, the entire fermentation process takes about 15 days, but it's said that if you store it in oak barrels for longer, the matured beer will have a more unique flavor.

Oak barrels also impart a unique woody aroma to the beer, enhancing its taste.

Besides beer, fruit wines like grape wine can also be brewed using this method.

But the ranch doesn't have a vineyard yet, so D has no way to make grape wine; he can only use Berries for brewing.

So, after putting the beer into barrels, D went to Porto Marinada Town and bought a pile of Berries.

Like Cheri Berry, Pecha Berry, Starf Berry...

Each type of Berry has a different taste, some are sweet, some spicy, and some quite astringent.

Different Pokemon have different taste preferences, so D tried a bit of everything.

The brewing process lasted for a full five days.

Because brewing in real life is too complex, and the specification of oak barrels is too large, you can't just throw hops into it and wait for a harvest like in Stardew Valley.

During this time, D was busy in the cellar and didn't go out much.

By the time he and Koraidon filled the last oak barrel with liquor, it was July 4th of League Year 198.

Autumn has arrived, and the air temperature is gradually becoming cool.

D stepped out of the house, feeling the refreshing coolness of autumn, and stretched lazily.

He turned his head to look at his arm, then curiously asked Koraidon beside him, "Did you notice I've gotten a bit paler?"

"Aga-siii?" Koraidon glanced over, tilted its head, not really noticing.

Last summer, he was busy working in the fields, running around under the blaring sun, which made him darker and more rugged.

Now, seeing himself suddenly getting paler, D felt quite happy inside.

He muttered to himself, "Looks like I should spend more time in the cellar in the future."

Last time, when he finished farm work and went to Porto Marinada Market for groceries, a teenage girl called him an uncle because of his messy hair and rough appearance, leaving D almost depressed to death.

He's only in his twenties, how could he be like an uncle!

The weather today is nice, the breeze is gentle, and the sunshine perfect, even though the fields haven't been plowed yet, it can wait.

So, D directed Dragonite and Cyclizar to move a rocking chair onto the lawn, lay on it while petting Pawmo, and basked in the sun.

Pawmo was already used to this kind of thing.

It lay flat readily for the petting.

However, as D was petting it, he suddenly uttered with a trace of confusion in his tone, "Huh? Pawmo, do you have fleas on you?"

"Bamo?!" At these words, Pawmo got a huge fright.

D fiddled with its fur and finally found a tiny grain of sand inside: "Oh, it's sand; I mistook it."

"Bamo!" Pawmo looked at D with great unease.

D giggled while patting its head, "It's just me overthinking. How could you have fleas? Even if you did, you could just zap them all dead with Thunderbolt."

Pawmo pondered carefully and realizing it was true, relaxed again and lay back down to continue sunbathing with D.

As they were basking, a shadow suddenly floated overhead.

D felt the sunshine being blocked. As he opened his eyes and looked, he saw a Wingull had come from who knows where.

Its beak was slightly open, and a photo slowly drifted down, landing right on D's face.

---

---

---

---

# 189: Chapter 186: Mewtwo Strikes Back Again (Three-in-One)

Three days ago.

Mew and Teddiursa's journey was still ongoing.

That noon, they arrived at a port shrouded in dark clouds, with strong winds and heavy rain.

The sea was pitch-black, with huge waves raised by the storm crashing against the shore one after another.

The rain poured down heavily, drenching many people who then rushed into the ferry terminal in the pouring rain.

A considerable number of young Trainers who wanted to take the boat to cross the sea had already gathered here.

Miss Jenny, responsible for maintaining order, stood at the entrance with a loudspeaker, shouting to the Trainers waiting in the hall:

"Everyone! Due to the storm, today's boat can't set sail! Please wait here for a moment, the sailings will resume once the storm stops!"

"Huh?"

Sitting on a steel beam above the hall, Teddiursa looked down at the group of humans with some confusion.

Mew's gaze, on the other hand, was directed outside at the storm.

Teddiursa noticed this and asked, "Huh?" (What's wrong?)

Mew stared at the pitch-black sea, eyes filled with curiosity: "miu." (This storm is influenced by a mysterious power.)

"Huh?" Teddiursa was a bit surprised, a mysterious power?

Not only a mysterious power, but also a very familiar aura.

Mew was extremely interested: "miu!" (Let's go see!)

Of course, Teddiursa had no objections.

The two Pokemon vanished in a flash, passing through the storm to arrive at a small island.

It wasn't raining here. On the island stood a castle, with a windmill by the coast.

Looking at the continuously rotating windmill, Mew and Teddiursa's eyes both brightened, and they sat on one of the blades, beginning to spin.

"Huh!"

"miu!"

The two Pokemon giggled, looking like they were having a lot of fun.

Just then, an explosion sounded from the direction of the castle not far away.

Mew and Teddiursa's attention was drawn towards it, and they flew in the direction of the sound, only to see a human being thrown out before they got close.

"miu?"

Acting as a benevolent savior of the world, Mew used Psychic powers to create a bubble, precisely catching the cap-wearing boy.

"Huh?!" Seeing the bubble, Teddiursa's eyes lit up, instantly captivated.

It hadn't seen this ability of Mew before!

It looked like a lot of fun!

Mew winked at Teddiursa, then created another bubble, and the two Pokemon began to joyfully play on it as if it were a trampoline.

They didn't notice the eerily silent atmosphere inside the hall.

"What kind of Pokemon is that?" Misty was full of astonishment.

Beside her, Brock shook his head: "I only know that the one beside it is Teddiursa."

The other Trainers who had crossed the storm to get here also wore looks of shock.

Standing in the center of the hall, the humanoid Pokemon brimming with terrifying psychic powers narrowed its eyes.

Its gaze locked onto Mew, raising its right hand to fling a pitch-black Shadow Ball.

The Shadow Ball pierced through the bubble created by Mew, causing Mew and Teddiursa to pause before they were drawn to Mewtwo's presence in the hall.

"Huh!" (Too much!) Teddiursa was a bit angry, having never encountered such a hostile Pokemon before!

But Mewtwo paid it no heed, instead flinging several more Shadow Balls, which Mew and Teddiursa deftly dodged.

Teddiursa was very angry, extremely angry.

It glared at the unfamiliar Pokemon, then somehow pulled out a small stone, hurling it at the opponent in anger.

"It's a Dark Type move, Fling." As a Gym Leader, Brock immediately recognized the move's name.

The stone enveloped in ghostly light shot towards Mewtwo like an arrow, but just before hitting, Mewtwo blocked it with a manifested Psychic Shield.

Its gaze finally fell on Teddiursa, releasing a terrifying aura. The overwhelming psychic power surged forth:

"This place has nothing to do with you!"

Teddiursa floated in mid-air, unmoving, as the psychic wave just grazed over its head, ruffling a tuft of hair.

"Did it miss?"

Mewtwo was slightly taken aback, not believing it had missed.

Realizing the other was attacking it, Teddiursa became even angrier.

They hadn't done anything!

This Pokemon was really rude!

Teddiursa gritted its teeth in anger, then broke away from Mew's flight and shot forth like a missile.

Seeing this, Mewtwo coldly said: "You are no match for me!"

It flung five or six Shadow Balls, but each one narrowly missed Teddiursa.

Mewtwo was dumbfounded: "Such good luck?"

In its moment of hesitation, the Psychic Shield around it had a slight opening, which Teddiursa's small body managed to slip through.

With a swift flick, Teddiursa's sharp claws extended, leaving a faint scratch on Mewtwo.

Mewtwo finally reacted, darkening its expression as powerful Psychic power erupted from its body, sending the approaching Teddiursa flying.

Teddiursa crashed onto a soft cushion, from which came a muffled groan, yet it was fortunate enough not to be harmed.

---

Brock, knocked to the ground, stared in shock at the Teddiursa on him.

Did he see that right?!

This Teddiursa actually hurt Mewtwo!

"Teddi!"

Teddiursa jumped off Brock, calmly observing the surroundings.

At this moment, Mewtwo's attention turned back to Mew once again.

The two looked at each other from a distance.

Mewtwo said in a deep voice, "Mew, the rarest Pokemon in the world. I was indeed created based on you, but I'm the stronger one, I am the real original! Only I can survive!"

"Miu..."

Mew just felt a bit baffled.

It had never provoked this unfamiliar Pokemon.

The reason it came over was because it sensed the presence of other Mews here.

As a result, it hadn't found another Mew but met this guy instead.

Mew didn't want to fight, it wanted to say more, but the other side attacked again.

Mew could only dodge while trying to communicate with the other.

But the opponent still didn't stop.

Mew felt a bit angry.

"Why won't you fight me? Are you avoiding battle because you're afraid of me?" Mewtwo questioned.

The aggressiveness of this Pokemon, plus the fact that it had just attacked Teddiursa, made Mew even angrier.

It didn't speak, just quietly unleashed its Psychic power, blasting Mewtwo away.

Upon seeing this, Teddiursa looked admiringly, "Teddi!"

Truly worthy of admiration!

"Hmph." Mewtwo snorted coldly, emerging from the ruins: "It seems you're a somewhat tricky opponent."

It flew above the group of cloned Pokemon and gave an indifferent order: "Let's determine now who is correct, Mew and I, the originals and the clones, who's stronger!"

"We were created to be stronger than the originals!"

With Mewtwo's command, all the cloned Pokemon moved below.

It and Mew turned into two distinctly different light clusters, colliding and entwining as they flew toward the sky.

Continuous explosions rang out from above, and below, in the ruins of the castle, the cloned Pokemon and their originals were locked in combat.

The situation suddenly turned like this, and Teddiursa felt both panicked and deeply confused.

It shouted loudly to the surroundings, "Teddi!" (What's going on? Why did things suddenly turn out this way?)

But no Pokemon responded, they continued to fight nonstop, whether it was really their own intention or not, it was unstoppable now.

Vulpix, Gyarados, Pidgeot, Pikachu, Bulbasaur...

Their injuries became more severe, their strength waning, even their movements were slowed...

Watching this tragic scene, Teddiursa felt extremely panicked and couldn't help but think it would be better if D were here.

But D wasn't here now, it could only rely on itself.

Teddiursa shouted loudly to the surroundings, "Teddi!" (Stop it! Stop fighting! Everyone is a Pokemon, why are we hurting each other?)

Not far away, Miss Joy looked pained and murmured in a low tone, "What is the point of this battle... whether original or cloned, everyone is alive now."

Brock: "Even if they were created, they are still creatures of this world!"

Misty, unable to bear it, said: "Both originals and clones are living beings, why do we have to decide a winner..."

Miss Joy clasped her hands tightly in prayer over her chest: "Living things don't easily give up their territory to others of their kind, they'll fight until the other is driven away, that's nature!"

"As long as Mewtwo and Mew don't stop, these Pokemon won't stop either..."

Teddiursa listened quietly to these words, realizing that during its journey, it had long since regarded every Pokemon it met as its own kind.

But now, these Pokemon were fighting each other relentlessly...

Teddiursa felt deeply pained, its eyes growing moist, it didn't want to witness this any longer.

So, it raised its head and shouted loudly, "Teddi!"

In mid-air, hearing Teddiursa's voice, Mew paused and decisively abandoned its fight with Mewtwo, landing on the field below.

Teddiursa rushed forward, pointing at the surrounding scenes and spoke to Mew, "Teddi... Teddi!"

The next second, Mewtwo's attack followed closely.

A huge Shadow Ball blasted forth, and the terrifying energy immediately engulfed Mew and Teddiursa.

Instantly, dust and smoke rose in the ruins, silence reigned, and everything became still.

"Hmph." Mewtwo snorted coldly.

However, in the next second, an even stronger force burst out from the dust and smoke.

Accompanied by a roar of "Teddi!", a mysterious light flickered on Teddiursa, it grabbed Mew's hand, then hurled Mew with all its strength.

Mewtwo's body was instantly knocked away, its mouth slightly agape, showing a surprised expression.

A blue light flickered in Mew's eyes.

The Teddiursa from behind vanished, appearing above Mewtwo.

It swung its sharp claws, seizing the moment to deliver a fierce strike to the unsuspecting Mewtwo.

"Teddi! Teddi?" (Enough! Do you really want everyone to die in battle? Everyone is a Pokemon, why are we hurting each other?)

"Miu!" Mew was also extremely angry.

---

But Mewtwo remained indifferent, blasting the two away: "To prove that we are the strongest!"

Teddiursa leapt forward again, questioning: "But, what does proving we are the strongest achieve? Afterward, don't we still have to live in this world? Why can't we just get along and live together already?"

Mewtwo was left speechless, only able to snort coldly, as its psychic energy transformed into a focused Psycho Cut and blasted out.

With Mew's Helping Hand, Teddiursa's figure flickered, dodging that Psycho Cut.

Then Mew replaced Teddiursa, and the two most powerful Pokemon in the world clashed once more.

At this moment, a silhouette suddenly rushed in, running between Mew and Mewtwo.

"Enough already!"

Mew and Mewtwo both paused slightly.

Mew instinctively tried to retract the move, but it was too late.

Two entirely different energies collided on Ash's body. In the violent explosion, Ash's body turned into a statue and slowly fell to the ground, lifeless.

Witnessing this scene, all the surrounding humans and Pokemon were stunned.

Mewtwo frowned deeply, speaking with extreme anger: "Foolish! A mere human trying to stop our battle!

"Pika Pika!"

A Pikachu ran anxiously forward, reaching Ash's side, repeatedly calling his name, trying to revive him with a Thunder Shock like always.

But even after depleting its body of Electric Type Energy, the person who had fallen could not stand up again.

"Pika Pika!"

Pikachu let out a cry both painful and sorrowful, tears uncontrollably falling onto the ground.

Witnessing this scene, Teddiursa was moved.

"But..."

It turned its head and realized that the surrounding Pokemon had stopped fighting at some point.

They looked at that human and Pikachu, starry tears dripped from their eyes.

Then, a miraculous scene occurred.

The starlight began to gather around Ash, accompanied by a warm glow, Ash slowly opened his eyes.

Pikachu's eyes lit up: "Pika Pika!"

"Pikachu!" Ash, seeing the battle had stopped, knew he had succeeded and joyfully embraced Pikachu.

"Is that... Life Dew?" Miss Joy murmured softly, knowledgeable as always.

"Life Dew?" Brock looked over in confusion.

Miss Joy: "It's a Water Type move, expressing emotional energy in the form of dew drops."

"A long time ago, a Pokemon lost its life in battle, and its Trainer, who had spent every day with it, used this move in extreme grief."

"The Trainer's tears fell on that Pokemon, and the Pokemon awakened."
