"Humans... can use Pokemon moves?" Misty and Brock were dumbfounded.

————

In mid-air, Mewtwo silently observed the scene below.

It didn't fight anymore, only feeling its mind in turmoil, deeply conflicted.

"Why?"

Beside it, Mew looked over: "Miu." (They are all life, part of this world, whether human or cloned Pokemon.)

Mewtwo closed its eyes and suddenly said: "Indeed, you and I both exist, both are... Pokemon."

"Perhaps it's best if no one knows about this, perhaps it's best forgotten."

"We were all born, we live, we need to continue living... somewhere in this world!"

Mew tilted its head, looking at Mewtwo, who seemed to have realized something.

A blue light shone from Mewtwo's body.

The cloned Pokemon below also began to shine with a blue light, following Mewtwo into the clear sky.

Seeing this, both Mew and Teddiursa followed along.

Mewtwo glanced sideways, looking at the fortunate Teddiursa and Mew.

Only then did it remember to ask: "Who are you?"

Teddiursa: "..."

Mew: "..."

The two Pokemon glanced at each other, then both sighed.

Teddiursa: "But!" (We are the Heroic Expedition Team traveling the world!)

Mew covered its mouth, stifling a laugh.

Mewtwo was puzzled: "Heroic... Expedition Team?"

Teddiursa explained: "But, but." (We travel while helping Pokemon companions in need, we've helped many Pokemon! Currently, it's just the two of us in the team.)

Mewtwo roughly understood.

It responded with an "Oh," turning to gaze into the distance, saying no more, beginning to silently reflect on something.

Time seemed inconsequential, and they landed on a deserted cliff.

The scenery here was exceedingly beautiful, the steep cliffs were a strong deterrent to any human visits.

Mewtwo looked around and spoke: "We are not ready to be discovered by humans, so we'll live here for now. What about you? Do you want to live with us?"

Teddiursa shook its head: "But!" (There are many Pokemon in this world waiting for us to help!)

Mew nodded in agreement: "Miu!"

Mewtwo was slightly taken aback: "I see."

The Heroic Expedition Team prepared to leave.

But before departing, Teddiursa took out its camera.

It called over Mewtwo, saying: "But!" (Everyone, let's take a group photo!)

Mewtwo was puzzled: "Group photo?"

Teddiursa explained about the camera and photos.

---

Upon hearing that humans would see them, Mewtwo immediately floated back, face darkening: "We don't want humans disturbing our lives."

Teddiursa furrowed its brow and exchanged a look with Mew, both feeling quite puzzled: "Umm..." (But, D is a really good human...)

Mewtwo: "D?"

Teddiursa nodded: "Umm... umm." (Yes, he's the one who helped me. Without D, I would still be living a clueless life in the mountains, stealing honey every day. Maybe one day I'd get caught by other humans... In short, D is the best human in this world!)

As Teddiursa spoke, its eyes were incredibly bright, as if stars sparkled within them.

Mew agreed wholeheartedly: "miu!" (D's cooking is the most delicious food in the world!)

Mewtwo: "..."

At this moment, Teddiursa suddenly remembered something: "Umm! Umm umm!" (Right, D has opened a tavern specifically for Pokémon! Pokémon in need will all go there to seek D's help!)

Mew generously shared its memories with Mewtwo.

After viewing this memory, Mewtwo fell into deeper silence.

Because that human... seemed a bit odd.

It couldn't even imagine burying its face in a Pokémon's belly.

Having inherited human genes, Mewtwo felt a bit of shame.

But it had to admit, that human was very kind to Pokémon.

It also understood Teddiursa and Mew's intentions.

However... they weren't ready yet, at least not now.

Teddiursa's tone turned downcast: "Umm..." (Alright...)

Both it and Mew looked rather dejectedly at the camera.

Mewtwo: "..."

After a moment's silence, it stepped forward: "Only one photo."

"Umm!"

"miu!"

Mew and Teddiursa's eyes sparkled instantly.

They took hold of Mewtwo, and alongside the cloned Pokémon group, snapped two photos.

One was kept for Mewtwo and the cloned Pokémon, the other intended to be sent back home to share their travels with D.

*

Looking at the photo in his hand, D nearly spit out the juice he had just sipped.

Did he see it wrong?

Was this Mewtwo?

When did Teddiursa and Mew run into Mewtwo?!

Could it be that Lucky Pudding played a role again?

"Scary."

Having encountered Mewtwo now, D couldn't even imagine what else they could meet in the future.

Once the excitement of seeing Mewtwo subsided, D gradually calmed down.

Stay calm, stay calm, Koraidon is already farming with him, seeing a photo of Mewtwo is no big deal.

With that thought, D stood up, framed the photo, and hung it on the wall of the small tavern.

He glanced at the time, realizing it was already 4:50 PM.

Just then, the sound of a truck came from outside the ranch.

D went out to sell today's milk and goat's milk to Zack, and then began to prepare dinner for the day.

After he and the Pokémon had finished dinner, the time was just 7 PM.

D leisurely opened the small tavern's door and began the evening's business.

At 7:05 PM, two Machamp arrived on time.

They ordered two glasses of juice as usual, chattering about the day's events.

D sat behind the bar, listening to their chatter.

Soon after, a Quaquaval swaggered in from outside.

This was the Pokémon captain of Porto Marinada Town's fire brigade.

Two days ago, it had somehow heard of a small tavern dedicated to Pokémon here, so every day after work, it would waddle its way over, sipping a glass of juice elegantly.

"Was today's work smooth?" D asked with a smile, pouring it a glass of juice.

Quaquaval took the highball glass gracefully: "Quaa." (Very smooth.)

It walked to an empty table, sat down, and listened to some Dratini and Dragonair chatting while sipping its juice.

Quaquaval sat in the tavern until 9 PM before leisurely getting up to pay for today's drinks—with five deep blue feathers.

D: "..."

It must be said, this Pokémon was quite narcissistic.

---

---

---

---

# 190: Chapter 187: Indeedee Sibling (5k)

Listening to Pokémon chat is a very interesting thing.

You can gather all kinds of information and intelligence from their chatter.

Such as where a fire broke out in town, what kind of Pokémon appeared where, or what Pokémon got lost, or if a Pokémon dueled with another for love.

When listening to their chatter, D doesn't interrupt, he just quietly sits behind the bar and reads.

However, his Pokémon occasionally join in, asking Machamp and Quaquaval other questions.

With the departure of Quaquaval and Machamp, it was already completely dark outside.

D glanced at the time, it was already 9:15 PM.

He looked outside the window and figured that there probably wouldn't be any more guests visiting tonight.

Thinking so, D planned to close up and rest, but just then, barking and the roar of a motorcycle engine came from outside.

D was a bit surprised.

So late and still a visitor?

He went outside to check and saw Miss Jenny in a police uniform chasing a Growlithe on a three-wheeled motorcycle.

Growlithe was running excitedly in front, barking as it ran.

"Growlithe, where are you going?"

Miss Jenny, chasing behind, was full of concern.

Just after their patrol ended and they were about to clock off, their police station buddy Growlithe suddenly dashed off like it was on something.

The other officers at the station were all dumbfounded.

Only Miss Jenny reacted quickly, hastily riding the motorcycle to chase after it.

Along the way, Growlithe would stop and start, seemingly waiting for her on purpose.

Seeing this, Miss Jenny was even more puzzled.

What on earth could be attracting Growlithe?

After a brief thought, Miss Jenny decided to join Growlithe and see what was going on.

They traveled through country roads shrouded in night, eventually reaching a brightly lit ranch.

Miss Jenny blinked and her gaze landed on a man standing in front of a wooden house.

Growlithe stopped in front of the man and barked twice.

Miss Jenny turned off the engine, got off the motorcycle, and looked at the scene puzzled.

She frowned, her face adopting a serious expression.

Could this person deliberately use some means to lure others' Pokémon over and then steal them?

Out of professional instinct, Miss Jenny subconsciously put her hand on the handcuffs at her waist, ready to subdue him if anything happened.

"Who are you?" Miss Jenny asked seriously.

D: "..."

D keenly noticed Miss Jenny's action, he smirked and said without words:

"No need to worry, Miss Jenny, I'm D of D Ranch. Your Growlithe probably heard from other Pokémon about the tavern being open, so it came here."

Miss Jenny was slightly taken aback. She looked at the young man through the night and then retracted her hand from the handcuffs.

"D Ranch... I've heard of this name in town."

D smiled and bent down to ask, "Growlithe, did you come to visit the tavern?"

Growlithe wagged its tail excitedly, nodding continuously: "Woof woof!"

"Then come on in!"

D found this Growlithe quite friendly, and couldn't help but ruffle its head before welcoming both into the tavern.

Inside the tavern, Dragonair, Dratini, and Pawmo were still chatting under the warm lights.

As soon as it entered, Growlithe's attention was caught by them.

It seemed even more excited.

"Woof woof!" There really is a tavern specially serving Pokémon!

Growlithe couldn't bother to order and happily ran to greet Pawmo and the others.

Miss Jenny walked into the tavern, looking at the scene, feeling rather helpless: "This kid is just too playful."

D grinned.

Miss Jenny never expected to find a small tavern open here.

But since she was already here, she might as well take a good rest and have a drink to relieve today's fatigue.

Just as she was about to order, she couldn't find the drinks menu.

Seeing Miss Jenny's puzzled expression, D turned around, grabbing a white milk jug and two clear glasses from the shelf.

He poured the watermelon juice from the milk jug into the glasses, added a few ice cubes, and then pushed the glass of iced watermelon juice in front of Miss Jenny.

"This tavern is primarily for serving Pokémon, but since it's just opened, this drink is on the house," D said.

Hearing this, Miss Jenny was full of surprise: "Exclusively... for Pokémon?"

"As you can see."

Miss Jenny turned her gaze towards the area and finally realized what was off!

This tavern was filled with Pokémon; there wasn't a single human!

"But... but..." Miss Jenny was incredibly astonished.

"You could say this is my side job!" D smiled, placing the milk jug back on the shelf, "I really like Pokémon, so I opened this tavern. This way, I get to see more Pokémon and watching them here relaxes me."

"This is just unbelievable!" Miss Jenny murmured.

She looked at the iced watermelon juice in front of her and politely thanked him.

---

D shook his head, indicating it was nothing.

He gestured for Miss Jenny to take a seat at the table over there and rest for a while.

Miss Jenny thought for a moment, then carried two glasses of watermelon juice to the table.

She called over Growlithe, and due to its body structure, Growlithe couldn't sit on a chair and could only squat on the ground.

But for Growlithe, that was enough.

Its big head just reached above the table, and it began sipping the watermelon juice through a straw, sneaking glances at the group of Pokemon beside it.

What a lively place! thought Growlithe, this place is so fun.

Meanwhile, Miss Jenny began to observe the decor of the bar.

Upon entering, there was the bar counter, and installed on the wall inside was a wine rack, next to it a cupboard, and on the other side, a refrigerator.

The lighting within the bar was a warm orange, and the floor at the entrance was covered with a red carpet, making it look even more warm and lively, creating a perfect ambiance.

On the left wall, there were simple line drawings like those made by children and several photographs.

Miss Jenny's gaze was drawn to the photographs.

She couldn't help but stand up and walk over to the photo wall.

The photograph showed a stunning cliff, adorned with lush grasslands and thickets, with a magnificent waterfall cascading down, forming streams through the mountains and forests below, appearing like the veins of the wilderness, visibly breathing in the scenery.

Standing on the grassland was a large group of Pokemon.

Some she recognized, others she didn't.

Teddiursa, Meowth, Growlithe...

Like a pink kitten, a small Pokemon with blue eyes.

Covered in pink, fuzzy fur, with an indifferent expression, it had a humanoid build with a big tail, looking unwilling, as if not wanting to take the photo.

Apart from this Pokemon, the other Pokemon looked quite happy.

Miss Jenny couldn't resist asking, "Are these Pokemon also guests of the bar?"

D looked up, seeing Miss Jenny standing in front of the photo wall.

He shook his head, "No, that photo... was sent back by a friend traveling outside."

"Oh," Miss Jenny understood, marveling, "I've never seen these two Pokemon before... It seems there are indeed many Pokemon in this world that humans haven't discovered yet!"

"Yes!" D smiled, not saying much more.

Soon, Growlithe had downed the glass of chilled watermelon juice.

Miss Jenny paid the bill, and since it was getting late, she led Growlithe out of the bar, ready to go home and rest.

Growlithe didn't want to leave yet, seeing its reluctant look, D smiled and waved, "Come back tomorrow! My bar is open every day except weekends."

"Woof woof!"

Growlithe's eyes lit up, finally content, it followed Jenny away.

On the way, it barked a couple more times while looking back.

D heard it say, "I'll be back tomorrow!"

Watching the figures of the person and dog disappear into the darkness, D stretched lazily and relaxed, then turned to the Pokemon around him and said:

"It's time for us to go to sleep too!"

"Vee～"

Glaceon was in a good mood, gave D a smile, then bid farewell and ran back to its Ice House.

The three Miltank yawned, each grabbing a cup of Gogoat milk before returning to the Pokemon House to sleep.

Dratini and Dragonair also started feeling sleepy.

They were so sleepy they could barely keep their eyes open, yet they still mustered the strength to run over to D and nuzzle his cheeks.

Then they happily returned to their Moon Lake, lying down in the reflection of the moon on the lake, snoozing away.

Watching this scene, Dragonite stomped its feet in frustration, muttering a few words at the little dragons competing with it for the Trainer, then hugged D tightly.

D: "..."

He helplessly patted Dragonite's chubby belly, "Alright, you should go to sleep too!"

Dragonite looked down, blinking, then copied Dratini and Dragonair's behavior, affectionately nuzzling D's face with its large round face.

D: "..."

"Roar～"

Dragonite was happy, swiftly turning around and slipping away under Koraidon's dangerous gaze.

"Agass…" Koraidon thought Dragonite was extremely childish.

It turned its head to look at D, whose face showed an expression of helplessness from all the nuzzling.

D sensed Koraidon's gaze and thought it wanted to nuzzle too, so he straightforwardly didn't resist: "Come on!"

He ran ahead, went to each of the remaining Pokemon, and gave them a nuzzle.

Koraidon widened its eyes, "Agass?!"

Cyclizar couldn't believe it, "Gawo?!"

Pawmo skillfully flew into D's arms and started to use Charm, "Bamo～"

As for Alcremie... it had already gone back to its nest to sleep early, not staying up late with them.

"Let's go, back to the house to sleep!"

D picked up Pawmo, followed by Koraidon and Cyclizar, walking towards their home under the moonlight.

*

The next morning, a new day had begun.

Today was already July 5th, and the fields lay fallow, yet to start the new round of planting.

D realized he couldn't continue to laze around, so early in the morning, he called on Diglett to help him turn the soil, planting a new batch of pumpkin seeds and sunflower seeds.

---

He ate a few pieces of Translation Nuts Cake, then called over several Pokemon to help with farm work.

Dragonite was still practicing Rain Dance by watering the fields.

Gogoat, as usual, came over to use Grassy Terrain to help take care of the crops in the field.

While they were busy, D seized the opportunity to take a look at the World Tree sapling.

[World Tree Seedling: A seedling that has absorbed a large amount of Power of Grass, underwent a mutation in the process of growth due to Gogoat, where exuberant Life Force is brewing within, and finally emerged from the soil. It still needs 99,995 years and 363 days to fully grow.]

"Two days less!" D was somewhat delighted.

No doubt, there's credit owed to Gogoat!

D turned his head to look at the three Gogoat.

"It seems Grass Energy can help speed up the growth of the World Tree!"

But speaking of which, because they are helping to take care of this seedling, Gogoat doesn't need to dance daily anymore...

This made D, who was used to seeing Gogoat dance, feel a bit unaccustomed.

He stood up, preparing to observe Gogoat tending to the pumpkins and sunflowers in the fields.

It was at this moment that a black luxury car suddenly stopped outside the ranch.

The Butterfree, sensing the unfamiliar presence, immediately informed D.

D looked up to see an old man in a brown suit and a fedora hat stepping out of the car.

The old man held a gentleman's cane in his hand, looking very dignified.

From a distance, the old man saw D and nodded with a slight smile, "D!"

"Mr. Sato!" D called out excitedly.

This old man was the owner of the two Indeedee that Allen had taken him to see more than two months ago.

If Mr. Sato appeared here...

D felt a stir in his heart and quickly went to greet him.

Mr. Sato still looked as friendly as ever, "Sorry, some matters delayed me, so I'm late."

"It's fine." D glanced at the luxury car and asked, "Would you like to come in for a cup of tea?"

Seeing his gaze, Mr. Sato laughed heartily: "No need for tea, I have to visit a friend later."

As he spoke, he turned around and said to the back seat of the black car, "Come on out! We're here."

The back seat door opened, and two normally sized Indeedee silently stepped out.

The larger one with upward horns pursed its lips, its face showing no expression.

This is the brother, D thought.

The slightly smaller one with downward horns was intentionally protected behind the brother.

Unlike the brother, the sister Indeedee had a very gentle expression, her eyes bright. Seeing D look at her, her lips curved up in a gentle smile.

The brother Indeedee noticed the sister's action, its lips turned downward, seemingly displeased.

It gave the sister a disapproving look and said softly, "Indee." (We agreed to first observe this human's character.)

The sister Indeedee quickly retracted her smile and said softly, "Indee!" (I know!)

Seeing this, the brother Indeedee shifted his gaze back to the human in front.

D, who heard their loud conspiracy, thought: "..." Luckily, he ate several pieces of Translation Nuts this morning to direct the Pokemon's work.

D didn't mind their words but rather observed their appearance, nodding to himself:

"In these past two months, they've grown quite a bit, almost unrecognizable from those little things from before."

Just wondering if they still remember him.

Mr. Sato introduced with a smile: "This is Mr. D, who will take care of you in the future. You've met him once when you were little, remember? He visited the manor and brought you pudding."

Hearing this, the sister Indeedee immediately nodded excitedly: "Indee!" (Of course I remember!)

The brother Indeedee's lips turned down, looking slightly unhappy: "Indee..." (We agreed...)

The sister Indeedee stuck her tongue out, said obediently: "Indee! Indee?" (I know, I know! Brother, did you like the pudding?)

The brother Indeedee remembered the event, having tasted the pudding.

Hearing the sister's words, it seriously recalled the taste and nodded: "Indee." (It was delicious.)

The sister Indeedee, somewhat puzzled: "Indee? Indee!" (Then why don't you like this human? The pudding is good, and the human is nice!)

The brother Indeedee responded calmly: "Indee." (That's different.)

It worried inwardly and seriously told the sister: "Indee." (When seeing a human for the first time, don't invest too much emotion, don't like them too much. If they don't like us and abandon us, it will be very distressing.)

This was advice from a Meowth in the manor who had experienced abandonment.

That Meowth had been captured by a human before, but the human disliked it for being too weak and abandoned it.

Later, it was lucky to encounter Mr. Sato, who adopted it, and it has lived in the manor ever since.

So, as the brother, before ensuring this human wouldn't abandon Pokemon, it must protect the sister.

The sister Indeedee seemed to understand only partially.

Hearing this, D looked at the brother Indeedee, who was acting like a little adult, diligently protecting its sister, feeling very complex inside.

He didn't directly converse with the two Indeedee but instead turned to Mr. Sato and said:

"Mr. Sato, let me introduce you to the ranch!"

As the caretaker of the Indeedee, Mr. Sato surely wanted to know what kind of life they would have here, so he needed to explain everything in detail.

As expected, hearing this, Mr. Sato's wrinkled face broke into a bright smile.

With a laugh, he said, "Then I will defer to your good judgment!"

---

# 191: Chapter 188: Scatterbrain and Grumpy

"Please come with me!"

D began to wander around the farm with Old Man Sato and the two Indeedee.

The over-fifty-year-old Old Man Sato casually glanced at the farm.

He saw neat fields, blooming flower beds, and meticulously trimmed lawns...

Next to the fields, a few Pokemon were helping out, including Gogoat, Dragonite, and a Psyduck wearing a straw hat, daydreaming on the grass.

Gogoat seemed to be using Grass Type energy to tend the crops, while Dragonite stood at the edge of the field, raising its arms in a cheer.

Suddenly, a dark cloud gathered, and a gentle drizzle began to fall, nourishing the farmland below.

When the rain stopped, a beautiful rainbow appeared in the sky above the fields under the sunlight.

Watching this scene, Old Man Sato nodded repeatedly.

From these things, it was clear that the owner of this farm was a very hardworking person.

D, alongside him, watched this orderly scene, organizing his thoughts in his mind before slowly speaking:

"I moved to this farm in the spring. Before that, it had been abandoned."

Old Man Sato was slightly surprised: "In the spring?"

D nodded and briefly introduced himself, "Yes, my parents and I moved to the Hoenn Region over a decade ago, and this farm had always been managed by my grandfather."

"Later, when he passed away, the farm gradually fell into neglect. Not long ago, I decided to return to my hometown to take a look, and on a whim, I started to restore and manage the farm again."

Old Man Sato showed an appreciative look: "Managing the farm to such a state all by yourself is truly impressive!"

D smiled warmly, his gaze shifting to the Pokemon playing on the farm:

"It's not just me; they all help out. We put in a lot of effort to turn it into what you see now."

Heaven knows how tiring it was at first.

Fishing trip after trip, selling fish, until the arrival of Gogoat and Naclstack made things a bit easier.

Hearing this, Old Man Sato's gaze turned to the distant grass.

There, an Alcremie was riding on a Naclstack, followed by four other Naclstack, spinning in circles.

On the adjacent grass, a Dolliv was basking in the warm autumn sunshine.

Looking further in, one could see a Glaceon lying in front of a small yellow flower, leaning in to gently sniff its fragrance.

Its face was filled with a leisurely and cheerful expression, and perhaps having accidentally inhaled some pollen, the Glaceon sneezed several times in succession.

Just from the vibrant appearances of these Pokemon, it was evident they were well cared for.

"The owner of this farm really knows how to take care of Pokemon." Old Man Sato's second impression of this young man formed in his mind.

"Let's go see over there!"

The two continued forward, walking along the country path D had cleared.

D began introducing the various facilities of the farm to Old Man Sato:

"This is the flower bed I developed separately. I plant seasonal flowers here every season, and Beautifly and Combee frequently visit. The honey collected by Combee is made into honey used by the Pokemon on the farm."

Old Man Sato followed the direction of his hand and indeed saw three Combee and a Beautifly fluttering among the flowers.

He nodded, saying, "They look like they really enjoy it here."

"Indeed!" D smiled, for there was nothing more joyous than having something he made be loved by Pokemon.

He continued introducing the remaining facilities:

"Those buildings over there are the small house where Miltank lives, the chicken coop, and the place where Naclstack stays. Although the house seems small, it actually has a passage leading to an underground cave where the Naclstack normally reside."

"Milk from cows and sheep, eggs, and rock salt—all these are the main sources of income for the farm and also provide the daily food for the Pokemon living here."

Things like Berry Milk, egg sandwiches, and in addition, eggs, milk, and flour can be made into bread and other pastries.

"Just as I imagined, it's full of a pastoral feel!" Old Man Sato laughed heartily.

His gaze moved to the orchard behind the buildings and asked, "If I'm not mistaken, those are fruit trees over there, right?"

"That's right." D nodded. Over several months, the saplings had grown into lush small trees. "In just over a month, they will yield Berries."

The selling price of Berries was too low, so D didn't plan to sell them. Instead, he intended to use them to make wine or as snacks for Pokemon.

Thinking of this, D felt his farm was becoming increasingly prosperous, feeling confident he could support a hundred more Pokemon.

However, despite this confidence, he couldn't actually do it.

Because taking care of Pokemon requires a lot of energy.

After visiting the farm, Old Man Sato was very satisfied.

This young man is not only hard-working and diligent, but good at caring for Pokemon. From his interactions with the Pokemon, it was clear the farm's Pokemon enjoyed being around him.

Old Man Sato nodded repeatedly in admiration, though he showed no outward expression, instead turning his attention to the two Indeedee silently following them.

The sister Indeedee's eyes were sparkling.

Even though she hadn't joined the farm yet, she could already feel the lively atmosphere here.

She really liked it and wanted to be part of it.

The brother Indeedee's focus was on the group of Pokemon on the farm.

As they walked along the path, those Pokemon were secretly observing them too.

He keenly sensed the emotions in those gazes.

Curiosity, surprise, happiness, wariness...

Then, the brother Indeedee noticed the expectant expression on his sister's face.

He pursed his lips, and his already displeased little face looked even less happy.

Seeing this, D was curious.

What did this little grown-up Indeedee want to say? Did he not like it here?

The brother Indeedee pouted, "Indeedee..." (Sister...)

The sister Indeedee blinked, seemingly guessing what he wanted to say, "Indeedee! Indeedee?" (Brother, I like it here! Don't you?)

The brother Indeedee replied earnestly, "Indeedee." (I like it too.)

D: "..."

If you like it, why show such an unhappy expression? He couldn't help but think.

But perhaps it wasn't his fault, as male Indeedee naturally have downturned mouths, making their expressions seem less happy.

"So, what do you think?" Old Man Sato asked with a smile, seeing the pair of Pokemon finish their discussion, "Would you like to stay here and live with D?"

Upon hearing this, the two Indeedee looked up at D.

D also looked at them, greeting them with a smile:

"It's been a long time, Indeedee. Let me formally introduce myself. I'm D, the owner of this farm, and if you are willing, I warmly welcome you both."

The sister Indeedee was thrilled, "Indeedee~"

The brother Indeedee used a bit of Psychic Power from the horn on his head to sense the other's emotions.

No malice, no disdain, only pure welcome and joy.

D noticed the brother Indeedee's action and turned to look at him.

Their gazes met in mid-air.

A moment later, the brother Indeedee nodded slightly, calmly saying, "Indeedee."

He agreed.

---

---

---

---

# 192: Chapter 189: Indeedee Will Always Remember This Day

"Ho ho ho ho ho!"

Seeing the usually serious and mature Indeedee nod in agreement, Old Man Sato let out a series of chuckles.

The two Indeedees turned to look at him.

Old Man Sato: "Alright, alright, looks like I don't have to worry anymore. In that case, I entrust these two to your care from now on."

D nodded in agreement: "Don't worry, I'll take good care of them."

Old Man Sato spoke to the two Indeedees: "Go on then!"

The siblings exchanged a glance, the sister happily moved to D's side, while the brother silently followed behind her.

Seeing this scene, D smiled, reaching out to pat the sister's head.

"Ai yi～"

As D placed his hand on the brother Indeedee's head, he could clearly feel the Pokémon's body stiffen.

"So downcast, how about a smile?" D said with a smile.

Hearing this, the brother Indeedee was slightly startled, then looked up.

Just now, when the other was introducing the ranch, although he didn't show any reaction on the surface, he was actually listening carefully and using his horn to sense the other's mood.

He felt very proud when talking about the ranch.

He must be proud of how well he has managed such a large ranch, the brother Indeedee thought.

And when discussing the Pokémon on the ranch, he sensed a strong sense of joy.

He really liked these Pokémon, and this liking was purely about liking them, unrelated to their strength or weakness.

In the autumn sunlight, the human's smile was like a gentle breeze, seemingly able to blow into one's heart.

The Indeedee's face showed no expression, it simply looked quietly at the other.

It thought, it would always remember this day.

It and its sister met this human.

A moment later, it responded: "Ai yi..."

The Translation Nuts they had in the morning were all gone, so D couldn't understand what the Indeedee was saying anymore.

But judging from the tone, it didn't seem to be saying something like "I was born not to smile".

Seeing them getting along well, Old Man Sato sighed in relief.

He hadn't misjudged the person!

He took a piece of paper from his suit pocket and handed it over: "This is the phone number for the estate. If you need any help, feel free to call."

D thought he wouldn't have anything needing help, but then thought again, the Indeedees' parents were there, they might miss their parents.

So he reached out and took it: "Alright, thank you very much!"

Old Man Sato smiled and then, leaning on his cane, waved goodbye to the Indeedee siblings with his other hand: "See you later, little ones!"

D and the Indeedees watched as Old Man Sato got in a car, watching the black luxury car drive away into the distance.

D originally thought the Indeedees might be downcast and in need of some comforting words.

But when he turned his head, he saw the sister Indeedee looking at him with bright eyes, and the brother Indeedee also looking up at him.

There was no sign of reluctance or sadness at all.

The words D wanted to say were stuck in his mouth, he was stunned for a moment, and then laughed: "I thought you guys would be sad and need some comfort."

"Ai yi～"

"Ai yi..."

The sister Indeedee showed a smile, while the brother Indeedee sighed a bit helplessly.

Things like this... how do you say it?

They had known from a young age that they would have a new human family someday, so from way back, they had always been anticipating it.

D ate a piece of Translation Nut and chatted with the Indeedees on this topic for a while.

He roughly understood their meaning,

Just like those Pokémon the Pokémon League prepares for new Trainers, they also look forward to their Trainer, imagining what they would be like.

And as for D's ability to understand Pokémon speak, the two Indeedees were very surprised.

The brother Indeedee showed a flustered expression for the first time, starting to recall if he had said anything he shouldn't have.

It seemed like he only mentioned not giving too much affection to humans since it was the first meeting…

The brother Indeedee asked anxiously: "Ai yi?" (Did you just hear us talking?)

He was afraid the other might dislike him for it.

D blinked, correcting: "Don't use 'you', that's a formal term, we are equals."

The sister Indeedee: "Ai yi?" (But that's how mom and dad taught us? They said the humans who take care of us would be our new master, and we should help him well.)

D listened patiently to their words, organized his thoughts a bit, and then replied:

"Instead of 'master', I think 'family' better describes the relationship between us. Just like a normal human family, emotions are mutual, you help me with chores, and in return, I'll take care of you."

"Ai yi?" (Is that so?) The sister Indeedee looked puzzled.

"Yes." D then looked at the brother waiting for an answer and asked: "Do you think I'm someone who would abandon a Pokémon just because they're weak?"

The brother Indeedee was stunned for a moment and then honestly shook his head.

From his observation, it didn't seem like it.

D smiled: "Isn't that good then?"

Saying that, he caught a glimpse of Pawmo, Glaceon, Cyclizar, Koraidon, Dragonite, and other Pokémon sneakily watching from a corner.

D waved them over.

"This Dragonite was clumsy before, only learning a few moves as it grew this big, while other Dragonites its age usually could learn many moves by this time," D said, "It should be considered weak, right?"

Dragonite: "..." However, it's not that weak.

Upon hearing this, the two Indeedees looked at the chubby Dragonite.

D continued: "But later, after coming to this ranch, it gradually grew stronger and has now learned many, many moves."

"Roar!" Dragonite looked proud.

"And this Pawmo." D looked at the Pawmo in a bright red cloak: "It was once a very weak Pawmi, so weak that even a hen could defeat it."

Pawmo nodded continuously and even cooperated with D, telling the Indeedees about its past:

"Pamo Pamo!" (Exactly, back then, I was caught by D when I was stealing food from the ranch. I was really scared then, but D didn't hurt me. He adopted me and let me stay here to work! D is the best human in the world!)

D: "And now, that once-weak Pawmi has transformed into a powerful Pokémon capable of controlling electricity at will and flying using electricity."

"Pamo!" That's right!

Pawmo's eyes were determined, showcasing a superhero-style take-off to the Indeedees.

The clean red cloak behind it flapped in the wind, thanks to D.

Fly, it's flying?!

The two Indeedees widened their eyes in disbelief.

They exchanged glances, roughly understanding what D was trying to convey.

D smiled, his gaze sweeping over the other Pokémon, reaching out to poke the large nose of the approaching Dratini:

"If you want to know more, you can talk to them later."

This way, the two Indeedees could quickly integrate into the ranch.

---

---

---

---

# 193: Chapter 190: Household Helper (Double Chapters in One)

"Let's go inside first!"

D let the other Pokemons keep playing, while he prepared to familiarize himself with the environment along with two Indeeedee.

The three of them crossed the lawn and arrived at the newly built house.

The two Indeeedee looked up at the house in front of them.

They were smart, recognizing immediately that this was where they'd be living from now on.

Compared to the estate villa, this house was much smaller, with two floors and a veranda-like entrance.

Above, the extended eaves just happened to cover the walkway, preventing rain from falling in.

The entire corridor was bright and spacious, with a string of wind chime bells hanging above, that would make a pleasant tinkling sound when the wind blew.

On the other side of the door hung a square box made of wood. Despite the extensive cognition training the two Indeeedee received before, they still couldn't recognize what it was.

"Aii?" the sister Indeedee asked curiously.

D followed her gaze and explained with a smile, "That's Butterfree's nest, I made it with wooden boards and nails, inside are leaves, grass, and petals."

Every evening, Butterfree would fly in and sleep inside.

The two Indeeedee nodded, indicating that they understood.

Seeing this, D pushed open the door and led them into the living room.

"This is our home," he said.

The siblings immediately looked around.

The first thing they saw was a neat and bright living room.

The living room was simply yet warmly furnished, with a soft sofa, a round wooden coffee table, a television opposite the sofa, and a green potted plant nearby.

What was most captivating, however, was the display shelf in the living room.

D introduced proactively, "Those are things Teddiursa sent back, it has the Pickup ability, Pokémon with this trait have a collecting habit, they like bringing various things home to keep."

"Aii?" Teddiursa? It seemed they hadn't seen it.

D: "Teddiursa is traveling outside now, you won't see it for a while."

Upon hearing this, the two Indeeedee looked surprised.

The brother Indeedee, who had silently followed without speaking, uncertainly asked, "Aii?" (Without a Trainer?)

"No," D shook his head, "It's a fortunate Teddiursa, not traveling alone, there's a Mew with it, Mew is a legendary Pokémon, very powerful."

"Aii?!" the sister Indeedee's eyes brightened, could they see them?

D laughed, "Perhaps, when they decide to come back, you will be able to see them."

The brother Indeedee was quite surprised, "Aii...?" (But... aren't you its Trainer?)

It was the first time it had seen a Pokémon leave a Trainer and travel alone.

D: "Trainer? Not really, Teddiursa is special, it can do well even without a Trainer."

Upon hearing this, the siblings exchanged glances.

The sister Indeedee communicated silently with her brother.

"Aii..." (Brother, don't you feel this ranch is strange...)

The brother Indeedee's eyes flickered, but he didn't speak.

Indeed very strange, D was strange, the Pokémon were strange... completely different from the Trainers and Pokémon they knew.

D pretended not to notice their thoughts and began to introduce the remaining places.

"This is where I usually read and rest, Pokémon from the ranch play here as well."

Hearing this, the two Indeeedee looked over.

They saw a brand new fireplace installed on the wall to D's right, with no dust inside, appearing unused.

In front of the fireplace was a rocking chair, with a round rug beneath it, scattered with books and toys.

There were round balls, a fluffy Skwovet plush, and a few red-and-white Pokeballs.

The two Indeeedee recognized the fireplace.

Seeing this scene, they could almost imagine the owner of the house sitting in the rocking chair in the winter, gently swaying in the firelight of the fireplace, leisurely flipping through a book.

D explained, "The carpet in front of the fireplace is where Cyclizar sleeps, it's quite afraid of the cold."

The area in front of the fireplace was indeed very warm, the two Indeeedee thought.

"This is the kitchen, I usually prepare food for the ranch Pokémon here."

The kitchen didn't require much introduction, so he mentioned it briefly before turning and opening the door to the basement:

"This is the cellar, used for wine-making in the ranch."

The sister Indeedee craned her neck to peek into the cellar's environment, and to help them see clearly, D kindly turned on the lights.

After a moment, the two Indeeedee withdrew their gaze, "Aii," indicating they remembered.

D smiled and continued, "The hallway leads to the master bedroom, where I usually sleep, the small room further in the kitchen is the Cream Workshop, I work there with Alcremie on new desserts when I have time, by the way, do you like sweets?"

"Aii~" the sister Indeedee nodded happily.

The brother Indeedee hesitated, then gave a barely noticeable nod.

---

Seeing this, D took out two packs of honey gummies from the fridge and gave them to them, then started going upstairs.

"Next is the second floor, your rooms are also on the second floor."

Saying this, they came to the small living room on the second floor.

D opened a door and revealed the room inside, filled with a child-like atmosphere.

"Since I don't know what you like, I decorated the room in a human way." He said.

As for the living space, the two Indeedees had no particular demands.

They initially thought they'd have to live in a Poke Ball, so having their own room was a delightful surprise.

D continued, "The other rooms are the study and the storage room. Oh, by the way, there's a video phone in the living room on the first floor. If you miss your parents, you can use that video phone to contact them."

The siblings were naturally overjoyed at this.

After the introduction, D glanced at the time and realized it was unknowingly already 10:30 in the morning.

He thought he'd prepare a lavish lunch today to welcome the two Indeedees.

But since the fridge was nearly empty, D planned to ride Koraidon to the town to buy some supplies.

He asked Cyclizar to take care of the two Indeedees, then left the ranch.

He quickly finished buying the necessary ingredients and, on his way back to the ranch, started thinking about the two Indeedees.

The two Indeedees were still young, not even three months old, truly the youngest Pokemon on the ranch.

D originally thought the two Indeedees would need his care for a while, but upon returning to the ranch, he was surprised to find the house tidied up by them.

The dust on the wooden floor had been swept clean, and every plank was wiped as clean as new.

The dirty clothes in the bathroom had also been washed clean, with the brother Indeedees silently hanging them up on the clothesline outside.

Not only that, D noticed the trash in the garbage bin had also been emptied.

The sister Indeedees even brought him a freshly brewed cup of tea.

"Thank you," D was stunned for a moment and reached out to take the tea. He happened to be a bit thirsty and drank it all in one gulp.

Hmm, the sister Indeedees' tea brewing skills were quite impressive!

D was even more surprised, eyes wide, as he watched the brother Indeedees finish hanging clothes and the sister Indeedees brewing tea.

"You know how to do housework?" D asked in astonishment.

"Iya~" the sister Indeedees nodded gently, with a very gentle smile on her face.

D patted his pockets, but couldn't find anything; he then remembered he'd eaten all the sunflower seed crisps he usually carried.

Seeing his actions, Koraidon seemed to understand D's thoughts.

It dashed back into the room and took a piece of sunflower seed crisp from D's snack stash to give to him.

D blinked and looked at the proud face of Koraidon, unable to hold back a laugh.

He patted the big creature's head, then ate the sunflower seed crisp.

The sister Indeedees repeated, "Iya~" (Before coming here, Mom and Dad specifically taught us to do housework~).

Whether it's sweeping, mopping, washing clothes, taking out garbage, hanging clothes, washing dishes, or brewing tea, they excel at it!

No wonder they came from a big estate!

D nodded in agreement. From the looks of it, the two Indeedees didn't seem to need much looking after.

He put the purchased ingredients in the kitchen, and his peripheral vision suddenly noticed the garbage bin in the corner.

D hurriedly asked, "Where did you throw the garbage?"

The brother Indeedees pointed to the trash bin on the road outside the ranch, "Iya."

Seeing this, D breathed a sigh of relief; at least it wasn't thrown in the grass or a nook.

Then, a sense of curiosity flashed in his eyes.

"But speaking of which, how did you know I like this kind of tea?"

Hearing this, the sister Indeedees turned to look at Cyclizar.

The brother Indeedees also looked at Cyclizar.

Clearly, both the type of tea and the location of the garbage bin were things Cyclizar told them.

"No wonder." That made sense now, "Why don't you rest for a while? I'll cook for you."

Seeing this, the sister Indeedees stepped forward, "Iya?"

D raised an eyebrow: "You know how to cook too?"

The sister Indeedees nodded, "Iya!" (Before coming here, both my brother and I learned, although my brother is clumsy and almost blew up the kitchen several times.)

Brother Indeedees: !!!

He immediately cast a disapproving look at his sister.

Such things should not be said out loud!

Seeing the brother Indeedees' embarrassed look, D couldn't help but laugh.

The brother Indeedees turned his head away, lowering his head.

Realizing she had sold out her brother, the sister Indeedees quickly covered her mouth but then realized it was already too late, so she hurriedly tried to cover for her brother:

"Iya, Iya!" (My brother and I specialize in different tasks. I'm good at cooking, brewing tea, taking care of the master and Pokemon, while my brother excels in housekeeping, being a butler, mowing the lawn, and managing the master's daily schedule, keeping everything in order!)

"Oh, I see." D nodded cooperatively, praising them, "You're both amazing!"

Indeed, if the two Indeedees collaborated, they could significantly reduce his workload.

---

The chores will be handled by them, and the Pokémon on the ranch can be left to them for feeding. As the rancher, he only needs to lie back and act as a tool for making magical cuisine.

This way, when winter comes and the fields lie fallow, he can entrust the ranch's affairs to the two Indeedees.

Then, he can confidently head to the Galar Region in search of Calyrex and its two steeds.

There's also the mines in Zapapico Town that are worth a visit.

They are rich in various kinds of stones there.

Although he's not Steven, as a former Pokémon player, he's very interested in collecting items like Evolution Stones and rare minerals.

After all, with Evolution Stones, he can be one step closer to his dream of collecting a full set of Eevee evolutions.

Besides that, there's Kitakami.

However, regarding this place, D has only heard of it and doesn't know much more, because when he crossed over, the DLC hadn't been released yet.

"If there's time later, I should go visit that place," D thought.

It's said that the pastoral scenery there is very beautiful, rich in apples, and various celebrations are held, creating a lively atmosphere.

D prepared lunch while imagining future life.

Today's lunch was completed by him and his sister Indeedee.

His sister Indeedee's cooking skills are quite good; half of today's dishes were prepared by her, one could say her culinary skills are maxed out.

A group of Pokémon gathered around the dining table, beside the large floor-to-ceiling window, through which the beautiful pastoral scenery outside could be seen.

On the dining table were all sorts of delicacies.

Pan-fried steak, sandwiches, corn stew ribs, omelet rolls, vegetable medley, crispy bass, pumpkin cream soup, Berry Platter.

Drinks and desserts included: Lucky Pudding, strawberry juice, Moo Moo Milk, honey gummies, baked cookies.

D's eyes swept over these foods, noticing many new dishes prepared by his sister Indeedee.

Like the vegetable medley, crispy bass, omelet rolls, and pumpkin cream soup.

These dishes inspired D greatly; he had felt he was in a bottleneck period for a while, having not created a new recipe in a long time.

He decided to have Indeedee teach him later.

It's worth mentioning that the Berry Platter, prepared by his sister Indeedee, doesn't have the effect of Skill Refinement.

Yet even so, a few Pokémon who had previously experienced the terrifying power of the Berry Platter were quite reluctant to eat it.

They didn't want their hard-learned moves to be forgotten, even though they could relearn them, the repeated practice was exhausting.

Seeing this, D held back his laughter.

Sister Indeedee looked utterly confused.

She gazed at the group of Pokémon in front of her, uttering a puzzled growl, "Indeedee?"

"Gaaoo..."

"Awwooo..."

Cyclizar and Dragonite explained the reason to sister Indeedee in a chorus.

Sister Indeedee was quite surprised, "Indeedee?!"

Is there really such a thing in this world?

The siblings looked at D.

"You can think of this power as another form of Psychic," D said casually. "Don't be too surprised, you'll get used to it over time."

Thinking about the effect of the Berry Platter, D couldn't help but feel quite regretful.

Why doesn't his ranch have a gambling Pokémon?

The kind that won't stop until it eats itself into realizing Divine Techniques.

"What a pity."

---
