---

# 194: Chapter 191: Grateful Hearts

After lunch, D began arranging specific duties for the two Indeedee.

As the sister Indeedee had mentioned, the brother Indeedee excelled at housekeeping, managing the owner's daily life, and organizing the household, so D entrusted the management of the entire house to him.

The sister Indeedee, on the other hand, was skilled at cooking and taking care of Pokemon, so D assigned her to look after the many Pokemon on the ranch.

Of course, this didn't mean that everything would be left to the Indeedee; he would also cooperate and work together with them.

The work of the sister Indeedee was divided into the following two tasks:

1. Feed the Pokemon on the ranch when he is not there.

2. Utilize her magical ability to sense emotions to take care of every Pokemon on the ranch, promptly understanding their needs and troubles, and conveying these to him.

When D explained these tasks to the two Indeedee, they exchanged a glance and promptly nodded.

It sounded quite relaxing!

Moreover, these tasks were inherently their responsibilities.

Seeing their expressions, D suddenly remembered the unique trait of the Indeedee species.

Unlike Cyclizar who need to obtain energy through meat.

The source of energy for Indeedee is various positive emotions, happiness, gratitude, joy...

They use their horns to receive these emotions, converting them into their own energy.

Precisely because of this characteristic, whether domestic or wild Indeedee, they diligently care for humans and Pokemon, seeking joyous and grateful emotions.

Speaking of care, D suddenly recalled something.

He looked at the two Indeedee and said, "Oh, I haven't introduced you to the Pokemon Tavern yet."

"Indeed?"

The first time she heard this term, the sister Indeedee had a confused expression on her face.

The brother Indeedee beside her was also somewhat puzzled, looking at D and waiting silently for an answer.

"As the name suggests, it's a place for drinking and gatherings." With that, D turned around: "Come, let me show you!"

The group crossed the lawn and came to the old house that had been transformed into a tavern.

D pushed open the door and stepped aside so the two Indeedee could clearly see the interior arrangement.

"This is the tavern, though it's not open for business at the moment." Seeing their bewildered expressions, D thought for a moment and asked, "You know what a café is, right?"

The brother Indeedee nodded: "Indeed."

D: "A tavern is similar to a café, both are places for relaxation and gatherings."

With his explanation, the two Pokemon immediately understood.

"However, my tavern is not quite the same as ordinary taverns." D's tone was mysteriously secretive.

The siblings were unsurprisingly intrigued and turned their heads to look.

D did not keep them in suspense: "My tavern does not serve humans, only Pokemon guests."

"Indeed?!" Hearing this, the two Indeedee widened their eyes in disbelief.

Serving only Pokemon... guests?

"That's right." D explained the current situation of the tavern in detail.

After listening, the sister Indeedee's eyes became increasingly bright.

She liked this place!

So when the tavern opened at seven o'clock in the evening.

After dinner, the two Machamp who swaggered over for a drink at the tavern saw the owner behind the bar welcoming guests had changed from D to a never-before-seen Pokemon.

The sister Indeedee cheerfully greeted the two guests: "Indeed~"

"Huh...?"

The Machamp were puzzled and couldn't help but ask where D had gone.

The sister Indeedee: "Indeed!" (D is doing housework with his brother! I'll be hosting you today!)

Upon hearing this, the two Machamp were relieved; they were initially worried D had run away and the place was closing down.

Seeing D was fine, they ordered two Berry Juices as usual and sat down to chat.

Before long, Quaquaval and Growlithe also arrived.

Seeing the change of the boss, they were as surprised as the Machamp.

Indeedee patiently explained to them and prepared two drinks according to their needs.

During this time, the sister Indeedee noticed that Growlithe seemed to dislike the sweet and sour flavor of Berry Juice. She thought for a moment and then served a Berry Juice made from Cheri Berry.

This Berry has a spicy taste, which is very popular with Fire Type Pokemon.

Just as expected, Growlithe was pleasantly surprised to find that he preferred Cheri Berry Juice over regular Berry Juice!

"Woof woof!"

The Growlithe, finally having found something to its taste, excitedly expressed its gratitude to the Indeedee.

"Ah-yee~" The sister Indeedee had a gentle smile on her face.

Meanwhile, in the room.

D noticed the brother Indeedee's absent-minded look and said, "If you're worried, just go and check on her! I'll handle the rest of the laundry."

"Ah-yee..." The brother Indeedee was a bit hesitant.

Before coming here, they were taught by their parents to always work seriously for their master, without being careless, so he always strictly demanded that of himself.

But this was the first time he had been apart from his sister...

The brother Indeedee thought of his playful little sister and couldn't help but worry.

After all, she's just a three-month-old little Pokemon. D waved his hand with a smile:

"Relax, don't be so tense, if you want to go, just go. You can also see if the tavern needs any help."

The brother Indeedee nodded his head gratefully, then turned and went downstairs. He jogged under the night sky to the brightly lit tavern.

His sister was safely standing behind the bar counter. Though she seemed to be in work mode, her attention was completely caught up by the chatting Pokemon.

The brother Indeedee breathed a sigh of relief, then calmly walked in.

"Ah-yee?" Seeing her brother arrive, the sister Indeedee was slightly stunned but then broke into a bright smile.

"Ah-yee!" she said, Brother, I like it here!

The brother Indeedee had never seen his sister so radiant.

He was taken aback for a moment, then stepped forward to help his sister with her tasks.

Late at night, after all the Pokemon guests had left, the brother Indeedee cleaned up the dishes and washed them with clear water.

The sister Indeedee, meanwhile, was briskly sweeping the tavern floor with a broom.

Tonight, the smile rarely left her face.

It seemed she had discovered the meaning of her existence.

"Ah-yee~" (Brother, this place is wonderful, those Pokemon are grateful to us~)

The brother Indeedee replied without turning his head: "Ah-yee." (D is a good person.)

The sister nodded, adding, "Ah-yee!" (The best human in the world!)

Just then, a beam of light shone from the door.

D, who had been waiting for the two Pokemon, appeared at the door with a flashlight.

"It's time to go back to sleep now!" D said somewhat helplessly, as it was nearly midnight, way past normal closing time.

The sister Indeedee looked a bit embarrassed: "Ah-yee..."

She had been chatting happily with the Machamp and Quaquaval and had lost track of time.

The two Pokemon quickly finished cleaning up the tavern, carefully closed the door, and followed D back to their dwelling.

D pointed to the fridge: "There's food and water in the fridge, if you're hungry or thirsty you can help yourselves, no need to ask me, just treat this place like home."

"Ah-yee~" The sister Indeedee nodded happily.

"Well then, good night." D watched them return to their room and then turned back to his bedroom to rest.

With the bedroom lights off, the whole ranch fell into silence.

Only the soft whispers of the two Indeedees could be heard.

The sister Indeedee cuddled up in her little bed, thinking about everything that had happened that day, too excited to sleep.

The brother Indeedee lay flat on the other bed, his sleeping posture serious and rigid. He lay there with his eyes closed, seemingly asleep.

But the sister Indeedee knew her brother was not asleep.

In a charming tone, she said: "Ah-yee~" (Brother, I'm so happy today~)

The brother Indeedee responded: "Ah-yee." (Time to sleep.)

The sister Indeedee was curious: "Ah-yee?" (Aren't you happy?)

The brother opened his eyes and answered earnestly: "…Ah-yee." (I am happy.)

Only then was the sister Indeedee satisfied, as she cuddled the Substitute dinosaur plush D had prepared for them, and softly murmured:

"Ah-yee!" (I wish I could live here forever!)

Hearing this, the brother Indeedee again thought of the Meowth in the manor who had been abandoned by the Trainer for being too weak.

He stared at the dark ceiling.

D... surely wouldn't abandon them, would he?

---

---

---

---

# 195: Chapter 192: Pawmo's Cape

The morning sunlight covered the entire ranch, and a few blue-feathered Squawkabilly leisurely perched on the rooftop.

Suddenly, several yellow-feathered Squawkabilly flew over from a short distance away.

The group of blue-feathered Squawkabilly immediately acted as if facing a great enemy, flapping their wings and cawing as they went to confront the yellow-feathered ones, beginning a territorial dispute.

Amidst the cries of distress, the yellow-feathered Squawkabilly were defeated and flew away in a very dejected manner.

Meanwhile, the victorious blue-feathered ones landed proudly back on the rooftop.

Hmph, this turf was theirs!

D was woken up by the chirping of the Squawkabilly.

He resignedly sat up in bed and weakly held his head.

Ugh! Those Squawkabilly are so annoying!

Not only do they make noise every morning and evening, but they also steal his crops and fly around in swarms, scaring the timid Combee so they don't dare leave their nest.

Previously, out of consideration that "Pokemon are intelligent creatures," he hadn't driven them away.

Now, it seemed that he had no choice but to chase them off!

So, early in the morning, D grabbed a stick and rode Dragonite to the rooftop.

The Translation Nuts he ate yesterday weren't expired, and he happened to hear the blue-feathered Squawkabilly saying nonsense like, "This turf is ours!"

D sneered and said to them, "This turf is mine! Dragonite, chase them all away!"

"Roar!"

Dragonite opened its mouth wide and let out a roar.

The startled Squawkabilly realized that they were no match for this Dragonite and flew away chirping, leaving a mess of blue feathers behind.

"Whew—"

Seeing the Squawkabilly fly far away, D finally breathed a long sigh of relief.

He tossed the stick aside and went back downstairs.

The two Indeedee had already woken up, with the brother Indeedee going to D's room to tidy up the bed while the sister Indeedee began cooking breakfast.

"So thoughtful!"

D rarely indulged in laziness, and while waiting for breakfast to be ready, he picked up Pawmo and called the Cyclizar, who wanted to grow stronger, preparing to have a healthy morning run around the ranch.

Dragonite, on the other hand, shouldered a small satchel to deliver fresh goat milk to Mrs. Dantes and Elo.

Koraidon yawned, lazily lying on the grassy terrain, watching them run past him over and over again.

While running, a strong white glow suddenly emanated from the small Pawmo.

"Pawmo?!"

Startled, Pawmo quickly stopped, carefully feeling the changes in its body.

Seeing this, D wasn't surprised; instead, he felt a sense of "finally."

Pawmot, be born!

D's heart surged with excitement.

The white glow engulfed Pawmo's body, rapidly changing its appearance within the light.

It grew taller and bigger, its fur becoming even fluffier, its size increasing from half a meter to nearly a meter.

When the light receded, the large Pawmot let out an excited cry, then leaped into D's arms.

D caught it steadily, unable to resist rubbing his cheek against its face.

"Aw, it got even cuter!"

"Pawmo～"

Pawmot let out a charming cry.

Beside them, Cyclizar stared blankly at this scene, feeling genuinely happy for Pawmot in its heart.

Not far away, Koraidon tilted its head.

It never noticed before, but now it suddenly realized that D seemed to be letting go more and more.

Watching D bury his face in Pawmot's belly, Koraidon felt a sense of envy.

It looked down at its own belly, thinking it also wanted…

With the morning run over, Dragonite returned to the ranch from Porto Marinada Town.

The sister Indeedee gently placed the breakfast on the table.

D sat around the table with a group of Pokemon, feeling very joyful: "Let's start!"

"Screech!"

"Roar!"

"Pawmo～"

"..."

After breakfast, D planned to take the sister Indeedee to milk and feed the Gogoat and Miltank.

However, before he could leave, the evolved Pawmot came over holding the red cloak, which had clearly become too small.

Its expression was full of grievance and sadness, eyes welled with tears as it looked at D: "Pawmo..."

D glanced down.

The evolved Pawmot had grown larger, and the original cloak was now too small for it to wear.

Seeing this, D thought for a moment and said, "Don't worry, I'll get you a new one from town later."

But Pawmot still wasn't too happy.

Because that red cloak held significant meaning for it.

It looked down at the cloak in its hand, memories of D scrubbing the cloak clean when it got dirty flooding its mind.

Pawmot was on the verge of tears, regretting its evolution.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but find it amusing yet endearing.

He patted Pawmot's head: "Isn't it the same? Later, I'll take you to town and get a custom one, and you can keep this one as a keepsake."

Only then did Pawmot feel slightly better.

Hearing that D would take it to town later, it obediently sat on the corridor under the eaves waiting, listening to the wind chime above while watching D and the two Indeedee milk and feed the Gogoat.

"...The grassy seed in this basket is Gogoat's main food. Just pour them into the feeding trough every day, and Gogoat will eat them on its own."

D demonstrated to the two Indeedee.

Watching his actions, the siblings nodded, indicating they understood.

D smiled and moved to the three Gogoat that had lost all their PP value while cultivating the World Tree:

"Come, say hello to our new partners, Indeedee. The one on the left is the brother, and the one on the right is the sister."

Hearing this, the three Gogoat turned to look at the two Indeedee.

They obediently let out a weak "baa" to the siblings.

D patted their heads, watching as the grassy seeds were prepared, and the three Gogoat got up to squeeze in front of the trough to gobble up the grassy seeds and replenish the grass energy in their bodies.

Once replenished, the Gogoat leisurely wandered to the field's edge, helping care for the crops and the orchard's berry trees using grassy terrain.

Seeing this, D planned to introduce the underground-dwelling Diglett and Naclstack to the two Indeedee.

But before he could enter the cavern, a Dragonite suddenly flew across the sky.

D was a bit surprised.

Could Teddiursa and Mew have sent him another postcard?

D thought it was quite extravagant this time, even sending a Quasi-God to deliver the postcard.

So he stopped and watched as the Dragonite landed in the ranch.

Yet the next second, D sensed something was off.

Because he realized that this Dragonite looked very familiar!

It was one of the Dragonites that wanted to come home with him when he first visited Dragon Island, but was stopped by Dragonite!

The actions of Dragonite confirmed this.

Witnessing the wild Dragonite land in the ranch, the lazing Dragonite instantly bounced up.

"Roar?!"

The wild Dragonite cheerfully replied, "Roar... Roar～"

The Translation Nuts were exhausted, and D couldn't understand their conversation.

Upon hearing the wild Dragonite, Dragonite cast its gaze towards the lake where Dratini and Dragonair lived.

A thought crossed its mind, and D stepped forward to ask, "Is it here to take Dratini and Dragonair back to the island?"

"Roar!"

Dragonite nodded, eyes gleaming, instantly changing its attitude toward the wild Dragonite.

It appeared very pleased, even affectionately leading the wild Dragonite towards the lake with its chubby paws.

The expression seemed eager to pack up the Dragonair and Dratini and send them off immediately.

D: "..."

Buddy, everyone's aware of your intentions!

---

---

---

---

# 196: Chapter 193: Twice as Reluctant to Leave

The arrival of the wild Dragonite disrupted D's plans.

Led by Dragonite, the wild Dragonite finally met the five stowaway Dratini and Dragonair in the lake at the ranch.

Seeing them, the wild Dragonite began to chastise them with its hands on its hips: "Roar, roar!"

The Dratini and Dragonair huddled together, trembling and responding pitifully.

Dragonite stood to the side, with a mischievous look as if enjoying the spectacle.

"Indeedee?"

The little sister Indeedee watched the scene with curiosity in her eyes.

D explained, "Those three Dratini and two Dragonair are guests here at the ranch. They live on an isolated island in the ocean."

Regarding this matter, Dragonite should have already informed the other Dragonites on Dragon Island, saying they would play for a while at the ranch before going back.

But those five little ones forgot the date while playing, so Dragon Island simply sent a Dragonite over to bring them back.

Seeing that negotiations over there would take a while, D took the opportunity to bring the two Indeedees to the underground cave, where he introduced them to five Naclstack and Diglett.

The cave was cool, with an underground lake connecting to underground rivers. Water droplets slowly dripped from the stalactites above, creating a mystical ticking sound as they hit the water's surface.

The five Naclstack and small Diglett obediently gathered in front of D, curiously looking past him at the two Indeedees.

D said, "These two will be living with us from now on. When I go out for work, they'll take care of you."

The little sister Indeedee waved her small hand kindly: "Indeedee~"

The big brother Indeedee nodded lightly: "Indeedee."

Naclstack and Diglett all turned to look.

D smiled and said, "The one on the left is the brother; the one on the right is the sister. They're siblings, and by the way, the sister's cooking is very delicious, even better than my cooking!"

"Squeak?!"

Hearing this, the food-loving Diglett's eyes lit up.

It thought D's cooking was already the best on earth, not expecting someone to outshine him.

The Naclstack were also very curious about Indeedee's culinary skills.

Seeing how well-received her cooking was, the little sister Indeedee was extremely pleased and couldn't wait to prepare some signature dishes for them to taste.

D, of course, showed his support: "I'll leave today's lunch for them to you, and later I'll tell you their preferred tastes and foods."

"Indeedee~" The little sister Indeedee nodded happily.

After the work was done, D took them back to the surface.

The big brother Indeedee returned home to clean up the room that had been messed up by Cyclizar and Pawmo.

The little sister Indeedee slowly walked around the ranch, making friends with Glaceon, Alcremie, and other Pokémon.

Her nature was already gentle and caring, and coupled with her knack for Pokémon care, she quickly made friends with the group of Pokémon.

When D turned his head, he saw Alcremie already perched on Indeedee's head.

Even Glaceon was influenced, showing a faint smile and quietly staying beside Indeedee.

The wild Dragonite by the lakeside continued to communicate with the Dratini and Dragonair.

D had Koraidon fetch a bag of Translation Nuts Cake, ate it, and then walked forward.

The wild Dragonite standing by the lake kept muttering, while three Dratini and Dragonair shrank below the water's surface, leaving only their heads visible, pitifully gazing at the wild Dragonite.

Meanwhile, Dragonite cheerfully watched the scene in front of it.

"What's the situation now?" D raised an eyebrow and asked.

Seeing him approach, the Dratini and Dragonair, as if seeing a savior, zoomed out of the lake and hid behind him.

"Woo!" (Don't want to go back!)

"Woo..." (Please, let us stay a few more days...)

The wild Dragonite, with a stern expression, said, "Roar!" (No, you've been playing for a long time! It's time to go back!)

The Dratini and Dragonair trembled in fright at its tone.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but say, "Is it really urgent? Just let them stay here a few more days."

After all, there isn't much to do back on Dragon Island, other than basking in the sun at the central lake or catching fish in the sea.

Facing D, the wild Dragonite lost its temper, its tone even sounding a bit aggrieved: "Roar..." (But they've been playing for so long...)

Dragonite widened its eyes.

D: "..."

I suppose that's true.

He thought for a moment, considering Dragonite had come from afar, it wouldn't be right to let it leave directly without showing some hospitality—he certainly should invite it for a meal.

So D said, "Then let's leave in the afternoon. Stay and have a meal with us, shall we?"

A meal?

The wild Dragonite instantly remembered the food D had brought to the island before.

It was immediately attracted, forgetting about taking the Dratini and Dragonair back to the island, and nodded repeatedly: "Roar!"

That works!

Dragonite: "!!!"

"It's settled then," D said cheerfully. "You all play here for a bit, and I'll go out to buy some ingredients. Dragonite, help me take care of our guest."

Dragonite, feeling a bit sulky, but thinking about how the wild Dragonite would take these five pesky little dragons away in the afternoon, decided to endure!

Dragonite agreed.

The Dratini and Dragonair then breathed a sigh of relief.

---

At least, they could still play here until the afternoon.

D wanted to ride Cyclizar to the town, but when he looked around, he saw Cyclizar playing with the Indeedees.

D didn't want to disturb them, so he found Koraidon, called Pawmot, and headed to Porto Marinada Town together.

They had agreed to go and get a custom cloak made for Pawmot.

*

Todays lunch was prepared by D and his sister, Indeedees, together.

Looking at the sumptuous food in front of him, the wild Dragonite showed an expression like a country dragon.

Dragonite: "..." Can't bear to watch!

It turned its head, silently eating a sandwich, deciding to endure it, after all, this Dragonite would leave in the afternoon.

However, the imagination was beautiful, but the reality was rather stark.

Until the sun set and nightfall descended, the wild Dragonite didn't mention returning to Dragon Island with Dratini and Dragonair.

Not only that, it enthusiastically joined in the fun with the Pokemon on the ranch.

Since D had previously taken the Cyclizar and Pawmo to Dragon Island, the wild Dragonite was not unfamiliar with these Pokemon.

D, Dragonite, and Dratini, Dragonair stood not far away watching this scene, with different expressions on their faces.

D felt very proud, thinking to himself that as expected of him! No Pokemon could resist the lure of delicious food! Look at this wild Dragonite, it's practically lost in joy!

Dragonite was full of questions, wasn't it supposed to leave in the afternoon? Now it was dark!

Dratini and Dragonair both sighed in relief in unison, they stepped back wanting to hide so they wouldn't be affected.

At this moment, D's sister Indeedees had prepared the dinner.

She nodded at D, and D called out to all the Pokemon to come back for dinner: "Dinner time!"

The wild Dragonite came running immediately.

By the table, upon seeing the low-spirited Dragonite, it couldn't help but be a little puzzled: "Roar?" (What's wrong?)

Dragonite reminded: "Roar?" (Did you forget something?)

The wild Dragonite blinked, did it?

It really seemed to have forgotten something important, but couldn't recall... oh well, nevermind!

The wild Dragonite happily indulged in the ocean of delicacies.

Dragonite: "!!!"

It wanted to say more but was held back by D.

D whispered: "Just let it stay here and play for a few more days! Anyway, there's nothing happening back there."

Dragonite feeling aggrieved: "Roar..."

D conspiratorially: "Do you remember I once talked to you about forming a Dragonite army, to let you lead a large group of Dragonites?"

Dragonite paused a bit, recalled, and then nodded.

Seems like there really was such a thing...

D: "So, now by making good relations with them through food, we can hire them to come help us in the future, and then I will appoint you as the head of Dragonite Delivery Company, and we will be one family."

We will be one family...

This sentence truly hit Dragonite right in the feels.

It opened its big eyes and fondly reminisced.

The Pokemons treated as family by D all seemed to have their own Poke Ball.

It had one too, but neither Dratini, Dragonair, nor this wild Dragonite had one.

Thinking of this, Dragonite immediately felt joyful.

They really are a family!

So, it didn't urge for this Dragonite to go back to Dragon Island anymore.

After all, in D's heart, it is the only Dragonite!

In the deep night, the Pokemons on the ranch gradually went to sleep.

The wild Dragonite also contently patted its full belly, lying on the soft grass, and entered a dreamland.

Here, everything was peaceful, but on Dragonite Island, they were bewildered.

The moon reflected on the lake's surface.

The Dragonites by the lake were all perplexed.

Where is the Dragonite we sent to bring Dratini and Dragonair back?

Why hadn't it returned this late? Did something bad happen?

Like getting captured by a human trainer, perhaps...

The Dragonites looked at each other, glanced up at the moon and the sea, thought about what was to come, and the next morning sent another adult Dragonite over.

As a result, they waited and waited, until the sun set and the moon rose, but this Dragonite still didn't come back.

Immediately, a commotion erupted among the Dragonites.

They gathered and murmured for a long time, eventually choosing a Dragonite with a fiery temperament, respected by strength, and disdainful of humans but absolutely wouldn't be captured by humans, to retrieve Dratini and Dragonair.

*

The following day, as daylight shone brightly.

The Dragonite that returned from delivering milk widened its eyes, staring at the two wild Dragonites, one holding Berry Milk in the left hand and the other holding a Honey Bun in the right, clearly indulging in their carefree enjoyment.

Darn! The food at home was nearly devoured by them!

Not just it, D also realized this.

These two Dragonites were like reincarnated hungry ghosts, their mouths hadn't stopped these past two days, besides the three regular meals, they also consumed a large amount of snacks.

The stock in the fridge was all gone!

"No way, we can't let them stay here any longer!"

Just as D prepared to think of a way to send them back to Dragonite Island, another Dragonite landed in the pasture.

Watching this scene, D's eyebrow twitched, and a thought simultaneously emerged in both his and Dragonite's mind: Seriously? Another one?

However, this Dragonite seemed different from the previous two.

It appeared fierce and was very reminiscent of Iris's Dragonite in the anime.

---

Not only that, the behavior of this Dragonite is also very violent.

After landing, it flew to the two foodie Dragonites and raised its paw to give them two hard flicks, making the two Dragonites hold their heads, tears welling up in their eyes.

D widened his eyes, looking at the scene in disbelief.

It seems like there's an unexpected development!

The Dragonite standing next to him looked thrilled: "Awoo!"

D was stunned for a moment: "You know it?"

Dragonite quickly nodded: "Awoo awoo!" (It's the most powerful Dragonite on the island! It hates humans, is incredibly strong, and has a fierce temper!)

Indeed, just as Dragonite said.

After teaching the two foodie Dragonites a lesson, this Dragonite looked down arrogantly, casting a glance at D from top to bottom.

Sensing the unfamiliar presence, Koraidon suddenly opened its eyes, leaping up from the shadows, its strong body standing in front of D, looking warningly at the opposite Dragonite.

Upon seeing Koraidon, the Dragonite's expression became serious.

Just as D thought the two Pokemon were about to fight, the Dragonite suddenly turned its gaze towards the Dratini and Dragonair hiding in the grass, watching secretly.

Dratini and Dragonair started trembling again.

The wild Dragonite roared: "Awoo!" (Return! The evolution ceremony is about to start!)

Hearing this, Dratini and Dragonair seemed to realize something. Though they were reluctant to leave, they obediently flew out of the grass.

They first woefully pressed against D, then went to the side of that wild Dragonite.

D was dumbfounded; he asked puzzled: "What does it mean by evolution?"

Could it be that the two foodie Dragonites came to pick up Dratini and Dragonair for this reason too?

On this matter, Dragonite knew a bit.

It explained to D: "Awoo..." (It's the evolution ceremony for the Dratini and Dragonair living on the island...)

From Dragonite's words, D roughly understood what was happening.

Every year at this time, some natural phenomena appear in the ocean.

For example, whirlpools forming under the sea, or currents flowing towards the moon.

And as Pokemon that are close to nature, they can best sense the existence of these natural phenomena.

So, the Dragonites take advantage of the power of nature and swim in the ocean.

If they successfully overcome the whirlpool's pull, then some Dratini and Dragonair can grow, and some might evolve into Dragonair and Dragonite.

Dragonite had participated before, but it failed. Later, it was only through constant effort and diligent training that it evolved into Dragonite.

D listened, half understanding and half not.

From a Human Trainer's perspective, overcoming the force of the currents in the whirlpool is indeed a good form of special training.

If explained in game terms, it would be like "Dragonite participated in the whirlpool training, gaining a lot of experience."

So, some Dratini and Dragonair are able to take the opportunity to evolve.

Dragonite, reminiscing, said: "Awoo, awoo awoo!" (It's spectacular! Everyone swims in the whirlpool and then flies towards the moon!)

Upon hearing this, a scene suddenly appeared in D's mind.

——A bright full moon hanging high in the night sky, its bright moonlight covering the ocean surface, a whirlpool emerging in the sea, countless Dratini and Dragonair swimming within it.

The Dratini and Dragonair that successfully overcome the whirlpool trial will fly into the sky and, under the moonlit baptism, evolve into stronger Pokemon.

This is nature's miracle!

D felt a stirring in his heart, eyes full of longing: "I want to go see!"

---

---

---

---

# 197: Chapter 194: Moon, Tide, and Dragonites

The quick-tempered Dragonite left with two reluctant Dragonite, three Dratini, and two Dragonair.

Before leaving, the two Dragonite managed to sneak away with a large bag of D's Poffin reserves.

It all started when D saw how reluctant the Dratini and Dragonair were, so he wrapped up a bag of Poffins for each of them as a farewell gift.

As a result, the two wild Dragonite saw it and immediately started acting cute.

D had no choice but to pack two more bags for them.

He originally thought about sending a gift to the quick-tempered Dragonite as well, but it wasn't interested in Poffins and just flew away.

"..." D was speechless, "Seriously!"

Seeing those five troublesome little dragons finally gone, Dragonite felt like the world was brighter.

It sniffed the floral scent wafting in the air and felt like life had never been so wonderful.

Dragonite joyfully tiptoed in a circle on the grass and then eagerly hugged the Cyclizar beside it, sharing its joy at that moment.

Cyclizar, overwhelmed by Dragonite's antics, could only give a helpless look for help to D, "Gah-roar..."

Koraidon watched the scene with a complicated expression, mercilessly bursting Dragonite's bubble of happiness:

"Gya-surr!" (D still has to go to Dragon Island tonight!)

In an instant, Dragonite felt its world turn gray again, and the flowers lost their fragrance.

D found this scene amusing, "Alright, alright, I'm just going over to have a look this time, I promise I won't bring back any more Dragonite."

Dragonite became cautious, "Roar-wu?" (What about the Dratini and Dragonair?)

"Not bringing them either." D thought for a moment, "We don't have enough food storage at home right now; we need to stock up, especially with winter coming. The crops in the field won't grow, so we have to prepare ahead."

Hearing this, Dragonite finally felt relieved.

It thought about it, convinced that the family's food had been eaten by those two wild Dragonite, and patted its chest to promise, "Roar!" (I'll help find food too!)

Dragonite had experienced winter before.

That season is very cold; snow covers the entire island, no Berries can be found, and even the fish in the sea swim to deeper waters to hide.

So some Dragonite would store several Berries in advance and stay in their nests to get through the winter.

"Good child." D smiled, "You don't need to go out finding food for the family just yet."

He pointed to a Berry Garden at the southwest corner of the ranch, "See that garden? In a while, it will be full of big, plump Berries, and with the harvest from the field, you'll have no problem eating through the winter."

D always felt incredibly proud whenever he thought of this.

Look! The once-deserted ranch now transformed under his care!

The fruits are abundant, the grass lush, crops bountiful, and the Pokemon live happily!

Life is complete.

D relaxed and stretched, waving his hand for the surrounding Pokemon to go play on their own, while he turned and headed back.

The Dragonites mentioned their evolution ceremony would begin when the moon rises.

So tonight, D planned to go and watch this special ceremony.

Thinking of the Dragonites evolving under the moonlight, D decided, "Looks like I need to buy a camera."

Such a precious moment cannot just pass unrecorded.

Besides, the photos could be framed and hung on the wall of the tavern to enrich its history.

So that noon, after lunch, D went to the department store in Porto Marinada Town to buy a camera.

In the autumn afternoon, the usually silent older Indeedee placed an exquisite small round table and a chair beside the flower field.

The younger Indeedee kindly brewed a cup of D's favorite red tea for him.

This special tea comes from leaves grown on the antlers of the Sawsbuck. Its fragrance is very strong, and many tea lovers favor it. Whenever there is a tea party, it is indispensable.

Looking at the tea party prepared by the Indeedee siblings, D felt surprised and touched.

He sat at the table under the younger Indeedee's hospitality and took a gentle sip from the fragrant teacup.

"Hmm... your tea-making skills are excellent!" D praised.

The younger Indeedee smiled demurely, "Ai-yi~" (Brother can make tea too~)

"Hmm?" D was instantly intrigued, "Then why haven't I ever seen him do it?"

The younger Indeedee glanced at her brother and found him looking very tense, but he still tried to maintain a stern face, only looking at her disapprovingly.

The younger Indeedee's eyes flickered as if unsure whether to speak or not.

Seeing her expression, D immediately remembered when the younger Indeedee mentioned her brother was clumsy and had blown up the kitchen. He suddenly understood, "Oh, I see!"

The older Indeedee's face showed a hint of embarrassment.

The younger Indeedee quickly waved her little hand, "Ai-yi!" (Brother, it wasn't me who said it!)

The older Indeedee: "..."

"No matter," D laughed heartily, "Everyone has things they're good at and things they're not."

"For example, I'm good at cooking, but if you ask me to sew clothes and dolls, I can't do it."

The older Indeedee's eyes glimmered as it suddenly remembered how while taking care of D's daily tasks and organizing his clothes these past days, it noticed several garments had holes.

---

Is that the reason?

"Did you notice?" D chuckled, "I have to admit, before you all came over, I tried to sew my clothes myself. Want to guess what happened?"

The two Indeedees looked over curiously.

"Not only did I fail to fix my clothes, but I also pricked my hand. In the end, I had no choice but to shove those clothes deep into the closet," D confessed.

Back when he used to farm every day, his clothes and pants would often get worn out, and it was a common occurrence for them to tear.

The brother Indeedee picked up on the meaning in D's words.

The expression on its face didn't look as awkward as before.

D then focused his attention on the group of Pokemon fiddling with a camera not far away.

After he bought the camera, seeing a few Pokemon taking interest in it, he handed it over to them so they could take pictures of things they liked. Later, he planned to make a photo wall at home as a decoration.

The Pokemon were all interested in this, and even Koraidon got involved.

It was even playfully competing with Cyclizar for the right to use the camera.

"It's so lively!" D remarked as he watched the group of Pokemon in a playful tussle, his tone full of emotion.

The sister Indeedee asked, "Hmm?" (But D, aren't you worried they might break the camera?)

Watching them fight over the camera, the sister Indeedee felt a pang of anxiety, fearing that someone might accidentally fling the camera, causing it to shatter on the ground...

D shook his head cheerfully, "It's just a camera. If it breaks, it breaks. As long as they're happy."

Of course, that's assuming it's accidental. If they break it on purpose, that's another story.

Hearing this, the brother and sister Indeedees exchanged a glance and started whispering to each other.

"Hmm…" (Brother, I told you it's a bit strange!)

"Hmm." (D really likes these Pokemon.)

"Hmm?" (Does he like us too?)

The brother Indeedee thought for a moment, "Hmm." (Probably.)

D didn't hear the soft murmurs of the two Pokemon.

He sat beside the flower field, enjoying the autumn blooms while sipping tea leisurely.

The tea party hosted by the two Indeedees today inspired him.

Maybe he could take some cues from it and transform the flower field into a vibrant garden with a set of tea tables and chairs in the center.

In this way, in his free time, he could enjoy the sun and tea, or rest with a book, or play with the Pokemon.

*

In the evening, as the dusk turned yellowish.

After a simple dinner, D planned to head to Dragonite Island to observe the evolution ceremony of Dragonites on the island.

"I'll leave the ranch in your care. The tavern can start business soon, but since today is the weekend, it's closed. You can do whatever you want with your time after, watch TV or play; it's up to you."

Before setting off, D explained the matters to be noted to the two Indeedees.

Upon hearing that the tavern was closed for the weekend, the sister Indeedee looked very disappointed.

Seeing its expression, D laughed, "Who actually enjoys working so much?"

The sister Indeedee: "Hmm…"

"Alright, alright." D sighed, "Do whatever you want; I only have one condition."

Hearing this, the sister Indeedee's eyes lit up immediately, speaking with great joy, "Hmm?"

D smiled, "That is, don't overwork yourself."

The sister Indeedee nodded repeatedly, "Hmm, hmm!"

It's not afraid of hard work! This amount of work is just too easy for them!

Moreover, running the tavern and hosting those Pokemon guests brings a lot of positive vibes to her and her brother.

They love this feeling!

"Alright then." D patted its head, then mounted Koraidon, followed by Dragonite, and began the journey to Dragonite Island.

This time, he only brought Koraidon and Dragonite, leaving the other Pokemon at the ranch.

Because D wasn't sure how long he would spend there tonight; it could be very late, so he decided to leave Pawmo and the others at the ranch to rest.

A group of Pokemon was obedient enough, especially Cyclizar, who was already used to such things.

From early on, D would go out at night for work, leaving it to help guard the ranch.

Back then, there weren't as many Pokemon in the ranch.

D was going out for proper business, and they shouldn't get in his way, Cyclizar thought.

Amid such habits, Cyclizar occasionally harbored a bit of longing in its heart.

——If only it could do more for D like Koraidon...

*

"Roar!"

Flying over the sea under the cover of night, Dragonite once again expressed how round the moon was.

D asked curiously, "Do you like watching the moon?"

He remembered the first time he saw Dragonite, it was sitting there, sadly gazing at the moon.

Dragonite nodded: "Roar." (Every day the moon is different. The round moon is like a plate, the crescent moon looks like a Berry bitten by Koraidon.)

Koraidon: "..."

D raised an eyebrow.

Oh ho, no wonder Dragonite was the one to feel heartache for love!

How did he not notice that his Dragonite has the potential of a poet within it?

At this moment, Koraidon couldn't help but speak up, reminding: "Ah geez!"

---

D looks up and sees Dragonite Island appearing in view.

They accelerate, and soon land on Dragonite Island.

The Dragonites and the many un-evolved Dratini and Dragonair on the island noticed their arrival.

In just a moment, D perceives dozens of sparkling eyes focused on him.

D: "?" What's going on?

With a "swish," several tiny figures darted out from the bushes.

They are the Dratini and Dragonair that left the pasture this morning.

Seeing their delighted expressions, D couldn't help but reach out to touch their heads, smiling as he asked, "Did you miss me?"

The Dratini and Dragonair nodded repeatedly: "Woo!"

The nearby Dragonite squints its eyes: "Awoo." It's only been half a day!

It wants to say more, but in the next moment, more Dratini, Dragonair, and Dragonites burst over, pushing the Dragonite to the side and surrounding D.

"Awoo!"

"Woo~"

"..."

Dragonite widened its eyes, suddenly watching this scene warily.

What's happening?

Why is this group suddenly so enthusiastic?

The growls of Dragon Type Pokemon rose and fell, and D felt the characters he saved with Translation Nuts were being rapidly consumed.

Even he was a little overwhelmed at the moment.

Through their growls, D roughly understands what is happening.

It turns out that during the day, an irritable Dragonite returned to Dragonite Island with two wild Dragonites and a group of Dratini and Dragonair, carrying Poffins that emitted a tempting fragrance.

The Dragon-type Pokemon on the island were attracted and came to ask what they were.

In response, the wild Dragonites, Dragonairs, and Dratinis proudly replied: "Awoo!" (These are the food D gave us!)

They happily shared their life in the pasture.

Especially the little Dratini and Dragonair.

Their vivid description unexpectedly succeeded in attracting a large group of guests eager to experience pasture life to D.

D: "..."

At this moment, Dragonite also realized what had happened, but it can no longer squeeze in.

It sadly thought: If I had known this would happen, I wouldn't have brought D to Dragonite Island at that time.

Regrets!

"Aga hiss..."

Koraidon adapted to D being warmly welcomed by Pokemon much earlier than Dragonite.

After all, no matter what, these Dragonites cannot go to D's room and sleep with him.

To D, Koraidon is unique!

Koraidon learned to find joy amidst suffering and tried to share this skill with Dragonite.

At this moment, a dragon roar drew many Dragonites' attention.

They turned around and then flew over with the Dratini and Dragonair to the other side of the island.

Finally released, D sweats profusely.

These Dragonites are just… too enthusiastic about his food!

But this played right into his hands, as he could soon use food as a reward to establish Dragonite Delivery Company.

Noticing what was happening over there, D asked the nearby Dragonite, "Is it starting?"

Dragonite's attention was also directed toward this matter.

It nodded and then flapped its wings to fly into the sky.

D rode Koraidon closely behind, arriving at the other end of the island.

The bright moon hangs high, and under the illumination of the gentle moonlight, the entire ocean seems like a dreamy world.

The sea is deep blue, and the moonlight is soft.

Underneath the sea, a whirlpool appeared at some point.

Dratini and Dragonair in midair plunge into the sea one after another, rushing into the vortex, swimming vigorously inside.

D hurriedly picked up his camera to capture this spectacular scene.

Suddenly, the seawater began to reverse flow, as if it had gained life, forming a water spout toward the sky and the moon.

Then, another water spout emerged from the sea!

Is this… the water flowing towards the moon that Dragonite mentioned before?!

D's pupils shrank, staring at the impossible scene unfolding before him, completely stunned.

But then, he noticed through the camera that there seemed to be something inside those water spouts.

D quickly adjusted the camera focus, enlarging the picture.

The next second, the true forms of those mysterious shadows appeared in the lens.

They are small, blue-bodied Pokemon, almost merging with the seawater!

They are small in stature, with a round head sporting ribbon-like antennae, a small mouth, a pair of large blue eyes, and an oval red gem on the chest.

It has a pointed, singular foot, seemingly yet to fully develop.

That is…

Phione?!

---

---

---

# 198: Chapter 195: The Prototype of Dragonite Express Company (4k)

Phione…

The Pokemon hatched from Manaphy's egg, born in the icy sea depths, living in warm sea waters, is renowned for its migratory nature.

Every year, at a certain time, they journey a great distance to return to where they were born.

At this moment, these sea-dwelling Pokemon are swimming in groups across this ocean territory, as if embarking on a pilgrimage.

With their movement, whirlpools are stirred in the ocean, countless azure-colored Phione dance lightly amidst the tides.

They sometimes leap up, sometimes dive deep.

When they leap, they lift masses of seawater, forming a pillar of water that seemingly reaches towards the moon.

When they dive deep, the seawater gathers around them, naturally forming a flowing vortex.

Their azure bodies merge seamlessly with the sea's hue, flying towards the moon with the water, soaring towards the sky.

The gentle moonlight intertwines with sparkling droplets above the deep blue sea, creating a breathtaking scene.

D gazes at the sight with awe.

After a brief moment of stupor, he decisively switches the camera mode to recording mode.

Below the sea, countless Dratini and Dragonair are swimming furiously.

They struggle against nature, against themselves, bravely overcoming the strength of the ocean currents, swimming upwards.

Yet the ocean's power is immensely overwhelming.

The juvenile Quasi-Gods appear weak and vulnerable in its presence.

Dratini and Dragonair constantly get swept into the deep sea by whirlpools, the fierce currents leaving marks on their bodies.

Nonetheless, the Dratini and Dragonair do not relent.

After adjusting slightly, they charge towards the ocean whirlpool once more, bearing their injuries.

In their fierce battle, finally, the first Dragonair breaks through the whirlpool.

Its graceful form pierces the vortex, leaping into the sky.

Glistening droplets fall from its body, and the next moment, a concentrated white light bursts from the Dragonair.

It is the light of life evolution!

"Awooo!"

With a melodious cry, a brand new Dragonite emerges within the light.

Then comes the second, the third...

On the island, the adult Dragonite flap their wings, levitating high in the air, watching the spectacle.

No Dragonite steps in to help, for they know that this is something that must be accomplished by oneself; every Dragonite has gone through this.

"This is truly magnificent!"

D can't help but exclaim.

D zooms in the picture, amidst the many Dratini and Dragonair, he spots the very conspicuous Shiny Dratini.

Almost lagging at the very end, its slender and tender body is continually pushed back.

From the footage, it's clearly visible that this Dratini's stamina is rapidly diminishing, to the point that it can hardly muster the energy to swim.

The small form repeatedly disappears into the whirlpool, swallowed by the pitch-black sea, until it doesn't appear for a long time.

From above, the scenes beneath the sea surface are unseen; seeing this, D's heart leaps to his throat instantly.

Dratini… it shouldn't drown, right?

And just then, the lens captures that delicate pinkish figure once more.

It struggles within the whirlpool, determination etched across its youthful face.

But the force of the sea is truly monumental; despite its utmost effort, it remains immobile against the fierce currents.

Momentarily, the situation reaches an impasse.

Watching the Dratini face being swept away by the sea again, D can't help but shout:

"Dratini, hang in there!"

Around, the Dragonite turn their gaze sharply in his direction.

D pays no attention, focusing entirely on the camera's display.

It seems the Dratini really hears his cheers.

It vigorously writhes its body, exerting an explosive burst of energy from its small frame.

With a whoosh, the Dratini finally breaks through the whirlpool, soaring into the sky.

Glittering droplets cascade from its form, the moment seemingly frozen in time.

Dratini's pink skin appears wondrous under the moonlight.

In the next moment, a ray emanates from its body.

Within the light, Dratini's body quickly transforms.

Its form becomes more slender, covered in smooth pink scales all over, with a bright white belly, its eyes black and shiny.

A tiny white horn emerges from its forehead; two small white wings grow on either side of its head, and a golden bead resembling a bell appears on its neck.

"It worked!"

D feels happy for Dratini from the bottom of his heart.

Dratini, no, Dragonair, after successfully evolving, immediately flies enthusiastically towards the shore, coming before D.

"Woo!"

D asks, "Did you hear?"

The Shiny Dragonair nods lightly, then affectionately nudges D with its head.

D chuckles as he strokes its head, examining the Shiny Dragonair's body.

Aside from its charming pink dragon scales, the most captivating feature is surely the golden bead on its neck.

"Could it possibly be a Nugget?"

Out of curiosity, D reaches out to touch it.

As he touches the bead, the Dragonair suddenly emits a mysterious aura.

With a hum, the originally ordinary golden bead starts to glow faintly, airflow continuously swirls around the Dragonair.

---

Suddenly, the weather in the sky began to change.

The clouds that had been covering the night sky miraculously dispersed, revealing a clearer and cleaner starry sky.

D's collar rustled as it was blown by the airflow from Dragonair.

He looked up at the sky and suddenly remembered the human world's records about Dragonair, this kind of Pokemon.

It's said that Dragonair's body stores tremendous energy, which they can concentrate on the crystal-like beads on their necks and tails to manipulate the weather.

Because of this, since ancient times, Dragonair has been worshipped by those engaged in agriculture as a controller of weather.

People believed it could manipulate the weather, so offerings were always plentiful in the lakes where Dragonair resided.

Now it seems, is this record true?

Dragonite beside them felt extremely envious witnessing this scene.

But it couldn't deny that this Dragonair's talent was higher than its own, even higher than most Dragonite on the island.

These days, Dragonite had come to understand that human trainers preferred more powerful Pokemon.

Thinking about how D had always been fond of this Dragonair, possibly liking it more, Dragonite felt so sad that it stayed silent.

D keenly noticed Dragonite's unusual behavior.

He knew his plump companion was unhappy again.

So he laughed heartily, hugged Dragonite and Koraidon's arms on his left and right, and said to Dragonair:

"We're all happy for you; your performance today was terrific, right, Dragonite?"

"Ow?" Dragonite realized D was comforting it; the little dark cloud above its head immediately floated away, replaced by a bright sun, nodding in agreement: "Ow!"

Dragonair didn't think much of it, and after evolving, it was naturally thrilled. After expressing gratitude to D, it looked around and then flew off to play with the other evolved Dragonairs.

Phiones' figures gradually drifted away.

The evolution ceremony was also coming to an end.

D sat with Koraidon and Dragonite on a rock by the cliff, listening to the sound of waves crashing on the shore while watching the video recorded on the camera.

"Tsk, this is incredibly valuable research material!" D marveled.

Whether it's the migrating Manaphys, or the Dragonairs and Dratinis evolving under moonlight, they are unparalleled treasures for those Pokemon researchers.

D believes, if he were to sell this ecological footage, he could fetch a hefty price.

But well... he doesn't lack money, so it's better to keep it as a family heirloom.

D put away the camera, and then, seeing that Dragonite wasn't paying attention, secretly led Dragonite and Koraidon to sneak away.

Regarding his actions, Dragonite was perplexed: "Ow?"

Wouldn't he say goodbye to them?

D stopped in his tracks: "If you want to invite some Dragonites back to the ranch as guests, it's possible."

Of course, that's not possible!

Dragonite understood; this time, without D needing to say, it immediately took the lead in departing, pulling Koraidon and D away from Dragonite Island.

Seeing its manner, D chuckled: "Don't worry, you're my favorite Dragonite."

"Grrr?"

D patted Koraidon's neck and said to it, "You're also my favorite Koraidon."

Now both Pokemon were satisfied.

D quietly breathed a sigh of relief in his heart.

He made up his mind that he couldn't go crazy collecting Pokemon like in games; otherwise, the effort to balance relationships among each Pokemon would tire him out.

Dragonite really thought it would never see those annoying Dragonites on Dragon Island again.

However, the next morning, at dawn, it saw some familiar figures in the ranch.

Looking at those two wild Dragonites and five Dragonairs, Dragonite rubbed its eyes in disbelief.

The wild Dragonites and Dragonairs blinked and greeted it: "Ow~"

Dragonite: "!!!"

Dragonite was extremely shocked.

But then, it thought of what D had said before and maliciously folded its arms.

"Ow!" (You can come to the ranch, but you must help before you can get Poffins!)

Upon hearing the word "Poffins," the two wild Dragonites immediately recalled that delicious and rich food.

They nodded repeatedly: "Ow ow!"

Seeing this, Dragonite breathed a sigh of relief, thinking these Dragonites must be here for food.

That made things easier.

"Ow, ow ow!" (D worked hard to prepare food, and humans are different from us; they need money to buy food. Yesterday, you ate all our Poffins, and now there's none left. If you want to eat, you need to help D work!)

Upon hearing this, the two wild Dragonites mentally equated Poffins with work.

A few curious Dragonairs nearby also looked over.

Unlike the two wild Dragonites, they were not only interested in Poffins but also liked D as a human.

The two wild Dragonites asked in confusion: "Ow?"

What is work?

Dragonite: "..."

It patiently explained to the Pokemon in front of it the things it usually does.

"Ow." (D manages the ranch very hard, and Pokemon in the ranch all work with him. You can do the same tasks as me; it's simple, just deliver fresh milk from the ranch to the customers who ordered it. But remember, you can't drink it secretly on the way!)

Dragonite looked at them and continued with a serious expression: "Ow." (If you drink it secretly, D won't make Poffins for you anymore.)

The two wild Dragonites wanted to taste Poffins again, so they shook their heads repeatedly, indicating they wouldn't steal drinks.

Dragonite was satisfied; it thought for a moment and added: "Ow!" (Starting today, you must listen to me!)

For these greedy Dragonites, they naturally had no objections.

Seeing this, Dragonite felt an inexplicable thrill.

It never thought that there would be Dragonites that listened to this silly Dragonite like itself.

"Uh?" At this moment, a few Dragonairs next to them asked.

What about them?

Dragonite kind of Pokemon are communal creatures; they typically care greatly for the Dratinis and Dragonairs yet to mature within their group.

Although these Dragonairs would compete with it for D's attention, Dragonite instinctively chose to take care of them.

"Ow." (Just try not to cause trouble, and when you become Dragonite, you can come help D.)

Dragonite waved its claws, sending the five Dragonairs off to the lake.

It itself stayed to teach the two wild Dragonites how to deliver milk.

So, early in the morning, people in Porto Marinada Town saw three rare Dragonites flying overhead, arriving at the town.

The townsfolk were stunned, incredibly shocked.

"Is that... Dragonite?"

"Am I seeing things? This kind of rare Pokemon appeared in Porto Marinada Town?!"

But some townspeople who had seen Dragonite deliver milk noticed something.

They keenly noticed that the lead Dragonite was carrying a small red satchel.

"Huh? Did D Ranch hire new employees again?"

"Oh, it's that Dragonite?"

"You know it?"

"Of course, it's quite famous in the town! Every morning, we could see it flying from D Ranch, delivering fresh milk to the customers who ordered it. It has a great personality, and people in the town like it very much. It's said Mrs. Dante often makes homemade cookies to give to it."

"Using Dragonite to deliver milk? That's too extravagant!"

The man who said this was a trainer from another region, and hearing these words, he showed a shocked expression.

"You'll get used to it." The townsfolk of Porto Marinada Town were accustomed to it: "Dragonite's flying speed is very fast, and it has a gentle nature; why can't it be entrusted to deliver milk?"

"This, this, this..." The man was speechless, unable to utter a word.

As he knew, Dragonite in the Johto Region's Dragon Taming Clan was considered a treasure!

If the clan members learned that someone in this world was using their family treasure as a delivery worker, wouldn't their eyes widen, legs kick, and they'd scold this as wasteful?
