# 199: Chapter 196: Glass Greenhouse and Phone Rotom (Two-in-One)

In addition to Mrs. Dantes who ordered milk earlier and Elo, the seed shop owner, D Ranch has received many more orders from the town these days.

There are orders for the magical milk produced by Gogoat, orders for rock salt, orders for fresh eggs, and orders for the MooMoo Fresh Milk from Miltank.

Although the town also sells these, they believe that the milk freshly squeezed from the ranch is fresher, more authentic, and tastes better.

There was even someone who ordered a basket of tender corn previously, saying they wanted to eat steamed corn.

The corn was delivered by D and Koraidon together.

All other milk, goat milk, and eggs were delivered by Dragonite.

Over the past few months, Dragonite has become more familiar with Porto Marinada Town than D.

Dragonite quite enjoys this job.

Because during the job, it can hear everyone's compliments.

And Dragonite's favorite customers are Mrs. Dantes living on Blue Ocean Street in the northeast corner of town and her husband.

Because this kind and amiable old lady always likes to make some cookies and other snacks to give to Dragonite.

At first, Dragonite was very anxious about this because D said not to randomly accept gifts from customers, so it refused.

But Mrs. Dantes was so enthusiastic.

Dragonite didn't want them, but she insisted, saying these were new flavor cookies she had just developed, and if Dragonite didn't eat them, she would be very sad.

Therefore, Dragonite had no choice but to accept them reluctantly.

After returning to the ranch, it immediately discussed this matter with D.

After a long discussion, D ultimately decided: from now on, use a bigger glass bottle to deliver milk to Mrs. Dantes.

Now, Dragonite, accompanied by two wild Dragonite, arrived in Porto Marinada Town, introducing them to the places that needed milk deliveries.

"Roar, roar!" (Remember, this house and this house! Every morning, D will have the fresh milk ready, and you just need to deliver it!)

The wild Dragonite blinked blankly: "Roar?" (And then?)

Dragonite: "..."

It suddenly felt that these two guys were even dumber than it was.

Thinking of the future plan D devised for it, Dragonite felt the weight of responsibility and took a deep breath before explaining in detail:

"Roar——" (You can't just drop the milk, you need to land in front of the door, knock on it, and then someone will come out to receive the milk.)

"Roar!" (But note that delivering milk isn't the end, because humans will exchange money for the fresh milk. Bring this money back to the ranch to give to D, and he will make delicious Poffins for you!)

"Roar!" Upon hearing this, the two wild Dragonite's eyes sparkled with excitement.

It sounded quite simple, didn't it?!

Next, Dragonite detailed the clients each dragon should be responsible for.

At the end, it did not forget to remind them: "Roar!" (You guys take care of those, and I'll handle this place!)

For other customers, Dragonite didn't mind, but Mrs. Dantes, who baked cookies for it, was a delivery only it could make.

The two wild Dragonite naturally had no objections.

After completing the instructions, the three Dragonite left Porto Marinada Town and flew back to D Ranch.

They came in a hurry, wandered around Porto Marinada Town, and left in a hurry.

This eye-catching scene not only caught the attention of the townspeople but also intrigued some Trainers camping in the countryside.

"It's a Dragonite! I've seen it in the Pokedex at the academy library!"

"How could such a Pokemon appear here?"

"That's even rarer than a Charcadet! I have to capture them!"

"Clefairy, let's go!"

"No way, I saw them first! If anyone's capturing them, it's me!"

"Then let's see who can catch up to them first!"

Two boys in Orange Academy uniforms ran off determinedly in pursuit of the Dragonite.

The girl left behind held her forehead in exasperation: "Honestly, these two are way too impulsive. Didn't they ever consider that these Dragonite might belong to another Trainer?"

She glanced at the tent beside her, then gritted her teeth and ran off without even packing up the tent.

*

Silence, silence is tonight's Kang Bridge.

D looked at the five familiar figures in the lake, momentarily unsure of what to say.

Instead, the five Dragonair greeted him first.

"Woo~"

D snapped out of it, walked to the lakeside, and looked at the Dragonair.

Including the Shiny Dratini, originally, there should have been three Dratini here.

But last night, all three Dratini evolved into Dragonair.

Looking at them now, it seems two Dragonair may have failed to evolve.

Was it due to a lack of accumulated experience?

As he looked at the five Dragonair, they also gazed back at him with wide eyes, appearing quite innocent.

D sighed helplessly: "Alright, since you're here, you might as well stay!"

After all, Dragonair are worshiped by humans as agricultural deities.

With them around, the ranch should be blessed with good weather for the crops.

D stood up and surveyed the surroundings.

He hadn't seen Dragonite since early morning and guessed that it had likely gone to deliver milk.

Not in a rush, D went for a morning run with Cyclizar and Pawmot, while also inspecting his ranch.

He had new tasks for today, one of which was to transform the flower field.

---

Last night, after some contemplation, D devised two renovation plans in his mind.

The first plan is to set up a support frame in the middle of the flower field, allowing flowers to climb naturally and form a natural flower arch.

Leave space in the middle, pave it with pebbles, and arrange tea sets and furniture.

This way, Combee and Butterfree can rest on the frame, while people enjoy tea below.

The second plan is to build a transparent Glass Greenhouse using a large amount of glass.

The advantage of this is that even in the cold winter, flowers and plants can grow inside with ease.

Furniture and tea sets can also be placed inside, allowing people to elegantly drink tea and admire flowers inside during winter, just like in spring.

Both plans have their own merits, leaving D somewhat undecided for the moment.

Just then, Dragonite returned.

What surprised D was that it unexpectedly brought two other Dragonite along!

As soon as those two Dragonite saw him, their eyes lit up instantly.

D: "..."

Well, this familiar feeling, those two gluttons are unmistakable.

D was not foolish; upon seeing the red pouch on Dragonite's back and its demeanor, he quickly guessed that it took them to the town to familiarize themselves with the route, paving the way for the future Dragonite Delivery Company.

"Did they agree?" D asked softly.

Dragonite nodded confidently.

D: "Then that's fine."

Since Dragonite was all set, he couldn't lag behind.

After pondering for a moment, D decided to write an announcement to post on the bulletin board in Porto Marinada Town square later.

As for the content, he already had an idea.

"D Ranch is now launching Dragonite Delivery services! Fresh eggs, nutritious milk, magical goat milk, large and plump pumpkins! Dragonite Delivery, straight to your home!

If interested, please call 5376898! Note: Currently, we do not support inter-city and inter-regional deliveries!"

As for why inter-city deliveries weren't supported...

It was because the Dragonite were still very unfamiliar with other cities, and there weren't any good navigational tools to guide them.

Speaking of navigation, D's first thought was Rotom.

Rotom was a very magical Pokémon; they could inhabit refrigerators, washing machines, all sorts of appliances, even phones and computers.

The renowned Rotom Phone quickly spread throughout the Pokemon World once released.

This device, which could look up and record the Pokedex, came with maps, navigation features, could communicate and take photos, was very popular among Trainers.

If available, he could equip each delivery Dragonite with one to assist them in deliveries, and keep one at home to maintain contact with Dragonite delivering outside.

Unfortunately, at this time, the Phone Rotom had not yet appeared.

D recalled: "If I remember correctly, the Phone Rotom should have been made by the company founded by Nemona's parents."

But now, Arven wasn't even ten years old yet, and Nemona was probably just a little kid.

And Penny, who adored her fluffy Eevee backpack, she might have just been born?

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but sigh in his heart.

"Oh well, we'll just have to wait gradually."

After all, even smartphones hadn't been invented yet...

Even if he managed to find Rotom, there was nothing he could do.

Every time this happened, D would mutter about how skewed this world's technology tree was.

As for inter-regional deliveries...

Dragonite's flying speed surpassing that of airplanes is a well-known fact in this world.

Even though they could circle the globe in sixteen hours, D still didn't plan on having them deliver to other regions.

Because other regions were not peaceful at the moment.

Take the Kanto Region and Hoenn Region for example, it would be disastrous if members from Team Rocket or Team Magma and Team Aqua posed as customers ordering deliveries, tricking Dragonite.

They would just need to set up a trap in advance, and once Dragonite reached the destination, it would immediately get captured.

That would truly be a loss.

This was all for the safety of the Dragonite.

"No way, besides equipping them with Phone Rotom, I also have to find ways to enhance Dragonite's abilities," D thought deeply.

Not expecting them to surpass Koraidon, but at least be on par with Gogoat.

*

After breakfast, D began making their favorite Poffins for the two foodie Dragonite.

Now that Big Brother Indeedee was around, he didn't need to worry about the daily chores, as Big Brother Indeedee would handle all housework orderly.

As a result, D had ample time and energy to prepare the favorite cuisines for the many Pokémon.

Little Sister Indeedee had never learned to make Poffins before.

She was naturally interested in preparing delightful dishes, and upon seeing D busying himself, Little Sister Indeedee immediately came over curiously to watch.

D glanced at her, raised his eyebrows, and asked, "Want to learn?"

"Indeedee!" Little Sister Indeedee nodded quickly; could she learn?

"Of course." D smiled, and then began to demonstrate the process of making Poffins for her.

"Like this, first place the Berry in cold water, then heat while continuously stirring to bring flavor to the Poffins..."

"But be mindful of the timing, if it's overcooked, the batter might get burnt, making the Poffins unpalatable, and no Pokémon would like it."

---

"Once the batter with the berry is cooked, you can pour it into the mold and put it in the oven to bake."

Little Indeedee listened carefully.

After D finished, he stepped aside and said to it, "Do you want to give it a try?"

"Indeedee~"

Little Indeedee happily stepped forward to try.

Unlike humans, Psychic Type Pokemon can cook and make desserts without using their hands.

They can skillfully manipulate ingredients and cookware with just telekinesis.

Watching the spoon enveloped in blue telekinesis, D found it quite amazing.

Judging by Little Indeedee's control and proficiency with these things, it has great talent in telekinetic manipulation.

"Great, the batter is ready; next, you can pour it into the molds and put it in the oven to bake." Seeing that it was about right, D instructed.

Little Indeedee happily complied.

It used its psychic powers to evenly pour the batter into the molds and then controlled the molds into the oven.

"We just need to wait for twenty minutes," D said.

He intended to wait with Little Indeedee, but at that moment, there was a sudden commotion outside the ranch.

D glanced outside and said to Little Indeedee, "You keep an eye on things here; I'm going to take a look outside."

"Indeedee~"

D went outside and saw three young students from Orange Academy had arrived at the ranch.

They stared in shock at the three Dragonites sunbathing on the grass.

One of the boys saw D and realized that the three Dragonites weren't wild Pokemon.

He had a shocked expression on his face and quickly said:

"Sorry to bother you! We're students from Paldea Academy and followed the three Dragonites here. Sorry, we didn't know they were your Pokemon!"

D raised an eyebrow, so they were attracted by the Dragonites?

In that case, it's fine.

He thought they were here for the Grass Trial!

"Don't worry about it," D said with a smile as he looked at the Dragonites. "They're the couriers from the ranch. When you saw them, they were probably on their way to deliver goods to the town."

"Deliver goods?" The trio had incredulous expressions.

D smiled and said, "Just like Machamp living and working with humans."

Upon hearing this, the three exchanged glances and swallowed hard.

Even though he said that, something still felt off...

What they didn't know was that the person standing in front of them had once even used the Creator God Arceus for farming.

Even though that was just in a game.

D was already used to people being shocked by using Dragonite for milk deliveries.

He was very calm about it.

At this time, the trio's attention was attracted by a Pawmot flying over from the Chicken House with a red cape to drink some water.

"Is that... a Pawmot?!"

"It's flying!"

Pawmot flew over and affectionately nuzzled D's cheek, then flew indoors to drink water.

The trio's eyes were fixed in the direction Pawmot left.

At this moment, the girl among them suddenly noticed several Dragonairs flying above the fields.

"Look at those Pokemon!"

The two boys turned their heads.

In the next second, they saw several Dragonairs lying on the giant pumpkins in the field.

"Pink Dragonairs!" The boy's eyes widened.

Shiny Pokemon, they're incredibly rare!

Even among a thousand Pokemon, you might not encounter one!

At this point, Koraidon wandered out of the room, preparing to find a cool spot to watch D work.

As usual, it greeted D with a "Gias!"

Hearing the sound, the trio turned their heads again.

Their eyes landed on the formidable-looking Koraidon, their eyes widened, and they shouted with a hint of awe:

"A Pokemon we've never seen before!"

D: "..."

There are just too many Trainers who haven't seen the world.

---

---

---

---

# 200: Chapter 197: Holding a Tea Party in the Secret Garden (4k)

After sending off three inexperienced Trainers, D began to work on the construction of the Glass Greenhouse.

After much consideration, he decided to use two plans simultaneously.

Not only would he build a flower stand, but he would also construct a separate warm Glass Greenhouse.

The flower stand was for people, while the Glass Greenhouse was for the Combee and Butterfree.

In winter, the temperatures are freezing, and heavy snow blankets the entire land. Not to mention flowers, even the hardy little grass cannot grow.

This season is very challenging for Combee and Butterfree.

They survive on nectar, so they can't find food anymore.

D doesn't know how the wild Combee and Butterfree solve this problem.

But since he is here, he naturally won't just watch that happen.

Therefore, he planned to build a Glass Greenhouse.

That way, come winter, he could move the Combee's hive and Butterfree's box into the Glass Greenhouse.

By then, he would place a few pots of greenery and flowers here, along with specially made honey sold in town, ensuring a safe winter for a few Pokemon.

D is very skilled with his hands.

That afternoon, he sawed several wooden strips and assembled them into a large flower stand.

The flower fields are now planted with numerous flowers.

Hydrangeas, roses, monthly roses, lilies, Phalaenopsis, and a few flowers unique to the Pokemon World.

Their names were all jumbled, and D couldn't remember them well; he just bought them because they looked nice and planted them.

Next, he had to figure out how to move the flower stand to the flower field.

D circled the flower field with the Indeedee Sibling, pondering what method to use while explaining his ideas to the puzzled brother Indeedee.

"...I want to build a garden where we can drink tea and enjoy the flowers, so we can spend leisure time here in the future."

"Indeedee?"

Hearing this, brother Indeedee suddenly remembered there was such a place in the manor too.

It immediately spoke up: "Indeedee!"

"Hmm?" D turned to look at it, "You have an idea?"

"Indeedee." Brother Indeedee nodded.

It gazed calmly at the lush flower field in front of it and spoke: "Indeedee... Indeedee."

D touched the corner of his mouth, visibly irritated, thought for a moment, and still pulled out a Translation Nuts Cake to eat.

Seeing this, brother Indeedee repeated: "Indeedee... Indeedee." (You need to move the flowers in the middle first, then place the flower stand in, and lastly, pave a pebble pathway on the ground.)

"Pebbles?" D pondered, nodding, "That's a good idea, thank you, Indeedee."

Brother Indeedee modestly nodded, not seeking credit.

Now that they had a plan, the next step was to move the flowers inside.

Conveniently, D planned to build a Glass Greenhouse later, so the moved flowers could be relocated inside.

After a brief contemplation, D called Koraidon and Cyclizar, who were watching from not far away, to bring over the empty flower pots from the storeroom, then began to dig up the flowers.

Butterfree and Combee, who were collecting nectar, didn't have any particular reaction when they saw this happening.

Anyway, D wouldn't hurt them.

Brother Indeedee watched for a while, then silently turned and left.

It returned to the house and quietly exchanged thoughts with its sister, who was making Poffins.

Sister Indeedee blinked and then stopped her work, fetched a few green Lum Berries from the Berry stash.

This is a type of Berry that can cure all abnormal statuses.

Sister Indeedee squeezed them into juice and poured it into a cup, handing it to her brother.

So, when D, covered in dirt, moved the potted flowers to the grass outside, he saw a glass of juice placed on the small table he used for tea.

Without guessing, D knew who made it.

Ever since the Indeedee Sibling joined, such considerate gestures and small surprises in life have not been uncommon.

He happily drank the juice and then went back into the house to use the video phone to order a batch of pebbles and paving mortar from Keri.

Keri wasn't out working today, so after receiving the call, she quickly delivered D's order.

Moreover, upon learning of D's plan, she even stayed to provide some guidance.

By five in the afternoon, the construction in the flower field was finally finished.

The flower field, originally square, had a large empty area cleared in the middle, and the large flower stand D made was moved to the empty area by Indeedee through Telekinesis.

The empty area was surrounded by a layer of undried pebbles, with a path connecting the central area to the outside.

In the future, the flowers would grow along the flower stand until it was completely covered.

Then, the pebble pathway and the flower stand would be hidden by lush flower vines, forming a natural secret garden.

Imagining that scene, D felt incredibly excited inside and thanked Keri: "I really have to thank you this time!"

Keri gave a slight smile: "No need to thank me, after all, you've already paid."

D: "..."

Thinking about the Glass Greenhouse again, he added: "Oh, right, I also want to build a Glass Greenhouse in the pasture, the kind with heating."

"Glass Greenhouse?" Keri thought for a moment and said, "It's possible, but the glass materials at the store are used up. The next batch is on the way and is expected to arrive mid-month. If you can wait, I'll deliver it to you then."

---

"Sure." D nodded, "As long as it's built before winter arrives."

His gaze turned towards the Butterfree and Combee not far away.

Keri followed his gaze and quickly guessed D's thoughts.

"Of course." She smiled slightly, then waved goodbye to D, "Alright, it's getting late, we should get going! See you later!"

D: "See you later!"

Just as Keri left, Zack drove up to the ranch in his truck.

D loaded several large barrels of milk and some bags of Rock Salt onto the truck, looking at the truck bed full of golden large pumpkins, he said with a smile:

"Looks like today's harvest is pretty good!"

Zack laughed heartily, "Indeed! This autumn, all the crops in Porto Marinada Town are thriving! The weather has been favorable, without any natural disasters! It's all thanks to the blessings of The King of Bountiful Harvests!"

Saying this, Zack suddenly turned mysterious:

"Hey, did you hear? The harvest in the neighboring town isn't so good this year! Didn't it pour for several days in the summer? Their fields got flooded!"

"It's amazing, we're only a few kilometers apart, yet the rainfall here wasn't so heavy."

D: "..."

He silently turned his head toward the Dragonair and Dragonite not far away, thinking of the legends that regard them as controllers of the weather. Could it be true?

Was it thanks to the presence of Dragonair and Dragonite that Porto Marinada Town wasn't flooded?

Zack seemed to suddenly remember something, looking surprised, he asked, "Could it also be The King of Bountiful Harvests protecting us?"

In Zack's mind, it was D who brought The King of Bountiful Harvests here, and everyone began to worship it with him. No one knew more about The King of Bountiful Harvests than D.

D politely smiled, "Well... I'm not really sure, as I've never actually seen The King of Bountiful Harvests myself. I've only heard stories, and all I know is that it's a very powerful Pokemon."

"Alright." Zack thought for a moment, feeling he shouldn't keep this information to himself, "No way, I have to ask the other farmers later!"

Go ahead! The King of Bountiful Harvests would surely appreciate you spreading the faith, after all, He can see this place, D thought.

The King of Bountiful Harvests blesses the crops for a bountiful yield, while Dragonite and Dragonair ensure the ranch has favorable weather; they truly are a perfect farming duo!

Zack came and left in a hurry, saying he was going to tell the farmers in the neighboring town about their King of Bountiful Harvests, to see if The King of Bountiful Harvests might protect them too.

Watching his figure disappear into the distance, D finally approached the lakeside, looking at the few Dragonair soaking in the water, he asked:

"Are you the ones ensuring favorable weather for the ranch?"

"Woo?"

The Dragonair appeared puzzled, seemingly not understanding what he was saying.

D smiled and patted their heads, "If everyone knew about this, they would be very grateful to you. Moreover, they might even bring you the crops and food they grow."

Just like in stories where people worship Dragonair by continuously offering tributes into the lake where they reside.

The Dragonair didn't seem to comprehend, as they hadn't done anything.

"Well." D pondered for a moment, speculating to himself: Maybe it's because of the special aura radiating from the five Dragonairs and the three Dragonites gathered together?

Could those auras be affecting the nearby weather...

Just like in the anime, the Dragon's Holy Sanctuary of the Dragon Taming Clan, where the flowers bloom all year round, said to be influenced by the Dragon Power of the Holy Sanctuary Guardian, Dragonite.

Come to think of it, Arceus created a Life Orb which can enrich the soil, containing a Dragon Origin Plate within it.

In this sense, Porto Marinada Town's fair weather might indeed be related to those Dragonite and Dragonair.

Tonight's dinner was prepared by his sister, Indeedee.

She was busy in the kitchen making Poffins all afternoon.

The diligent sister, Indeedee, made as many as two large boxes of Poffins throughout the afternoon.

She distributed the Poffins among the Pokemon at the ranch, especially to the two wild Dragonites and the five Dragonairs.

Receiving their gratitude and joy, sister Indeedee was overjoyed and immediately assured that she would make more Poffins whenever they wanted.

Seeing this, D was at a loss for words.

Good heavens! This wasn't a ploy to exploit his sister Indeedee, making her prepare Poffins to feed the two wild Dragonite!

With their favorite Poffins, the two wild Dragonites joyfully carried the Poffins, preparing to return to Dragon Island with the five Dragonairs.

Seeing this, D was puzzled and hurriedly stopped the departing Pokemon, asking, "Why are you leaving already?"

The wild Dragonite blinked, "Roar!"

D ate a Translation Nut, and then heard the wild Dragonite say:

"We promised the Dragonair and Dratini on the island that we would return every night with food, so they won't worry!"

The other wild Dragonite added, "Those Dratini said when they grow up, they also want to come over here like us!"

When they return and tell the other Dragonair and Dratini about today, they too might want to come here to work.

D: "..."

"Alright then, make sure to stay safe!"

Seeing them planning to share the Poffins with other dragons, D handed them two more Poffin Boxes filled with Poffins:

"From now on, these boxes are your wage cards. After working each day, you'll receive a full box of Poffins."

Hearing this, the eyes of the two wild Dragonites lit up.

They embraced their Poffin Boxes with a sense of anticipation for the future, cheerfully setting off on their way home.

D wasn't worried. Considering the speed of the Dragonite, if they flew at full speed, they could reach Dragon Island in just a dozen minutes.

As for why it takes him so long to reach Dragon Island, it's because if Dragonite flew that fast, he'd get dragon-sick.

After dinner, it was time for the tavern to open for the night.

D didn't go today; instead, he stayed home, watching TV with Koraidon.

Having eaten a lot of sunflower seed snacks recently, D was feeling a bit under the weather, so he was controlling his intake. Even if he went to the tavern, he couldn't understand the Pokemon's conversations.

But suddenly, he heard the sound of a siren outside.

D's heart skipped a beat, and he quickly got up to check outside.

He saw the familiar Miss Jenny arriving on her motorcycle with Growlithe, heading to the tavern.

However, Growlithe didn't go into the tavern to visit today; it stayed by Miss Jenny's side, seeming to have some task to do.

"Sorry to intrude!" Seeing D, Miss Jenny greeted him first, then said, "I'm going to park my bike here and later take Growlithe to the tavern for a drink."

D thought of the bad guys who stole other people's Pokemon in the anime and froze: "Are there Pokemon Hunters in the area?"

Miss Jenny was momentarily taken aback but then chuckled, shaking her head:

"Not at all; Porto Marinada Town is known for its good security, there are no Pokemon Hunters around. We're just going into the mountains for a patrol tonight."

Hearing this, D let out a sigh of relief, "A patrol?"

"Yes!" Miss Jenny nodded gently, "It's autumn, so there are a lot of dry leaves in the mountains. Some Trainers occasionally camp there, and there are wild Fire Type Pokemon. A little carelessness can lead to a wildfire."

"So, every evening during this season, I do a round of patrols in the mountains, otherwise it would be disastrous if a fire broke out."

D nodded, thinking of a forest fire scene, "Indeed, thank you for your hard work!"

"As long as that situation doesn't occur."

Miss Jenny smiled softly, then her expression turned serious.

"Winter is approaching, and if a fire occurs, not only will there be Pokemon casualties, but the food in the forest will also be burned away. Even if some Pokemon survive, they'll find it hard to get through the winter without food, so it's a very important task."

Growlithe also nodded earnestly, "Woof woof!"
